### [1] [The cubic threefold is symplectically irrational](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01577)
*Jiaji Cai*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 本文证明光滑三次三维簇（cubic threefolds）是辛不可有理的，从而恢复了经典的Clemens-Griffiths不可有理定理。其障碍来自量子连接在不规则奇点处的形式单值性（formal monodromy）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典代数几何中，Clemens-Griffiths证明了光滑三次三维簇不是有理簇，这是双有理几何中的重要结果。本文目标是给出该经典定理的一个新的、基于辛几何与量子连接视角的证明或推广。

Method: 作者利用量子连接（quantum connection）在不规则奇点（irregular singularity）处的形式单值性（formal monodromy）来构造辛不变量，该不变量成为三次三维簇为辛不可有理的障碍。

Result: 成功证明了光滑三次三维簇是辛不可有理的（symplectically irrational），即不存在从该簇到射影空间的辛同胚（或保持辛结构的等价映射），从而恢复了经典的Clemens-Griffiths有理不可性定理。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了辛几何与双有理几何之间的联系，表明量子连接的不规则奇点的形式单值性可以成为强有力的辛不变量，为经典不可有理定理提供了新的证明路径，并可能推广到更一般的簇类。

Abstract: We prove that smooth cubic threefolds are symplectically irrational. This recovers the classical irrationality theorem of Clemens-Griffiths. The obstruction is given by the formal monodromy of the quantum connection around the irregular singularity.

</details>

### [2] [Hypergraphs on Riemannian manifolds and their double homology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00708)
*Shiquan Ren*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文通过为Δ-流形上的分级子流形构造双重同调，研究了黎曼流形上超（有向）图的双重同调理论，建立了相关的交换图，并探讨了与辫群的联系及其在球填充/覆盖滤过同伦型和正则映射刻画中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将超图（有向图）的代数拓扑不变量从离散/单纯复形背景推广到光滑/黎曼流形设置，并建立双重同调理论，以更深入地理解超图在几何空间中的拓扑结构，同时为理解流形上超图的同伦类型和群论性质提供新工具。

Method: (1) 为Δ-流形上的分级子流形构造双重同调；(2) 利用从有序序列到无序集合的典范投影π，证明超（有向）图双重同调的交换图和微分形式空间的交换图；(3) 通过π诱导的同态，建立流形上超图的基本群（群胚）与曲面上的（纯）辫群之间的联系；(4) 应用这些工具研究黎曼流形上带超图约束的球填充与覆盖滤过的同伦类型，并用独立性复形和超图的几何实现刻画黎曼流形上的正则映射。

Result: 成功构造了Δ-流形上分级子流形的双重同调，证明了双重同调和微分形式空间的两个交换图；建立了超图基本群（群胚）与曲面辫群之间的同态关系；给出了带超图约束的球填充/覆盖滤过的同伦类型结果，并利用独立性复形和超图几何实现完成了对黎曼流形上正则映射的刻画。

Conclusion: 该研究将超图理论、代数拓扑和黎曼几何有机融合，为超图在流形上的拓扑性质提供了系统性的双重同调框架，并在辫群理论、填充/覆盖的同伦分类以及正则映射刻画等问题上给出了有价值的应用。

Abstract: In this paper, by constructing double homology for graded submanifolds of $Δ$-manifolds, we study the double homology of hyper(di)graphs on Riemannian manifolds. With the help of the canonical projection $π$ from ordered sequences to their underlying unordered sets, we prove a commutative diagram of the double homology of hyper(di)graphs on Riemannian manifolds and a commutative diagram of the spaces of differential forms of hyper(di)graphs on Riemannian manifolds. Besides, we give some relations between the fundamental goup(oid)s of hyper(di)graphs on surfaces and the (pure) braid groups on surfaces by certain homomorphisms induced from $π$. As applications, we study the homotopy types of filtrations for packings and coverings of balls on Riemannian manifolds with hypergraph constraints, and we characterize regular maps on Riemannian manifolds by geometric realizations of the independence complexes and hypergraphs on the manifolds.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 本文采用理论推导的方法，在有限偏序集范畴内定义适当空间和适当映射，并发展其相关性质与理论体系。

Result: 在有限偏序集上成功建立了适当空间与适当映射的完整理论框架。

Conclusion: 该理论为有限偏序集的拓扑研究提供了新的工具和视角，拓展了适当空间理论在组合结构中的应用范围。

Abstract: We develop a theory of proper spaces and proper maps in the context of finite posets.

</details>


### [4] [Sequential $m$-contiguity distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02116)
*Nilay Ekiz Yazici,Nursultan Kuanyshov,Ayse Borat*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文引入了有限多个单纯映射的序列m-邻接距离（sequential m-contiguity distance），作为邻接距离的高维类比，统一推广了高维邻接距离和m-邻接距离，并提供了序列m-同伦距离的组合对应物。文章还定义了单纯复形的序列m-离散拓扑复杂度，并建立了其与m-单纯LS范畴之间的不等式关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在拓扑复杂性理论中，需要将经典的同伦距离和拓扑复杂度概念推广到单纯场景和m维（高维）情形。已有的高维邻接距离和m-邻接距离分别处理了不同维度的推广，但缺乏一个统一的框架来同时处理多个单纯映射的序列版本，因此需要引入更一般的不变量。

Method: 作者定义了有限多个单纯映射的序列m-邻接距离，作为邻接距离的组合类比。通过研究其在强同伦类型下的不变性、复合运算、范畴积和重心细分下的行为来建立基本性质。在此基础上定义了序列m-离散拓扑复杂度，并利用序列m-邻接距离对m-单纯LS范畴进行刻画。

Result: 证明了序列m-邻接距离和序列m-离散拓扑复杂度是强同伦类型的不变量；建立了m-单纯LS范畴与m-离散序列拓扑复杂度之间的不等式，将拓扑复杂度的经典结果推广到了单纯和m维设定。

Conclusion: 该工作为拓扑复杂度的组合和离散化研究提供了一个统一的框架，特别是将经典拓扑复杂度理论中的LS范畴不等式推广到了单纯复形的m维（高维）情形，具有理论意义和应用前景。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce the notion of sequential $m$-contiguity distance for finitely many simplicial maps as a higher analogue of contiguity distance. This invariant generalizes both higher contiguity distance and $m$-contiguity distance, and provides a combinatorial counterpart of sequential $m$-homotopic distance. We investigate its fundamental properties, including invariance under strong homotopy type, behaviour under compositions, categorical products, and barycentric subdivision.
  Moreover, we define sequential $m$-discrete topological complexity of simplicial complexes. As applications, we characterise this invariant (along with $m$-simplicial LS category) in terms of sequential $m$-contiguity distance and prove that they are invariants of strong homotopy type. Furthermore, we establish inequalities relating $m$-simplicial LS category and $m$-discrete sequential topological complexity, extending classical results from topological complexity theory to the simplicial and $m$-dimensional setting.

</details>


### [5] [A family completion theorem for tempered cohomology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02390)
*Leonard Tokic*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文研究了定向P-可除群G在诺特E∞-环R上的表示理论，证明了在有限群G上，R(G)G-模在子群族F处的完备化与在理想I_G(F)上的代数完备化一致。该结果推广了Adams-Haeberly-Jackowski-May家族完备化定理和Atiyah-Segal完备化定理，核心工具是温特征标栈G{BG}上的支撑理论，并推广到局部诺特几何基栈上的版本，适用于真等变拓扑模形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典的Atiyah-Segal完备化定理和Adams-Haeberly-Jackowski-May家族完备化定理统一推广到更一般的框架中：在诺特E∞-环上的定向P-可除群表示论中建立完备化定理，从而涵盖更广泛的等变同伦论与代数拓扑场景，包括真等变拓扑模形式等应用。

Method: 作者引入了一种针对温特征标栈G{BG}点的支撑理论，类似于Segal对复数表示环素谱的分析。证明了支撑一个点是G的交换子群的单一共轭类，且支撑在F内的点恰好是affinization映射下V(I_G(F))的原像。通过这些支撑理论建立完备化同构。

Result: 完成了两个主要结果：(1) 对诺特E∞-环上定向P-可除群的完备化定理，统一了AHJM家族完备化定理（取G=μ_{P^∞}在KU上）和Atiyah-Segal完备化定理（平凡族情形）；(2) 推广到局部诺特几何基栈版本，代数理想被G(BG)的开子栈替代，适用于真等变拓扑模形式。

Conclusion: 该工作通过温特征标栈上的支撑理论建立了一个统一的完备化定理框架，将经典的Atiyah-Segal完备化和AHJM家族完备化作为特例囊括其中，并进一步推广至更一般的几何基栈情形，为真等变拓扑模形式等现代课题提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: Let ${\mathbb{G}}$ be an oriented $\mathbb{P}$-divisible group over a noetherian $\mathbb{E}_\infty$-ring $R$, let $G$ be a finite group, and let $\mathcal{F}$ be a family of subgroups of $G$. We show that completion of $R({\mathbb{G}})_G$-modules at $\mathcal{F}$ agrees with algebraic completion at the ideal $I_{\mathbb{G}}(\mathcal{F})=\bigcap_{H\in\mathcal{F}}\mathrm{ker} (π_0R({\mathbb{G}})^{ G}\to π_0R({\mathbb{G}})^{ H}).$ For ${\mathbb{G}}=μ_{\mathbb{P}^\infty}$ over $\mathrm{KU}$ this recovers the family completion theorem of Adams, Haeberly, Jackowski, and May, and for the trivial family the classical Atiyah-Segal completion theorem. The main input is a theory of support for points of the tempered character stack ${\mathbb{G}}\{\mathbb{B} G\}$, in the spirit of Segal's analysis of the prime spectrum of the complex representation ring: we show that the support of a point is a single conjugacy class of abelian subgroups of $G$, and that the points supported inside $\mathcal{F}$ are exactly the preimage of $V(I_{\mathbb{G}}(\mathcal{F}))$ under the affinization map. We also prove a version over locally noetherian geometric base stacks, in which the ideal is replaced by an open substack of ${\mathbb{G}}({\mathbb{B}} G)$, the analogue over such a base of $\mathrm{Spec}\,R({\mathbb{G}})^{ G}$, and which applies for instance to genuine equivariant topological modular forms.

</details>


### [6] [Colimits in Oriented Category Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02592)
*David Gepner,Hadrian Heine*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文发展了"定向余极限"(oriented colimits)的理论，以纠正高维范畴论中lax余极限的几何缺陷。作者引入与Gray张量积兼容的Grothendieck构造版本，证明其在笛卡尔纤维化与$(\infty,\infty)$-范畴的预层之间建立了Gray-富化的等价。定向余极限同时推广了lax余极限和Gray张量积，并应用于更高范畴主丛的分类、高维伴随的表示以及Quillen定理A和B的高维推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维范畴论中，lax余极限虽被视为比普通（同伦）余极限更有用且可以通过合适的局部化恢复普通余极限，但从几何角度看它并非正确的粘合概念。lax余极限与范畴维数、Gray张量积等基本几何操作不相容，因此需要一种既能保持lax余极限优点又能纠正其几何缺陷的新概念。

Method: 作者引入了一个与Gray张量积富化兼容的Grothendieck构造版本，证明了该构造在笛卡尔纤维化和$(\infty,\infty)$-范畴的预层之间诱导出一个Gray-富化的等价。以此为基础定义定向余极限，它同时推广了lax余极限和Gray张量积，其与lax余极限的差异类似于Gray张量积与笛卡尔积的差异。

Result: 作者证明了若干高维范畴论中的基本构造不是lax余极限而是定向余极限的实例，说明了定向余极限的必要性。作为应用，他们分类了高维范畴主丛，用$(\infty,\infty)$-范畴的双笛卡尔纤维化表示高维伴随，并获得了Quillen定理A和B的高维版本。

Conclusion: 定向余极限是lax余极限在几何上更合理的替代品，在维数不超过1时与lax余极限一致，并为高维范畴论提供了一个自然统一的框架，能够优雅地表述主丛理论、伴随表示和Quillen定理等核心概念的高维推广。

Abstract: In higher category theory, lax colimits are often understood to be a more useful and powerful generalization of usual (homotopy) colimits, which can be recovered from the lax colimit by a suitable localization. However, lax colimits do not provide the correct notion of gluing from the geometric perspective. Indeed, they are incompatible with the notion of categorical dimension, the Gray tensor product, and other basic geometric operations. In this paper, we develop the theory of oriented colimits, which correct the defects of lax colimits, and agree with lax colimits in dimension less than or equal to one.
  In order to study oriented colimits, we introduce a version of the Grothendieck construction which is compatible with enrichment in the Gray tensor product. We prove that the Grothendieck construction induces an equivalence between cartesian fibrations and presheaves of $(\infty,\infty)$-categories, which is enriched in the Gray tensor product of $(\infty,\infty)$-categories. Oriented colimits simultaneously generalize the concept of lax colimits and the Gray tensor product, and differ from lax colimits in much the same way in which the Gray tensor product differs from the cartesian product. We demonstrate the necessity of oriented colimits by showing that various fundamental constructions in higher category theory fail to be lax colimits but are instances of oriented colimits.
  As applications, we classify higher-categorical principal bundles, represent higher dimensional adjunctions by bicartesian fibrations of $(\infty,\infty)$-categories, and obtain higher categorical versions of Quillen's Theorems A and B, which admit very natural formulations in our framework.

</details>

### [7] [Gauss Maps in Hyperbolic Surface Theory:A Unified Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00051)
*Magdalena Toda,Erhan Güler*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在统一的框架下综述了双曲三维空间\(\mathbb{H}^3(-1)\)中浸入曲面的多种高斯型映射，包括双曲高斯映射、Legendre高斯提升、调整高斯映射和共形高斯映射，阐述它们的靶空间、解析性质及相互关系，特别讨论了广义DPW方法、调整秩一数据的平坦性要求以及Willmore曲面的调和映射刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双曲三维空间中浸入曲面携带多种自然的高斯型映射，这些映射在几何中扮演不同的角色（如记录法向测地线的理想端点、位置-法向数据与接触结构、规范归一化与Iwasawa分解、平均曲率球丛等），但缺乏一个统一的框架来理解和比较它们。

Method: 作者构建了一个共同的概念框架来统一呈现这四类高斯型映射，系统比较它们的靶空间和解析性质，并着重分析三者核心主题：广义DPW方法、调整秩一数据中的平坦性必要条件、以及Willmore曲面的调和映射特征刻画。这种视角区分了浸入曲面承载的渐近、接触、可积与共形几何信息。

Result: 通过统一框架，论文阐明了各类高斯映射之间的相互关系和区别，揭示了调整高斯映射中平坦性的必要性，并建立了Willmore曲面与调和映射之间的特征联系，从而有效区分了曲面上不同层次（渐近、接触、可积、共形）的几何信息。

Conclusion: 该综述提供了一个全面且统一的视角以理解双曲空间中浸入曲面的各类高斯映射，为后续研究（如DPW构造、Willmore曲面理论）提供了清晰的概念框架和理论支撑。

Abstract: Immersed surfaces in hyperbolic three-space carry several natural Gauss-type maps with distinct geometric roles. The hyperbolic Gauss maps record the ideal endpoints of oriented normal geodesics; the Legendre Gauss lift retains the position-normal data and its contact structure; adjusted Gauss maps arise from gauge normalization and Iwasawa splitting in Weierstrass--Kenmotsu representations; and the conformal Gauss map encodes the mean-curvature sphere congruence in Möbius geometry. We present these constructions in a common framework, emphasizing their target spaces, analytic properties, and mutual relations. Particular attention is given to the generalized DPW method, the necessity of flatness in adjusted rank-one data, and the harmonic-map characterization of Willmore surfaces. The resulting viewpoint distinguishes the asymptotic, contact, integrable, and conformal information carried by an immersed surface in \(\mathbb{H}^{3}(-1)\).

</details>


### [8] [Weighted Hodge Laplacians on Manifolds with Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00244)
*Zhe Su,Yiying Tong,Guo-Wei Wei*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文提出了带边流形上的加权Hodge Laplacian框架，涵盖理论与计算两方面。通过引入流形上的权重函数，在适当的边界条件下建立了加权de Rham-Hodge理论，其调和谱捕捉全局拓扑信息，非调和谱编码由权重引起的局部几何性质。通过蛋白质柔性分析实验验证了方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典无权重Hodge Laplacian在流形数据分析中虽然强大，但无法处理具有变化局部特征的数据。这种限制阻碍了对流形上数据局部几何信息的编码与提取，因此需要一种能够适应局部特征变化的加权框架。

Method: 提出加权Hodge Laplacian框架，在带边流形上引入权重函数；在适当的边界条件下构建加权de Rham-Hodge理论，证明加权Hodge Laplacian的核与加权调和空间一致，且与底层流形的de Rham上同调同构。调和谱保留全局拓扑信息，非调和谱编码局部几何信息。

Result: 通过蛋白质柔性分析的proof-of-principle实验，验证了该方法在分析流形数据拓扑与几何特征方面的有效性和潜力。

Conclusion: 该加权框架使研究者能够在不同权重下研究流形上数据的拓扑和几何特征，并可通过选择突出感兴趣区域的权重来强调局部结构，为流形数据分析提供了更灵活的工具。

Abstract: The spectrum of the Hodge Laplacian on differential manifolds encodes rich topological and geometric information and thus provides a powerful tool for analyzing data on manifolds. However, the classical unweighted formulation is restricted in its ability to study data with varying local features. To address this limitation, we propose a weighted Hodge Laplacian framework for manifolds with boundary, both in theory and in computation, by incorporating a weight function on the manifold. Under appropriate boundary conditions, we formulate the corresponding weighted de Rham-Hodge theory, in which the kernel of the weighted Hodge Laplacian coincides with the weighted harmonic space, and remains isomorphic to the de Rham cohomology of the underlying manifold. The harmonic spectrum of the weighted Hodge Laplacian captures the global topological information, while its non-harmonic spectrum encodes the local geometric property induced by the weight. The proposed framework therefore enables the study of topological and geometric features of data on manifolds across varying weights, and in addition, allows local structure to be highlighted by choosing weights that emphasize regions of interest. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method through proof-of-principle experiments in protein flexibility analysis, and the results show its promise.

</details>


### [9] [Balanced Bismut torsion-parallel fourfold with constant holomorphic sectional curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00457)
*Qingsong Wang,Fangyang Zheng*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了非Kähler几何中的一个经典猜想对所有复维数为4的平衡Bismut扭转平行（BTP）流形成立，即具有常全纯截面曲率的紧致Hermitian流形，当常数非零时必须是Kähler的，当常数为零时必须是Chern平坦的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: An old conjecture in non-Kähler geometry states that, if a compact Hermitian manifold has constant holomorphic sectional curvature, then the metric must be Kähler (when the constant is non-zero) or Chern flat (when the constant is zero). It is known to be true in complex dimension $2$ by the work of Balas and Gauduchon in 1985 (when the constant is negative or zero) and Apostolov, Davidov and Muskarov in 1996 (in general). In dimension $3$ or higher, the conjecture is only known in some special cases, such as for all twistor spaces by the work of Davidov, Grantcharov, and Muskarov, or for the locally conformally Kähler case (when the constant is negative or zero) by the work of H. Chen, L. Chen and Nie, or for all non-balanced Bismut torsion parallel (BTP) manifolds by the work of S. Chen and Zheng, where they also showed that the conjecture holds for all balanced BTP threefolds, utilizing a classification result by Zhao and Zheng. In this article, we show that the conjecture is valid for all balanced BTP manifolds in complex dimension $4$. The interesting part is that while balanced BTP manifolds are highly restrictive, the classification is still lacking in dimensions $4$ or higher, and this study might shed some light on the structure of balanced BTP manifolds in dimension $4$.

Abstract: Let \((M^m,g)\) be a closed Riemannian manifold with \(\sec_g\leq-1\). We prove that the bottom spectrum of its universal cover attains McKean's lower bound if and only if the universal cover is hyperbolic space of constant sectional curvature \(-1\). More generally, for every \(1<p<\infty\), the variational \(p\)-fundamental tone satisfies \[
  λ_{1,p}(\wti M)
  \geq\left(\frac{m-1}{p}\right)^p, \] and equality for some \(p\in(1,\infty)\) holds if and only if \(\wti M\cong\bH^m(-1)\). In that case, equality holds for every \(p\in(1,\infty)\). The proof converts the two McKean defects of a minimizing sequence into a stationary probability measure on the compact horospherical suspension; heat-kernel positivity then forces its zero-defect support to contain a complete leaf.

Abstract: Several inequivalent notions of tangent space exist for diffeological spaces, which has hindered the development of Riemannian geometry in this setting. On Blohmann's elastic diffeological spaces, the tangent functor carries a genuine tangent structure in the sense of Cockett and Cruttwell, and we use this to develop Riemannian geometry.
  We introduce connections adapted to this setting and show that they induce covariant derivatives on vector fields satisfying the usual axioms, together with the associated curvature tensor, whose basic properties we record following Cockett--Cruttwell. Given a Riemannian metric, we establish a Koszul formula and prove that the Levi-Civita covariant derivative is unique if it exists, while its existence remains open in general.
  We then develop the theory of geodesics and of the energy functional: geodesics are always critical paths of the energy functional, and on elastic spaces for which the real line is a curve object, completeness of the geodesic spray yields existence and uniqueness of geodesics, as well as the converse statement that critical paths are geodesics.
  As a fundamental class of examples, we show that for a closed manifold $M$ and an elastic Riemannian diffeological space $N$ these structures on the mapping space $C^\infty(M,N)$ are obtained pointwise from those on $N$, under a natural tangent-commuting hypothesis that holds automatically when $N$ is a manifold; in particular, the geodesic spray of $C^\infty(M,N)$ is complete whenever that of $N$ is.

</details>

Abstract: We obtain closed explicit formulas for the spectral determinants of the smooth hyperbolic Bolza surface and the Klein quartic. For a scalar charge outside the gravastar the self-force is nonzero---in contrast to the exactly vanishing result for a Schwarzschild black hole of the same mass---and behaves as $\tfrac{2}{5}q^2 M R^2/r_0^5$ at large distances, while for an electric charge the universal Smith--Will force $e^2M/r_0^3$ is corrected by a structure-dependent term $\tfrac{4}{5}e^2 M R^2/r_0^5$. Inside the gravastar the scalar self-force is directed toward the center at leading order in $M/R$, vanishes linearly at the center, and produces harmonic oscillations of the charge about the center; the electromagnetic self-force inside the gravastar behaves similarly. The results demonstrate explicitly that the self-force depends not only on the local curvature surrounding the particle but also on the global structure of spacetime: although the exterior geometry of a gravastar is identical to that of a Schwarzschild black hole, the interior boundary conditions modify the regular field and therefore produce distinct self-forces.

Abstract: Cosmological collider signals are usually discussed for isolated massive particles, whose exchange produces characteristic logarithmic oscillations in primordial correlators. In this work, we study how this signal is modified when the exchanged states form a continuous mass spectrum. We first develop a spectral representation for inflationary correlators mediated by a continuum field. In the soft limit, the non-analytic part of the seed function is expressed as a Fourier--Laplace transform of the spectral weight with respect to logarithmic momentum variables. This representation shows that a continuum superposes clock signals with different frequencies and can dephase the sharp oscillation associated with a single massive particle. We then realize this mechanism in an RS2-like inflationary braneworld, where the inflaton is localized on a de Sitter brane in a five-dimensional AdS bulk. The tensor sector contains a localized massless graviton and a continuum of Kaluza-Klein gravitons starting at \(m=3H/2\). We derive the KK wavefunctions and identify the brane spectral weight fixed by the continuum wavefunction on the brane. Applying this weight to the inflaton four-point function, we find a smooth seed function rather than a persistent logarithmic clock oscillation. This behavior follows from the fact that the continuum starts at zero clock frequency, while the KK spectral weight vanishes near threshold. Our results provide a concrete higher-dimensional example in which deviations from the standard cosmological collider signal encode the structure of a continuum high-energy spectrum.

Abstract: We investigate the holographic dual of the extended bulk thermodynamics of dyonic dilaton AdS black holes by allowing both the cosmological constant and Newton's gravitational constant to vary. In the dual CFT thermodynamics, the central charge $C$ and chemical potential $μ$ as its conjugate enter the thermodynamic relations, in addition to $(\tilde{T},\tilde{S})$, $(\tilde{Q},\tildeΦ)$, $(\tilde{P},\tildeΨ)$, $(\tilde{\mathcal{P}},\tilde{V})$. We consider sixteen different ensembles related to those five pairs of thermodynamic variables which we separate into fixed volume and pressure ensembles. Due to the symmetry on ($\tilde{Q},\tilde{P}$), we only need to analyze five ensembles each in fixed volume and pressure ensembles. In the fixed pressure ensembles, it is found that richer structures appear where the sign of $μ$ heavily influences phase space for several ensembles. For fixed ($\tilde{Q},\tilde{P},\tilde{\mathcal{P}},μ$) and ($\tildeΦ,\tilde{P},\tilde{\mathcal{P}},μ$), they show the zeroth-, first-, and second-order phase transitions. For the fixed ($\tildeΦ,\tildeΨ,\tilde{\mathcal{P}},μ$) ensemble, there is only a zeroth-order phase transition occurs. The other ensembles which we do not mention eventually do not show any phase transition. These findings provide deeper insight into how bulk gravitational variations, particularly regarding the cosmological and gravitational constants, translate to exact critical phenomena and richer phase structures within CFT.

Abstract: A conservation-law-guided classification framework is developed for the execution of coarse-graining operations in quantum field theory. Coarse-graining produces a selection at the level of Feynman diagrams. Diagrams with nonzero momentum transfer are suppressed by oscillatory factors, zero-momentum-transfer ladder diagrams can accumulate through geometric series resummation to produce a spectral pole, and single-bubble topologies contribute only to the continuum. Conservation laws govern this classification. For operators protected by a Ward identity, the matrix element at zero momentum is guaranteed to be nonvanishing. For operators protected by BRST symmetry, the Slavnov-Taylor identities provide no mandatory suppression. The two cases differ in the strength of the algebraic guarantee. For unprotected operators the injection term vanishes and the spectral function remains continuous. The sign of the single-bubble contribution is determined by spin statistics. A positive sign leads to amplificative feedback in the ladder resummation, a negative sign leads to suppressive feedback. These two attributes, the nonvanishing of the injection term and the sign of the single-bubble contribution, are the defining criteria of the classification. As a direct application, a general classification of local operators is established within the emergence framework and verified on eight physical channels and three known solvable systems. The framework indicates which emergence paths are possible for each operator; whether the critical condition is reached is left for independent nonperturbative computation. The logical structure of this classification is parallel to the strategy used in deriving fluid equations from molecular kinetic theory in classical statistical physics.

Abstract: We formulate the loop equations of pure $4d, N=1$ super Yang--Mills (SYM) theory in a finite geometric form. The usual equal-point loop derivatives are singular because the order of gauge-field insertions is lost when contour points coincide. Our path-ordered operator calculus (POOC) keeps the insertions in distinct ordered slots, forms the graded commutators and Jacobi combinations, and only then takes the coincidence limit. This removes the spurious kinematical singularities without introducing a cutoff; the genuine ultraviolet contact remains a separate physical distribution.
  We apply POOC to the exact Itoyama--Takashino superloop hierarchy and construct a Lorentzian supersymmetric Hodge-dual (SHD) surface functional. Its Hodge-resolved area derivative is annihilated by the local chiral and anti-chiral loop operators. Exact additivity under surface-geodesic sewing shows that the exponential of this zero mode multiplies any solution of the complete finite-$N$ hierarchy without changing the equations. For planar contours the SHD functional is the geometric area. In particular, for a long rectangular Wilson loop, $W[C_{T,L}]\sim\exp[-iσ_kLT]$ and $E_k(L)=σ_kL>0$. Thus the construction gives an exact nonperturbative confining area-law factor in $ N=1$ SYM.
  After Euclidean continuation, the same SHD factor is an exact fixed point of the deterministic zero-noise SYM gradient flow. Its interpretation as a dynamically selected equilibrium still requires stability in the long-flow-time, large-volume, and zero-noise limits, but this does not affect the exact zero-mode and finite-$N$ dressing theorems. The complete planar Wilson-loop solution, including the undressed fluctuation factor and excitation spectrum, will be developed in the next papers of this series.

The third is a new charge that probes the average of the tail coefficients. We conjecture that the third charge is associated with a new class of large gauge transformations which diverge linearly in retarded time.

</details>

Abstract: Why do local subtractions produce renormalization-group monotones in some settings but fail in others? We propose rank matching. Local counterterms fix how many scale derivatives scheme independence requires. Every derivative adds one connected insertion, while the available positivity inputs are bilinear forms or positive second variations and therefore control only quadratic data. Degree one is the last subtraction whose scale derivative stays within their direct reach. First-order subtractions can close when a Ward or entropic identity supplies a signed quadratic form. Higher orders need extra dynamics. Three exactly solvable tests exhibit both outcomes and show that the endpoint inequality can hold while running monotonicity fails. Massive scalars yield nonmonotone filtered free energies on every odd dimensional $p$-sphere with $p\geq3$. A generalized-free surface-defect $b$-function is strictly monotone when the defect-primary dimension $\widehatΔ$ satisfies $1/2\leq\widehatΔ<1$, and necessarily nonmonotone for $0<\widehatΔ<1/2$. At the threshold, its flow coefficient is completely monotone in the canonical spectral coordinate, with derivatives of every order alternating in sign. A local four-dimensional monodromy defect realizes the full transition. Under a stated assumption on the large-component entropy limit, disk entropy and sphere free energy share endpoints and total $F$ loss but distribute it differently over scale. Rank matching separates endpoint ordering, running monotonicity, and the distribution of loss over scale.

</details>

Conclusion: 本文建立了量子纠错译码与格点规范理论（特别是中心扭绞杨-米尔斯理论）之间的严格对应关系，证明了全局拓扑扇区的抑制（对应量子码的纠错能力）与局域谱性质（对应粒子的质量隙）是两个不同层面的物理量，这一区分对理解禁闭现象和量子码阈值的本质具有重要意义。

Abstract: We study the relationship between quantum error correction, confinement, and lattice Yang-Mills theory. We first formulate decoding for finite Abelian homological codes in terms of higher form gauge fields. For positive local noise, the logical classes are topological sectors of a Nishimori ensemble, and the optimal decoding error is determined by the relative weights of the nontrivial sectors. We derive Fourier relations between logical probabilities, disorder operators, and information in the channel environment, and we give contour and fractional moment criteria for a threshold. We then study a four dimensional $\Z_N$ memory and its possible relation to confining $\PSU(N)$ vacua. Finally, we define a finite curvature center sheet model coupled to Wilson $\SU(N)$ link variables. In this model the conditional logical probabilities are center twisted Yang-Mills partition functions. A strong coupling expansion gives the leading effective interaction for the syndrome and shows that local syndrome correlations can decay even when the global sheet sectors are mixed. We also show that the likelihood for a separated pair of syndrome worldlines is the center monopole correlator. Its decay determines a transfer matrix mass. This distinguishes the suppression of global flux sectors from the local spectral information needed to discuss a mass gap.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jacobian猜想自1939年提出以来一直悬而未决，此前的研究主要围绕该猜想在二维情形的可能有解性展开。2026年7月Alpöge在三维给出反例，随后Gallagher给出无穷族，Speyer给出几何解释。本文的动机是：（1）对这一突破性成果给出一个自包含、完整的阐述；（2）将Speyer的切线扫掠机制从平面曲线推广至更高维超曲面上的方向场，从而在所有大于2的维度上系统性地构造反例。

Method: 本文的核心方法是切线扫掠（tangent-sweep）机制的推广。Speyer的核心洞见是：反例映射通过扫掠平面曲线的切线族来构造，经典对偶性（duality）迫使其映射多数点被多次击中。作者将此机制从平面曲线推广到超曲面上的方向场（direction fields），在任意n>2维中构造反例映射。具体地，给出了五个新显式多项式映射（三维四次、四维五次和十次、五维六次和十二次），所有恒等式通过精确有理算术验证，附录用Gröbner基确定精确纤维结构。

Result: 成功在一切大于2的维度构造Jacobian猜想的反例，且每个维度的反例几何度（典型点的原像个数）可以任意大。文中具体给出五个新反例映射：一个三维四次映射、两个四维（次数分别为5和10）、两个五维（次数分别为6和12）。这些反例同时是满足处处无分歧（étale）、但非真（not proper）的覆盖C^n→C^n，其非单射性仅源于点逃逸至无穷远。

Conclusion: 本文不仅完整阐释了2026年Jacobian猜想三维反例的切线扫掠机制，还将其推广至任意高于二维的情形，从根本上否定了Jacobian猜想在高维的成立可能性。所有计算均以精确有理算术验证，反例具有清晰的几何结构（切线扫掠/方向场）和可控的几何度，为理解Jacobian猜想的失败原因提供了系统的构造框架。

Abstract: The Jacobian conjecture, open since 1939, asks whether every polynomial map of $\C^n$ whose Jacobian determinant is a nonzero constant must have a polynomial inverse. It was refuted in dimension three by Alpöge on July 19, 2026, with an infinite family by Gallagher (July 20) and a geometric explanation by Speyer (July 23): the counterexample sweeps the tangent lines of a plane curve --- a map that classical duality forces to hit most points several times. We give a self-contained account of this tangent-sweep mechanism and generalize it from plane curves to direction fields on hypersurfaces. The resulting construction produces counterexamples in every dimension greater than two and, in each dimension, of arbitrarily large geometric degree (the number of preimages of a typical point). We work it out in five new explicit maps: one three-dimensional of degree four, two four-dimensional of degrees five and ten, and two five-dimensional of degrees six and twelve. The counterexamples provide explicit examples of étale coverings $\C^n\to\C^n$ that are not proper: they are everywhere unramified, and fail to be injective only through points escaping to infinity. All identities were verified in exact rational arithmetic; an appendix determines exact fiber structures through Gröbner bases.

</details>

Abstract: We observe that the motivic analogue of Suslin reciprocity (and similar degree-zero statements) is a formal consequence of localization (plus purity/some six functor formalism). In particular, the statement of Suslin reciprocity for smooth schemes over fields due to Kriz is a corollary of localization for higher Chow groups, over any base; we write down the framework yielding such relations with coefficients for schemes smooth over any base in $\A^1$-invariant motivic cohomology. As an application, we refine some relations between cup products of modular units to be integral in coefficients and in the base: first, we imitate the (rational-coefficients, complex-analytic) Busuioc--Park--Patashnick--Stevens argument for full-level-$N$ elliptic schemes, extending the result to integral bases and coefficients using the elementary reciprocity statement. We then refine the construction and resulting relations to the setting of motivic sheaves; in particular, this gives analogous relations at non-full level structure, as well as over any smooth global quotient stack.

Each cover in Bruhat order contributes a two-face operator determined by a transported coroot, a tail determinant weight, and an explicit Milnor--Witt frame degree. A finite torus-support filtration makes this explicit; after inversion of two it splits by the characters of the component group of the real split torus, and the support spectral sequence degenerates. Abstract: We prove analogues of Tannakian reconstruction theorems of Lurie and Bhatt--Halpern-Leistner in \emph{derived} algebraic geometry, where the basic geometric objects are spectra of animated rings rather than $\mathbb{E}_\infty$-rings. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: J-斜率半稳定性是J-方程可解性的核心判别条件，Datar-Mete-Song猜想将J-斜率半稳定性与拓扑J-斜率通过测试类的最小J-斜率联系起来。该猜想不仅深化了对J-方程和J-流理解的理论理解，也为刻画J-零迹（方程退化点集）的解析性提供了关键工具。

Method: 主要方法包括：(1) 利用代数几何中的big和nef类理论，通过构造适当的双有理测试类来分析J-斜率的下界；(2) 引入J-零迹作为半稳定对的退化集，并利用Kähler几何与toric几何的工具（如曲面分类理论和toric不变量）证明其解析性；(3) 在toric不变情形下，结合Murakami弱解的构造，通过正则性分析证明弱解在稠密大环面上的光滑性和Kähler性。

Result: (1) 完全证明了Datar-Mete-Song猜想：半稳定对的所有big且nef双有理测试类斜率不小于拓扑J-斜率，不稳定对则存在斜率更小的测试类。(2) J-零迹在紧Kähler曲面和紧toric Kähler流形上为解析子集。(3) 在toric不变情形下，Murakami的弱解在(C*)^n上光滑且为Kähler度量。

Conclusion: 本文通过J-斜率测试类刻画了半稳定性，并建立了J-零迹的解析性质。在toric情形下进一步将弱解正则性推进到光滑Kähler解，为J-方程的存在性和正则性理论提供了重要进展，同时证实了Datar-Mete-Song猜想。

Abstract: We prove a conjecture of Datar-Mete-Song \cite{DMS} characterizing $J$-slope semi-stability by the minimal $J$-slope. More precisely, for a semi-stable pair of Kähler classes $(α,β)$ on a compact Kähler manifold $X$, every big and nef birational test class has slope at least the topological $J$-slope, whereas an unstable pair admits a test class with strictly smaller slope. We also introduce the $J$-null locus of a semi-stable pair and prove that it is an analytic subset of $X$ if $X$ is a compact Kähler surface or a compact toric Kähler manifold. In the toric invariant case, we show that Murakami's \cite{Murakami} weak solution to the $J$-equation is smooth and Kähler on the dense big torus $(\mathbb{C}^*)^n$ of $X$.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Popa-Schnell关于Viehweg双曲性的定理推广到更一般的Deligne-Mumford叠的范畴，从而在更广泛的几何背景下建立模空间与双曲性之间的联系。此前的相关工作（如Kebekus-Kovács和Patakfalvi）已在该方向有所推进，本工作旨在借助Campana-Păun的结果和Viehweg-Zuo的方法，将结论拓展到DM叠的情形。

Method: 基于Popa-Schnell的方法论，结合Viehweg-Zuo的经典结果和Campana-Păun的辅助定理，将原本适用于光滑射影簇的论证推广到光滑真DM叠的框架中。通过分析叠的自然对数典范丛的大性（bigness），建立极大变化的一般型族与对数典范丛大性之间的等价关系，并进一步考察粗模空间上的推论。

Result: 证明了对任意光滑真DM叠，若其粗模空间是射影的且该叠参数化极大变化的（具一般型的）概型族，则其自然对数典范丛为大丛。该结果推广了Popa-Schnell在光滑射影簇上的对应定理，并覆盖了Kebekus-Kovács及Patakfalvi的相关推广成果。

Conclusion: 本文在DM叠的框架下建立了Viehweg双曲性定理的新版本，表明该双曲性性质在更一般的模叠结构中依然成立。这为模空间的几何性质分析提供了更广的工具，并在多个典型模空间上给出了具体应用。

Abstract: We generalize work of Popa-Schnell on Viehweg hyperbolicity for smooth projective varieties to the case of smooth Deligne-Mumford stacks. The results of Popa-Schnell build on results of Viehweg-Zuo, utilize a result of Campana-Păun, and extend results of Kebekus-Kovács and Patakfalvi. We show that if a smooth proper DM stack with projective coarse moduli space parameterizes a family of varieties of general type having maximal variation, then the natural log canonical bundle of the stack is big. We also consider implications for the coarse moduli space of the stack, and apply the results to several standard moduli spaces.

</details>

Result: 复杂实现分类了$n\geq5$时分裂二次曲面$Q_{2n-1}$上的定向秩$(n-2)$向量丛，并据此给出了射影模的精确余秩二分裂和有效生成判据。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了特殊线性塔中两个跨阶段态射的奇偶阶段性态，建立了Motivic同伦理论中的代数结果到向量丛分类和射影模分裂判据的桥梁。

Abstract: We determine the two cross-stage morphisms arising from adjacent Stiefel fiber sequences in the special linear tower. Over characteristic-zero fields, the first is the Wood morphism at even stages and vanishes at odd stages; the second is induced stably by the motivic Hopf element at even stages and is zero at odd stages. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究射影空间中曲线法丛的稳定性是代数几何中的基本问题。此前关于P^4中Brill-Noether曲线法丛稳定性的完整刻划尚未给出，且已有结果多限于特征零情形。本文旨在彻底解决这一问题，并将相关结果推广到任意特征。

Method: 本文通过将Larson-Vogt关于N_C(-1)的插值（interpolation）结果从特征零推广到任意特征，结合Brill-Noether曲线的退化与模空间方法，对法丛N_C的稳定性和半稳定性进行精确分类分析。

Result: 给出了P^4中一般Brill-Noether曲线法丛N_C稳定性的完整判据：当且仅当(g,d)不属于五个例外对时N_C稳定；在(3,7)和(5,8)情形N_C为严格半稳定；在(2,6)、(6,9)和(7,10)情形N_C为不稳定。所有结论在任意特征下均成立。

Conclusion: 本文完整解决了P^4中一般Brill-Noether曲线法丛稳定性问题，给出了精确的例外情形分类，并将相关插值结果推广至任意特征，为法丛稳定性的研究提供了统一而完整的框架。

Abstract: We prove that a general Brill--Noether curve $C$ of genus $g \geq 2$ and degree $d$ in $\mathbb{P}^4$ has stable normal bundle $N_C$ if and only if $$(g, d) \notin \{(2,6), (3,7), (5,8), (6,9), (7,10)\}.$$ Moreover, $N_C$ is strictly semistable if $(g, d) \in \{(3, 7), (5, 8)\}$, and is unstable if $(g, d) \in \{(2, 6), (6, 9), (7, 10)\}$. Our results are valid in any characteristic. Along the way, we also generalize previous results of Larson--Vogt on interpolation for $N_C(-1)$, from characteristic zero to arbitrary characteristic.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限阿贝尔子群在代数群中的结构是自 Borel 在 1960 年代初开创的经典课题。此前已有结果对此类子群的结构给出描述，但仍有改进空间。作者希望进一步细化这些结构结果，并通过这些精细结构来解决多个相关的开放问题：Totaro 关于 G-主齐性空间在迭代劳伦级数域上的问题、Tits 关于 E₈-主齐性空间分裂域的乐观假设，以及亏格 1 曲线函数域对某些主齐性空间的分裂问题。

Method: 作者通过对有限阿贝尔 k-子群 A 的精细结构分析，证明存在极大环面 T 使得指数 [A:(A∩T)] 整除 Grothendieck 挠性指数 t(G)，并且商群 A/(A∩T) 在适当意义下是"小"的。这些结构定理随后被应用于主齐性空间的分类问题，通过控制子群的剩余部分来推导关于分裂域和不可分裂性的结论。

Result: (i) 对 Totaro 关于 k_r = k((t₁))((t₂))…((t_r)) 上 G-主齐性空间的问题给出肯定回答；(ii) 证明 Tits 关于 E₈-主齐性空间分裂域的"乐观假设"的一个变体；(iii) 证明某些 k_r 上的主齐性空间不能被亏格 1 曲线的函数域分裂。

Conclusion: 本文通过改进有限阿贝尔子群的结构定理，统一解决了一系列关于主齐性空间分裂性的重要问题，展示了 Grothendieck 挠性指数在控制子群嵌入极大环面时的关键作用，并为相关领域提供了新的工具和视角。

Abstract: Let $k$ be an algebraically closed field, and let $G$ be an algebraic $k$-group. We study finite abelian $k$-subgroups $A \subset G$ whose order is not divisible by the characteristic of $k$. This is a classical topic in the theory of algebraic groups going back to the work of Borel in the early 1960s. We sharpen previously known results on the structure of $A$. In particular, we show that there exists a maximal torus $T$ of $G$ such that the index $[A: (A \cap T)]$ divides the Grothendieck torsion index $t(G)$. We also show that there exists a maximal torus $T$ such that the quotient group $A/(A \cap T)$ is ``small'' in a suitable sense.
  As applications of these results, we (i) give a positive answer to a question of Totaro for $G$-torsors over fields $k_r = k((t_1))((t_2)) \ldots ((t_r))$ of iterated Laurent series, (ii) prove a variant of the ``hypothèse optimiste'' of Tits about splitting fields of $E_8$-torsors, and (iii) show that certain torsors over $k_r$ cannot be split by the function field of a genus $1$ curve.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 结合双有理几何中log canonical singularity的理论工具，对一般自同态f分析其分歧除子R_f的奇点。在n=2的情形，通过考虑f的迭代f^s，利用log canonical threshold的估计与最优下界证明，构造出log Calabi-Yau对。

Result: 证明了对于一般f，对(P^n, R_f)是log canonical的。在n=2时，存在整数s≥1使得lct(P^2; R_{f^s}) ≥ 1/(q^s-1)，且该下界最优。特别地，(P^2, R_{f^s}/(q^s-1))是log Calabi-Yau对，完成了Gongyo猜想在光滑射影曲面上的证明。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了对于一般极化的自同态，其分歧对具有log canonical奇点；对于射影平面情形，迭代是必要的且下界是最优的。最终结果完整证明了光滑射影曲面上Gongyo猜想的正确性。

Abstract: Let $f:\mathbf{P}^n\to\mathbf{P}^n$ be a $q$-polarized endomorphism, where $q>1$, and let $R_f$ be its ramification divisor. We study the singularities of the ramification pair $(\mathbf{P}^n,R_f)$. We show that, for a general $f$, the pair $(\mathbf{P}^n,R_f)$ is log canonical. When $n=2$, we prove that there exists an integer $s\geq1$ such that the log canonical threshold $\mathrm{lct}(\mathbf{P}^2;R_{f^s})\geq1/(q^s-1)$. The passage to an iterate is necessary in general, and the lower bound is optimal. In particular, $(\mathbf{P}^2,R_{f^s}/(q^s-1))$ is a log Calabi--Yau pair, completing the proof of Gongyo's conjecture for smooth projective surfaces.

</details>

Result: 在商栈[X/G]（X为光滑拟射影几何整k-簇，G为线性k-群，k为数域）的设定下，证明了étale-Brauer障碍是最精细的局部-全局障碍，它优于（细于）迭代下降、有限下降、经典下降以及Brauer-Manin等各类障碍。

Conclusion: 本文将局部-全局障碍之间的经典关系从代数簇推广到了商栈[X/G]的范畴，确立了étale-Brauer障碍在各类障碍中的最优（最精细）地位，为后续更广泛框架下研究局部-全局原则提供了理论基础。

Abstract: For quotient stacks [X/G] with X a smooth quasi-projective geometrically integral k-variety and G a linear k-group, where k is a number field, we extend some relations between local-global obstructions on varieties, showing that étale-Brauer obstruction is the finest one among various obstructions (such as iterated descent, finite descent, descent, Brauer-Manin}).

</details>

Conclusion: 该工作将曲线邻域中叶状结构构造从曲面情形推广到任意维数，实现了对叶状结构数量（从0到n）的精确控制，并同时约束了亚纯函数域的大小。这一结果精细化了此前关于无叶状结构或无非常数亚纯函数的曲线邻域构造，为理解高维复流形中叶状结构与亚纯函数之间的关系提供了新的构造性框架。

Abstract: Given a connected projective curve $C \subset \mathbb{P}^n$, $n \geq 2$, and an integer $0 \leq \ell \leq n$, we construct an n-dimensional (non compact) complex manifold, obtained as a neighborhood of an embedded copy of $C$, which carries exactly $\ell$ codimension one holomorphic foliations; moreover, every codimension one distribution on it is one of these foliations. We also determine the field of meromorphic functions of these manifolds: it can be prescribed to be $\mathbb{C}$ or a purely transcendental extension of transcendence degree one, and no larger field is possible as soon as the number of foliations is finite. This extends to arbitrary dimension, and refines, previous constructions of neighborhoods of curves in surfaces without foliations or without non-constant meromorphic functions.

</details>


### [97] [Oka complements of cubic hypersurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02413)
*Song-Yan Xie*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文对复射影空间中维数至少为2的三次超曲面补空间进行Oka性分类（包括可约和不可约、非约化的情况）。结论是：除非三次超曲面是三个不同超平面并集，且它们包含一个公共的余二维射影子空间，否则补空间是Oka的；该例外补空间不是Oka的。证明依赖于多种技术：光滑情形通过无分歧循环三重覆盖和补空间保持的喷雾构造，利用三次曲面上的27条直线以及Kaliman–Zaidenberg的切传输几何；不可约奇异情形通过奇异点投影、Kusakabe局部化定理和Hanysz定理归纳维数；可约情形归约为低次仿射超曲面补空间，用完全向量场和初等标准型识别例外构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决复射影空间中三次超曲面补空间的Oka性质完全分类问题。此前对低维或光滑情形的Oka性已有零散结果，但缺乏对一般三次超曲面（包括可约、非约化情形）的完整刻画。该分类对理解Stein流形到射影空间补空间的全纯映射丰富性以及Oka理论中的正性几何有重要意义。

Method: 研究采用分情形证明策略：(1) 光滑三次情形：通过无分歧循环三重覆盖，沿剩余仿射圆锥曲线构造补空间保持的喷雾；在三次曲面上利用27条直线构成有限支配族；高维情形通过沿直线的爆破重新缩放喷雾，并结合Kaliman–Zaidenberg的切传输几何使切方向在各点张成整个切空间。(2) 不可约奇异情形：从奇异点投影，利用Kusakabe局部化定理和Hanysz关于亚纯图补空间的定理对维数做归纳。(3) 可约情形：归化为由次数不超过2的多项式定义的仿射超曲面补空间，通过构造显式完全向量场和初等标准型来分离并识别唯一的例外构型。

Result: 完成了一般三次超曲面补空间Oka性的完整分类：所有三次超曲面补空间均为Oka，唯一次要例外是三个不同超平面并集且包含公共余二维射影子空间的情形，该补空间不是Oka的。该结果统一并推广了此前对光滑及低维情形的已知结论。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了三次超曲面补空间Oka理论中一般情形的空白，不仅处理了不可约奇异与可约情形，还精确刻画了唯一例外构型。所发展的喷雾构造、切传输几何方法以及向量场方法可望推广到更一般的射影超曲面补空间的Oka性研究中。

Abstract: We classify complements of cubic hypersurfaces in complex projective spaces of dimension at least two, including reducible and nonreduced cubics. The complement is Oka unless the cubic is the union of three distinct hyperplanes containing a common projective subspace of codimension two; this exceptional complement is not Oka. For a smooth cubic, we pass to an unramified cyclic triple cover of the complement and construct complement-preserving sprays along residual affine conics. On cubic surfaces the twenty-seven lines give a finite dominating family. In higher dimensions we rescale the sprays so that they descend from blowups along lines, and use the tangent-transfer geometry of Kaliman--Zaidenberg to make their tangent directions span at every point. For an irreducible singular cubic, projection from a singular point, Kusakabe's localization theorem, and Hanysz's theorem on meromorphic-graph complements yield an induction on dimension. Reducible cubics reduce to complements of affine hypersurfaces defined by polynomials of degree at most two; explicit complete vector fields and elementary normal forms isolate the exceptional configuration.

</details>

*Minghao Wang,Junrong Yan*

TL;DR: 本文证明了在闭实解析Kähler流形上的全纯场论中，Feynman图积分在热核正规化、Cauchy主值正规化和zeta函数正规化下均收敛，且三种正规化方法给出相同的结果。证明基于代数几何中的wonderful紧化理论，并由此建立了这些图积分的规范反常公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Feynman图积分的发散性是微扰量子场论研究的核心问题之一，通常需要正规化才能严格定义这些积分。作者希望为全纯场论中的Feynman图积分提供一个严格的数学框架，证明其在多种正规化方案下的收敛性及一致性。

Method: 作者利用代数几何中的wonderful紧化理论来获得对Feynman图积分的几何理解，并在此框架下分别证明热核正规化、Cauchy主值正规化和zeta函数正规化三种方案下积分的收敛性，进而证明三者收敛到相同的值。

Result: 证明了闭实解析Kähler流形上的全纯场论中，Feynman图积分在三种正规化方案（热核、Cauchy主值、zeta函数）下均收敛且结果一致，并由此推导出这些图积分的规范反常公式。

Conclusion: 该工作为全纯场论中的Feynman图积分提供了严谨的数学基础，确立了不同正规化方案之间的等价性，并通过规范反常公式展示了理论的内在一致性。

Abstract: The divergence of Feynman graph integrals is one of the central issues in the study of perturbative quantum field theories. A rigorous formulation of these integrals usually requires renormalization. In this paper, we prove that the Feynman graph integrals arising from holomorphic field theories on closed real-analytic Kähler manifolds are convergent with respect to heat-kernel renormalization, Cauchy principal value renormalization, and zeta-function renormalization. Moreover, these three renormalization procedures produce the same value. Our proof is based on the theory of wonderful compactifications in algebraic geometry, which provides a geometric understanding of these integrals. As a consequence, we establish a gauge anomaly formula for these graph integrals.

</details>

Method: 利用辛群在拉格朗日格拉斯曼流形上的传递作用，构建拉格朗日Radon变换的辛形式化方法；将Wigner变换的边缘视为基本断层扫描图（tomograms），推广至任意拉格朗日子空间，形成广义断层扫描框架；在此基础上重新审视Pauli重构问题并建立密度算子重构公式。

Result: 建立了统一的广义断层扫描框架，使得位置和动量测量可通过辛旋转统一处理；为Pauli重构问题提供了新的视角，并推导出从广义拉格朗日断层扫描图恢复密度算子的重构公式。

Conclusion: 辛几何框架为量子断层扫描提供了统一且自然的数学基础，拓展了传统Radon变换的应用边界，并为量子态重构问题提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We develop a symplectic formulation of the Lagrangian Radon transform based on the transitive action of the symplectic group on the Lagrangian Grassmannian. This geometric framework naturally extends the classical notion of the marginals of the Wigner transform, viewed as elementary tomograms, to arbitrary Lagrangian subspaces. The resulting generalized tomography provides a unified treatment of position and momentum measurements related by symplectic rotations. As applications, we revisit the Pauli reconstruction problem and establish reconstruction formulas for density operators from generalized Lagrangian tomograms.

</details>


### [100] [Remarks on Regular Approximations to the Robin Aharonov-Bohm Hamiltonian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00637)
*Cesar R. de Oliveira*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维空间中带Robin边界条件（参数L>0）的磁Aharonov-Bohm算子，证明了可通过具有可穿透环形螺线管和可变电导率的Schrödinger算子族来逼近该算子（强预解意义下），并通过Γ-收敛技术实现。同时证明了对螺线管内部施加势垒和光滑势逼近可得到Dirichlet边界条件，结果推广到光滑闭紧曲面及连续有界磁势的一般情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Aharonov-Bohm效应是量子力学中重要的拓扑效应，其数学严格处理通常涉及在螺线管（奇点或薄区域）上施加边界条件。本文旨在构建一类可穿透螺线管模型，通过可变电导率（即势函数的缩放）来严格逼近具有Robin边界条件的磁Aharonov-Bohm算子，从而为这类算子的物理实现和数学理论提供桥梁。

Method: 采用Γ-收敛（Gamma-convergence）技术，在R^3中构造一族Schrödinger算子，其势函数具有可穿透环形螺线管结构且电导率可变（L>0为Robin参数），通过调整电导率参数使算子族在强预解意义下收敛到所期望的极限算子。对于Dirichlet边界条件情形，则进一步结合光滑势逼近和在螺线管内部设置势垒的两步逼近策略。

Result: 成功证明了：(1) 具有可变电导率的可穿透环形螺线管Schrödinger算子族强预解收敛到带Robin边界条件（参数L>0）的磁Aharonov-Bohm算子；(2) 通过光滑势加内部势垒的逼近可得Dirichlet边界条件；(3) 上述收敛结果在更一般的框架下成立，即光滑闭紧曲面及连续有界磁势的设定中。

Conclusion: 本文建立了通过可穿透螺线管模型逼近磁Aharonov-Bohm算子的严格数学框架，利用Γ-收敛技术不仅覆盖了Robin边界条件情形，也覆盖了Dirichlet边界条件情形，并将结果推广到更一般的几何与势能设定，为该类算子的数值模拟和物理应用提供了坚实的理论依据。

Abstract: In the space $\mathbb R^3$, for Robin parameter $L>0$, it is shown that there is a family of Schrödinger operators with penetrable toroidal solenoid and variable conductivity that approximates the magnetic Aharonov-Bohm operator with a Robin boundary condition at the solenoid (border). It is also shown that approximations via smooth potentials and then a barrier in the solenoid interior give Dirichlet boundary conditions. The approximations are in the strong resolvent sense and obtained through the $Γ$-convergence technique, and they hold for the more general setting of smooth, closed and compact surfaces and continuous and bounded magnetic potentials.

</details>


### [101] [Analysis of Conjectural Improvements to Minkowski's Lower Bound on the Sphere Packing Density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00920)
*Carlo Vanoni,Alan Guo,Salvatore Torquato*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Torquato-Stillinger可实现性猜想的前提下，证明了一族超均匀对关联函数可在高维空间中给出形式为φ_max ≳ d^β 2^{-d}的多项式改进下界（对任意固定β>1），且β可随维度增长从而连续逼近指数级改进。此外，作者从Cohn-Elkies对偶线性规划上界公式推导出相同的指数渐近率，表明径向目标测试函数无法渐近排除具有Torquato-Stillinger密度标度的堆积，为高维异常稠密无序球堆积的存在性提供了新证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Minkowski经典下界给出的高维球堆积最大密度的下界在指数基数上远非最优，Torquato和Stillinger猜想可通过对关联函数优化框架实现指数级改进。验证这一猜想需要构造满足可实现性条件的超均匀对关联函数，并证明其能产生相对于Minkowski下界的实质性改进。

Method: 作者使用一族超均匀对关联函数（pair correlation functions）作为测试函数，在Torquato-Stillinger可实现性猜想成立的条件下进行优化。他们将所得结果与Cohn-Elkies对偶线性规划上界公式进行对比分析，从两个独立方向推导相同的密度渐近标度，验证径向目标测试函数在高维情形下无法排除具有该标度的堆积。

Result: 证明了对任意固定β>1，在高维空间中存在超均匀对关联函数族给出φ_max ≳ d^β 2^{-d}的下界，这是对Minkowski下界（约为2^{-d}量级）的多项式改进。当多项式指数β随维度增加时，该结果连续逼近Torquato-Stillinger所猜想的指数级改进。同时，从Cohn-Elkies对偶公式导出了相同的指数渐近率。

Conclusion: 两种独立方法（对关联函数优化与对偶线性规划）得出的一致性结果表明，高维空间中可能确实存在异常稠密的无序球堆积，这为Torquato-Stillinger猜想的指数级下界提供了有力支持，并为该方向后续研究奠定了理论依据。

Abstract: Torquato and Stillinger conjectured an exponential improvement of Minkowski's classical lower bound on the maximal density of sphere packings in high-dimensional Euclidean space $\mathbb{R}^d$ using a pair-correlation-function optimization framework. Conditional on their realizability conjecture, we show that a simple family of hyperuniform pair correlation functions yields polynomial improvements over Minkowski's lower bound of the form $φ_{\mathrm{max}} \gtrsim d^β2^{-d}$ for every fixed $β>1$ in sufficiently high dimensions. As the polynomial exponent is allowed to increase with dimension, this family continuously approaches the previously conjectured exponential improvement. We further derive the same exponential asymptotic rate independently from the Cohn--Elkies dual linear programming upper bound formulation, demonstrating that its radial objective test functions cannot asymptotically exclude packings with the Torquato--Stillinger density scalings. The agreement between these alternative approaches provides new evidence that exceptionally dense disordered sphere packings may exist in high dimensions and strengthens the case for the Torquato--Stillinger conjectural lower bound.

</details>


### [102] [Connes spectral distance on twisted fuzzy torus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01110)
*Zhi-Kang You,Bing-Sheng Lin*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了模糊环面上状态的Connes谱距离，通过交换子和反交换子构造Dirac算子及谱三元组，发现了谱距离间的互反勾股定理、对角态最优元素的对角性以及循环对称性，并研究了共形扭曲下模糊环面中谱距离与共形参数的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Connes谱距离是非交换几何中度量空间概念的重要推广，模糊环面作为非交换几何的典型模型，其谱距离性质尚未被充分研究。理解模糊环面上谱距离的结构有助于深化对非交换空间度量的认识，并为量子引力等物理应用提供数学基础。

Method: 首先通过交换子和反交换子构造模糊环面上的Dirac算子，进而建立对应的谱三元组；分析谱距离的若干性质，包括互反勾股定理；构造最优元素的条件期望函数以实现Lipschitz半范数的收缩；显式计算基础态和简单混合态的谱距离；并引入共形扭曲构造推广的模糊环面，研究共形参数对谱距离的影响。

Result: 证明了谱距离之间存在互反勾股定理；发现对角态的最优元素仍为对角的；显式计算出基础态及若干简单混合态的谱距离；揭示了对角态之间最优元素和谱距离的循环对称性；建立了共形扭曲模糊环面中共形参数与谱距离之间的关联。

Conclusion: 该研究系统地刻画了模糊环面上Connes谱距离的核心性质，揭示了其丰富的对称结构与度量性质，为深入理解非交换几何中的度量概念及后续物理应用提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the Connes spectral distance between states on the fuzzy torus. We construct a Dirac operator by commutators and anticommutators. Based on this Dirac operator, we construct a spectral triple of the fuzzy torus. We study some properties of the spectral distance on the fuzzy torus. We find that there is a reciprocal Pythagorean theorem between the spectral distances. We construct a conditional expectation function of the optimal element which can lead to a contraction of the corresponding Lipschitz seminorm. We find that for any diagonal states, the corresponding optimal elements of spectral distances are also diagonal. We explicitly calculate the spectral distances of some simple states, including basic states and some simple mixed states. We find that there are some kinds of cyclic symmetry in both the optimal elements and the spectral distances between the diagonal states. Furthermore, we also construct a fuzzy torus with some type of conformal twist, and study the relation between conformal parameters and spectral distances.

</details>


### [103] [Dual Variational Principles for Curl Forces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01219)
*Arash Yavari,Amit Acharya*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种针对curl力作用下粒子动力学的对偶变分公式化方法。通过引入位置和速度的对偶变量及辅助函数，构造预对偶作用量，使得运动方程作为约束条件。对偶作用量的欧拉-拉格朗日方程能够恢复原始运动方程及初始条件，从而证明非保守curl力动力学在缺乏普通势能或传统拉格朗日量的情况下仍可拥有变分描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: curl力是位置依赖、非保守且非耗散的力，通常无法从普通势能导出，其运动方程也不遵循标准变分原理。因此，需要一种方法以便在变分框架下处理这类动力学系统。

Method: 论文采用了双重变分公式：引入位置与速度的对偶变量以及一个辅助函数，构造预对偶作用量，其中运动方程作为约束。对原变量求驻值定义了对偶到原变量的映射，代入预对偶作用量后获得完全由对偶变量表示的作用量。该对偶作用量的欧拉-拉格朗日方程能够恢复原始运动方程及规定的初始条件。此外还引入了一个沿驻值对偶轨迹守恒的辅助对偶哈密顿量。

Result: 通过二维和三维中的两个非线性curl力场以及经典Ziegler柱的数值示例验证了该公式的有效性，证明了非保守curl力动力学即使在缺乏普通势能或传统拉格朗日量的情况下也能够拥有变分描述。

Conclusion: 该研究扩展了变分原理在非保守动力学系统中的应用范围，为处理curl力等非保守力的动力学问题提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: Curl forces are position-dependent, non-conservative, and non-dissipative forces that, in general, cannot be derived from an ordinary potential energy. Consequently, their equations of motion do not, in general, follow from a standard variational principle. In this paper, we present a dual variational formulation for particle dynamics under curl forces. By introducing variables dual to position and velocity and an auxiliary function, we construct a pre-dual action in which the equations of motion act as constraints. Stationarity with respect to the primal variables defines a dual-to-primal mapping, whose substitution into the pre-dual action gives an action expressed entirely in terms of the dual variables. The Euler--Lagrange equations of the dual action recover both the original equations of motion and their prescribed initial conditions. We also introduce an auxiliary dual Hamiltonian that is conserved along stationary dual trajectories, although it does not represent the physical energy. The formulation is illustrated using two nonlinear curl force fields in two and three dimensions and the classical Ziegler column. These examples demonstrate that non-conservative curl-force dynamics can admit variational descriptions even in the absence of an ordinary potential energy or a conventional Lagrangian.

</details>


### [104] [Beyond $K$-Theory: Geometry and Holomorphy in Hyperbolic Band Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01596)
*Steven Rayan*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究双曲晶体上自由费米子能带理论中拓扑K-理论的局限性。与欧氏晶体中K-理论提供稳定的分类原理不同，在双曲带论中，K-理论虽然能保留量化配对和局域荷，但会丢失广义动量扇区之间的几何与全纯结构变化。作者在纤维K^0(X)、占据态K^0(B)和算子代数K-理论三种设定下证明了非因子化现象，并建立了Kotani–Sunada底带Hessian与Hodge内积的显式联系，从而通过Torelli定理和单值化恢复双曲曲面本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在欧氏晶体中，拓扑K-理论是带隙自由费米子相稳定分类的决定性工具。但作者指出，在双曲带论中，广义动量扇区通过模空间变化时，普通K-类会丢失其几何与全纯变分信息，导致分类不完整。本文旨在精确刻画这种信息损失的机制与程度，并回答拓扑在双曲情形中仍然保留哪些量的核心问题。

Method: 作者区分了运动学全纯性（复几何组织扇区空间）与动力学全纯性（几何信息输入哈密顿量或投影子），并将信息损失表述为可观测量因子化问题。在紧致双曲曲面X上，从纤维K^0(X)、占据态K^0(B)与算子代数K-理论三个方向证明非因子化。利用Kotani–Sunada底带Hessian与H^1(X;R)上Hodge内积的显式关联（含面积因子），结合整相交形式恢复同调标记的主极化Jacobian，再由Torelli与单值化重建复/双曲曲面。在算术同余塔中对解析可观测量和C^s可观测量分别证明收敛率。

Result: 证明了双曲情形中K-理论在三种设定下的非因子化，受影响量涵盖谱与Higgs谱曲线、Berry全纯与量子度量、部分填充Hall响应、费米面与节点几何；拓扑仍保留量化配对和局域荷。Kotani–Sunada底带Hessian（除面积因子外）恰为Hodge内积，结合整相交形式可恢复主极化Jacobian，并由Torelli和单值化确定底层双曲曲面（但非完整Teichmüller标记）。有限秩扇区与热力学体相分离；局部忠实覆盖精确再现多项式迹并控制连续谱观测量。算术同余塔中解析观测量O(|G_n|^{-η})收敛（η>0），C^s观测量O((log|G_n|)^{-s})收敛。

Conclusion: 拓扑K-理论在双曲带论中不是决定性分类工具，它仅保留离散的量化不变量而丢失连续几何信息。然而，通过将底带Hessian与Hodge理论联系起来，可以恢复比K-类更丰富的几何信息——包括双曲曲面本身（可精确到Torelli意义下）。该工作确立了双曲带论中拓扑、几何与动力学之间的层级关系，并给出不同收敛速率下的谱可观测量的渐近行为。

Abstract: Topological $K$-theory supplies the decisive stable classification principle for gapped free-fermion phases in Euclidean crystals once symmetry and stabilization are fixed. In hyperbolic band theory, by contrast, it is less decisive for the full band problem: generalized momentum sectors vary through moduli spaces, but passing to ordinary $K$-classes collapses their geometric and holomorphic variation. We distinguish kinematical holomorphy, in which complex geometry organizes the sector spaces, from dynamical holomorphy, in which that geometry informs a Hamiltonian or projector, and formulate the resulting loss as an observable-factorization problem.
  For a compact hyperbolic surface $X$, we prove nonfactorization in three settings: fibrewise $K^0(X)$, occupied-state $K^0(B)$, and operator-algebraic $K$-theory. The affected quantities include spectra and Higgs spectral curves, the Berry holonomy and the quantum metric, the partially filled Hall response, and the Fermi surface and nodal geometries. We also identify the quantized pairings and local charges retained by topology.
  Up to an explicit area factor, the Kotani--Sunada bottom-band Hessian is the Hodge inner product on $H^1(X;\mathbb R)$. Together with the integral intersection form, it recovers the homology-marked principally polarized Jacobian and hence, by Torelli and uniformization, the underlying complex and hyperbolic surface, though not a full Teichmüller marking. We also separate finite-rank sectors from the thermodynamic bulk. Locally faithful covers reproduce polynomial traces exactly and control continuous spectral observables. In arithmetic congruence towers, analytic observables converge as $O(|G_n|^{-η})$ for some $η>0$, and $C^s$ observables as $O((\log |G_n|)^{-s})$, while established coherent large-rank limits recover bulk density-of-states moments.

</details>


### [105] [Collective superintegrable systems from the Guillemin--Sternberg torus action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01878)
*L. Feher*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的集体哈密顿系统超可积性方法，通过利用Guillemin-Sternberg环面作用，证明了G-不变函数的函数维度与其中心化子的函数维度之和等于流形维度，从而确立了集体系统超可积性的充分条件，并为Bolsinov和Jovanović的早期结果提供了补充性见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超可积系统是可积系统理论中的重要概念。集体哈密顿量（由动量图拉回G-不变函数所构成）是否超可积是一个经典问题。作者旨在为这一系统提供新的超可积性判据，补充和深化Bolsinov与Jovanović先前的结果。

Method: 作者利用Guillemin-Sternberg（GS, 1983）引入的哈密顿环面作用方法。通过该环面作用，构造动量图，研究Hilbert（或Poisson代数）H=J*(C∞(g*)^G)及其在C∞(M)中的中心化子F，证明函数维度满足ddim(H)+ddim(F)=dim(M)的等式关系。结合非平凡性条件，得出H构成超可积系统，且GS环面作用的动量图可提供作用变量。

Result: 证明了在群G为连通半单紧致李群、M为辛流形的条件下，函数维度等式ddim(H)+ddim(F)=dim(M)成立；在该等式及非平凡性条件下，H表示一个超可积系统；GS环面作用的动量图给出了系统的作用变量。该结果补充了Bolsinov和Jovanović的早期工作。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个新的集体超可积性判据，将超可积性与Guillemin-Sternberg环面作用联系起来，为集体哈密顿系统提供了构造作用变量和第一积分的系统方法，丰富了超可积系统理论。

Abstract: We present a novel approach to the superintegrability of collective Hamiltonians invariant under a Hamiltonian action of a connected semisimple compact Lie group, $G$, on a symplectic manifold, $M$. By exploiting a Hamiltonian torus action that goes back to Guillemin and Sternberg [GS,1983], we demonstrate that the functional dimensions of $\mathfrak{H} := \mathcal{J}^*(C^\infty(\mathfrak{g}^*)^G)$, where $\mathcal{J}: M \to \mathfrak{g}^*$ is the momentum map of the $G$ action, and its centralizer $\mathfrak{F}$ in $C^\infty(M)$ satisfy the equality $\mathrm{ddim}(\mathfrak{H}) + \mathrm{ddim}(\mathfrak{F}) = \mathrm{dim}(M)$. Together with a non-triviality condition, this ensures that the Abelian Poisson algebra $\mathfrak{H}\subset C^\infty(M)$ represents a superintegrable system, and it also follows that the momentum map of the GS torus action yields action variables for the system. Our work provides a new insight into collective superintegrability complementing earlier results of Bolsinov and Jovanović.

</details>


### [106] [Geometric theory of generalised continua using moving frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01996)
*Boris Kolev,Cl{é}ment Ecker*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过规范场论（gauge theory）框架统一分类广义连续介质力学模型（如微态、应变梯度、微极等），解决了模型选择缺乏系统性判据和"物质方向子"物理地位模糊这两个开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义连续介质理论（如应变梯度、微形态、微极模型）在模拟超材料中很有效，但存在两大未解决问题：(1) 缺乏系统性的准则来选择适当的模型；(2) 局部坐标系（"物质方向子"）的物理量状态存在歧义——它们究竟是描述微观结构状态变化的物理量，还是仅仅是描述变形的任意运动学描述符？

Method: 采用有限应变下的规范场论表述。将广义构型定义为经典构型上的移动标架（moving frames），并证明了参考广义构型下的不变性是规范不变性的充要条件。通过结构群约化对一阶广义介质进行系统分类，通过对流标架恢复应变梯度连续介质，并处理受约束介质（如偶应力理论）。

Result: (1) 证明了参考广义构型不变性是规范不变性的充要条件；(2) 将微形态理论恢复为任意物质方向子变形的通用理论；(3) 通过结构群约化实现对一阶广义介质的系统分类；(4) 通过对流标架恢复应变梯度理论，并涵盖偶应力等约束介质。

Conclusion: 该规范场论框架为广义连续介质力学提供了统一的分类体系，区别于现有文献中专注于缺陷等特定微观结构现象的做法，其目标在于对可用力学模型进行统一分类，为模型选择提供系统判据，并澄清了物质方向子的物理地位。

Abstract: Generalised continuum theories couple the macroscopic deformation and the micro-/meso-scopic deformation of an underlying micro-structure. They can account for internal length-scale effects and higher-order mechanical loadings absent from classical Cauchy elasticity and has shown its efficiency in modelling metamaterials. This has led to a proliferation of higher-grade and higher-order models (e.g. strain-gradient, micromorphic, micro-polar) whose underlying kinematic reduction strategies, in particular to reduce the number of material parameters, are rarely identifiable from the free energy alone. It leaves two open issues: the lack of a systematic criterion for selecting an appropriate model, and a persistent ambiguity regarding the physical status of the local frames (''directors of matter'') used in their kinematic description. Are they physical quantities describing the change of state of the given micro-structure or arbitrary kinematic descriptors of its deformation\,? This work addresses both questions through a gauge-theoretic formulation of generalised continua in finite strains. While relying on tools and modelling choices consistent with the existing geometric literature on continuum mechanics, the present approach departs from it in its objectives, aiming at a unifying classification of available mechanical models rather than the description of a specific microstructural phenomenon such as defects. In this work, generalised configurations are defined as moving frames over classical configurations, and invariance with respect to the reference generalised configuration is shown to be a necessary and sufficient condition for a gauge invariance, recovering the micromorphic theory as the general-purpose theory of the deformation of arbitrary directors of matter. A systematic classification of first-order generalised media follows from structural group reduction, while strain-gradient continua are recovered through convected frames, and finally constrained media (e.g. couple-stress) are addressed.

</details>


### [107] [A Hamiltonian approach to the CH-KP equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02028)
*R. Camassa,G. Falqui,E. Sforza*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文从欧拉方程出发，通过长波渐近和哈密顿约化技术推导了浅水层表面准单向波传播的控制模型，得到CH-KP方程（Camassa-Holm-Kadomtsev-Petviashvili方程），这是Kadomtsev-Petviashvili方程的弱非线性版本。模型通过Dirac约化从分层流体的全非线性长波系统获得哈密顿结构，并在特定尺度下约化为经典的一维CH方程，同时提供弱尖峰孤子（peakon）型解的碰撞行为示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在从第一性原理（欧拉方程）出发，系统地推导浅水层表面的准单向波传播模型，建立非线性、色散与第二平面坐标弱依赖性之间的平衡关系，得到一个兼具物理合理性和数学可处理性的模型——CH-KP方程，并揭示其与经典CH方程及KP方程之间的层级联系。

Method: 采用长波渐近分析结合哈密顿约化技术，从欧拉方程推导准单向波传播模型；通过Dirac约化从分层流体的全非线性长波系统获取模型的哈密顿结构；考虑特定尺度将系统约化为CH方程，并构造弱尖峰孤子型解以研究碰撞行为。

Result: 推导得到CH-KP方程作为KP方程的弱非线性版本；建立了模型的哈密顿结构（通过Dirac约化）；证明在特定极限尺度下可还原为一维CH方程；提供弱peakon型解的示例以展示极限方程所支持的碰撞行为。

Conclusion: 该工作为浅水层准单向波的传播提供了一个新的理论框架，在保持哈密顿结构的前提下将全非线性长波系统与经典CH方程联系起来，并通过弱尖峰孤子型解的碰撞行为示例验证了模型的有效性与丰富性。

Abstract: A model governing quasi-unidirectional wave propagation at the surface of a shallow layer of water is derived from Euler equations by long wave asymptotics together with Hamiltonian reduction techniques. The balance between nonlinearity, dispersion and weak dependence on the second planar coordinate is examined and results in the so-called CH-KP equation, a mildly nonlinear version of the well known Kadomtsev-Petviashvili equation. The Hamiltonian structure of the model is obtained by Dirac reduction from the intermediate step of a fully-nonlinear, long-wave system for stratified fluids. A particular scaling is considered that reduces such system to the one-dimensional CH equation in a limiting case. Examples of weak peakon-type solutions are provided, chosen to illustrate the collision behavior supported by the limiting equation.

</details>


### [108] [Quantum Vortices in a Boundary Layer: New Results and Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02057)
*Sergei V. Talalov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了薄圆形量子涡旋丝在无限平面表面附近的运动，该表面周围流体以平行于表面的非零速度运动。通过作者先前发展的新型量子化方法，计算了涡旋的能量谱，发现其有效质量具有张量性质，且在特定量子态下可出现正负两种有效质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子涡旋丝在平面表面附近及平行流场中的运动特性，探索该量子系统与边界层理论的适配性，并揭示涡旋能量谱与有效质量在外流速度影响下的非平凡行为。

Method: 采用作者此前发展的经典闭合涡旋丝的新型量子化方法，结合边界层理论框架，对薄圆形量子涡旋丝在平面表面附近（周围流体以平行于表面的非零速度运动）的量子系统进行建模，进而计算涡旋能量谱E=E(p)并分析其有效质量张量。

Result: 结果表明涡旋能量谱E(p)对速度v具有复杂且非平凡的依赖关系；涡旋的逆有效质量具有张量性质；在特定量子态中，系统可同时存在负有效质量和正有效质量。

Conclusion: 该量子系统适用于边界层理论，且涡旋在平面表面附近运动时表现出丰富的量子特性（如正负有效质量共存），这为理解量子涡旋与表面及外流的相互作用提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Here, we investigate the motion of a thin circular quantum vortex filament near the infinite planar surface. The fluid surrounding this surface moves with a non-zero velocity $\bf{v}$, which is parallel to the surface. We study the specific features of this quantum system and show that they are quite suitable for the boundary layer theory. The developed model allows us to calculate the vortex energy spectrum, $E = E({\bf p})$, where ${\bf p}$ is the total momentum of a vortex ring. 
We have demonstrated that this function has complex non-trivial dependence on the velocity $\bf{v}$. It is stated that the inverse effective mass of a vortex under consideration is of a tensorial nature. In certain quantum states, the system shows the possibility of both negative and positive effective mass existing. This study employs a novel quantization method for classical closed vortex filaments, developed by the author earlier.

</details>


### [109] [$q$-Deformed Topological Recursion: Quantum Curves and Non-perturbative Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02179)
*Fridolin Melong,Raimar Wulkenhaar*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究(r,s)-Airy结构的q-形变及其通过q-差分算子的实现，构建了量子谱曲线与可积系统之间的桥梁，并提供了q-量化曲线对应的全阶q-WKB解，最终给出满足q-拓扑性质的(r,s,q)参数与q-Casimir构型的严格分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将(r,s)-Airy结构与q-形变框架相结合，建立q-量子谱曲线与可积系统之间的联系，并通过q-拓扑递归为q-量化镜像曲线提供严格的数学基础。

Method: 利用q-差分算子构造(r,s)-Airy结构的q-形变，构建全阶q-WKB解；通过推导满足的移位q-圈方程（即q-形变W(gl_r)代数的Ward恒等式），并结合q-拓扑性质条件对可容许的(r,s,q)参数对和q-Casimir构型进行分类。

Result: 证明了得到的非微扰连通q-振幅满足一组移位q-圈方程，可解释为q-形变W(gl_r)代数的Ward恒等式；给出了满足q-拓扑型性质的(r,s,q)参数对与q-Casimir构型的严格分类，确保半经典展开由q-拓扑递归唯一决定。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了q-形变Airy结构与q-拓扑递归之间的严格联系，为q-量化镜像曲线及其底层代数结构提供了新见解，推动了量子谱曲线与可积系统理论的交叉发展。

Abstract: This work investigates the $q$-deformation of $(r,s)$-Airy structures and their realization via $q$-difference operators, providing a bridge between quantum spectral curves and integrable systems. We construct an all-order $q$-WKB solution for the matrix systems associated with the $q$-quantized curve $E_q(x,y)=0$. We demonstrate that the resulting non-perturbative connected $q$-amplitudes satisfy a set of shifted $q$-loop equations, which can be interpreted as the Ward identities of a $q$-deformed $\mathcal{W}(\mathfrak{gl}_r)$ algebra. Our main result provides a rigorous classification of admissible $(r,s,q)$ pairs and $q$-Casimir configurations that satisfy the $q$-topological type property. This ensures that the semi-classical expansion is uniquely governed by the $q$-topological recursion, offering new insights into the $q$-quantization of mirror curves and their underlying algebraic structures.

</details>


### [110] [On the local conformal structure of Imaginary Liouville theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02543)
*Baptiste Cerclé,Romain Usciati*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了虚Liouville理论中的Ward恒等式和Belavin-Polyakov-Zamolodchikov微分方程，为严格实现中心荷小于1的共形场论的共形引导程序迈出了第一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚Liouville理论最近被提出作为中心荷小于1的非有理共形场论的路径积分构造，但其内在性质此前仅在精确计算层面被研究。为了严格实现共形引导程序，需要建立相关恒等式与微分方程。

Method: 假设关于虚高斯乘法混沌的Laplace变换衰减的精确猜想，在此基础上推导Ward恒等式和Belavin-Polyakov-Zamolodchikov微分方程。

Result: 成功建立了Ward恒等式和BPZ微分方程，为共形引导程序在低中心荷共形场论中的严格实施提供了第一步基础。

Conclusion: 本文首次超越精确计算层面，系统研究了虚Liouville理论的内在性质，为未来在此框架下严格开展共形引导程序铺平了道路。

Abstract: Imaginary Liouville theory was recently proposed as a path integral construction of a non-rational conformal field theory (CFT) with central charge less than one (Usciati et al. 2026). In the present work, we establish Ward identities and Belavin-Polyakov-Zamolodchikov differential equations in this framework, assuming a precise conjecture on the decay of the Laplace transform of Imaginary Gaussian multiplicative chaos. These results provide a first step towards a rigorous implementation of the conformal bootstrap program for conformal field theories with central charge lower than one. This work is the first to investigate the intrinsic properties of Imaginary Liouville theory beyond exact computations.

</details>

### [111] [Quantum Simulation of SPAD in the Space Radiation Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00040)
*Durgesh Tinker,Kavita Lalwani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于量子仿真框架的SPAD（单光子雪崩二极管）建模方法，将SPAD建模为三能级量子系统（基态、激发态、陷阱态），利用Jaynes-Cummings模型描述光子-SPAD相互作用，并结合Lindblad主方程和Qiskit门噪声通道模拟空间辐射环境中的器件退化行为，全面获取效率、定时抖动、热暗计数及后脉冲等关键性能参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SPAD在空间量子通信网络中至关重要，但太空辐射环境会导致位移损伤，从而降低探测效率、增加热暗计数、损伤硅晶体并提高后脉冲率。传统TCAD仿真依赖半经典近似，无法捕捉单光子相互作用的离散量子统计特性以及陷阱态的动力学行为，因此需要一种能够精确模拟量子统计效应的新方法。

Method: 论文将SPAD探测器建模为三能级量子系统（基态|g⟩、激发态|e⟩、陷阱态|t⟩）；利用第二量子化与Fock空间截断对光子进行量子建模；采用Jaynes-Cummings模型描述光子-SPAD封闭系统的相互作用；通过Lindblad主方程和Qiskit门噪声通道控制开放系统的动力学演化。该方法可提取效率、定时抖动、热暗计数、后脉冲及辐射效应等SPAD关键特性。

Result: 论文成功通过量子仿真获得了SPAD在空间辐射环境下的关键性能参数，包括探测效率、定时抖动、热暗计数、后脉冲及辐射效应，验证了该量子仿真框架能够全面表征SPAD的退化行为，且与基于半经典近似的TCAD方法在物理机制描述上具有本质区别。

Conclusion: 该量子仿真框架为SPAD在空间辐射环境下的行为提供了更精确的建模手段，弥补了传统TCAD仿真的不足，能够捕捉单光子交互的离散量子统计和陷阱态动力学，为设计抗辐射SPAD器件和保障卫星量子通信可靠性提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: Single-Photon Avalanche Diodes (SPADs) are critical components of emerging quantum communication networks that detect single photons. They are placed in satellites for long-distance communication and are susceptible to radiation-induced displacement damage in space. This degrades SPAD performance parameters, including reduced efficiency, increased thermal dark counts, damage to the Si crystal, and increased afterpulsing rate. This paper simulates SPAD in a space radiation environment by introducing a quantum simulation framework, modeling the SPAD detector as a three-level quantum system, ground state ($|g\rangle$), excited state ($|e\rangle$) and a trap state ($|t\rangle$). Furthermore, to model the photon as a quantum system, second quantization and Fock-space truncation are used. The interaction between the photon-SPAD closed system is simulated using the Jaynes-Cummings model, and the open-system dynamics is governed by the Lindblad master equation and Qiskit's gate-based noise channels. The key characteristics, such as the efficiency, timing jitter, thermal dark counts, and afterpulsing \& radiation effects, are obtained using quantum simulation of SPAD. This approach differs significantly from conventional TCAD simulations, which rely on semiclassical approximations that do not capture the discrete quantum statistics of single-photon interactions and the dynamics of trap states.

</details>


### [112] [Symmetry-initialized quantum Gibbs sampling: a non-Abelian asymmetry cascade](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00055)
*Soo-Jong Rey*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子吉布斯采样器中弱破缺对称性作为混合瓶颈的情况，证明正确的初始化目标由表示论决定，并提出速度提升与预因子之间的二分定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子吉布斯采样器的混合时间受弱破缺对称性瓶颈限制，而传统初始化方法无法有效解决该问题。作者希望从表示论角度建立正确的初始化理论，从根本上加速混合过程。

Method: 通过精确消除慢模式重叠将名义预因子降低转化为渐近加速；对非阿贝尔群G证明正确的初始化目标是群平均不对称性，需要G不变输入；部分子群不变输入产生由子群格索引的级联加速；在SU(2) Davies采样器中解析和数值验证。

Result: 证明了速度提升与预因子的二分定理；建立了非阿贝尔群对称性破缺下初始化目标与表示论的联系；在SU(2) Davies采样器中验证了加速级联现象，弛豫速率随对称性破缺参数线性缩放。

Conclusion: 弱破缺对称性下的量子吉布斯采样器初始化应由表示论决定，群平均不对称性是可直接测量的正确初始化目标，且部分子群不变输入可产生级联加速效果。

Abstract: For a quantum Gibbs sampler whose mixing bottleneck is a weakly broken symmetry, I show that the correct initialization is determined by representation theory. I first prove a general speedup-versus-prefactor dichotomy: by exactly eliminating slow-mode overlap, I convert a nominal prefactor reduction into a fundamental, asymptotic acceleration of the system's mixing time. I then show that when the bottleneck is a weakly broken symmetry, the otherwise exponentially expensive bottleneck eigenvector is the symmetry charge.For a non-Abelian group $G$, I prove that the correct initialization target is the group-averaging asymmetry. Projecting this asymmetry out requires a $G$-invariant input. Partial, subgroup-invariant inputs produce a cascade of mixing-time speedups indexed by the subgroup lattice. Conversely, matching first moments alone is provably insufficient.The asymmetry is a directly measurable initialization target. I verify these results analytically and numerically in an $SU(2)$ Davies sampler where total-spin multiplets constitute the slow modes. The predicted speedup cascade emerges with relaxation rates scaling linearly with the symmetry-breaking parameter. Finally, the model maps the boundaries of the asymmetry target, illustrating where the separate matching of conserved logical data becomes necessary.

</details>


### [113] [Caustics and Superenergy in the Quantum Bouncer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02427)
*Marko. M. Ćosić,Andrew N. Jordan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子弹跳球问题中的经典焦散现象（称为量子焦散），通过高斯波包展示了与经典轨迹族相关的焦散，将焦散附近的相位奇异链与基于尖点灾变线的半经典Pearcey函数联系起来，并量化了超能量（局部能量超过最大本征能量本征值的区域），最后使用Madelung/Bohm轨迹对焦散和超能量行为进行补充描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索经典光学中的焦散现象在量子力学系统中的对应表现，揭示经典-量子对应关系，特别是理解焦散附近的量子干涉效应、相位奇异结构以及能量异常区域的性质。

Method: 采用高斯波包初始条件研究量子弹跳球问题；将焦散附近的相位奇异链与半经典Pearcey函数（基于尖点灾变线）建立联系；量化超能量现象；并使用Madelung/Bohm流体力学轨迹方法对焦散和超能量行为进行补充描述。

Result: 证明了与经典轨迹族相关的焦散在量子弹跳球问题中得以呈现；建立了相位奇异链与Pearcey函数之间的联系；量化了超能量区域的存在和大小；Madelung/Bohm轨迹揭示了轨迹能量及其穿越相位奇异链的行为。

Conclusion: 量子焦散为经典-量子对应提供了一种新的视角，焦散附近的相位奇异结构和超能量现象可通过半经典方法（Pearcey函数）和流体力学轨迹方法（Madelung/Bohm）得到一致的描述。

Abstract: We investigate the quantum interference and energetic phenomena associated with classical caustics in the quantum bouncing ball problem, we refer to as quantum caustics. By considering an initial Gaussian wavepacket, we show that caustics associated with the underlying classical trajectory families are exhibited. We connect the associated phase singularity chains in the vicinity of the caustic with the semiclassical Pearcey function built on the cusp catastrophe lines. We also quantify the amount of superenergy exhibited in these solutions - regions of space where the local energy exceeds the largest constituent energy eigenvalue. We give a complimentary description of the caustic and superenergy behavior using the Madelung/Bohm trajectories, which gives additional insight about the energy of the trajectories and how they traverse the phase singularity chains.

</details>


### [114] [Optimal Decoding for Measurement-Based GHZ State Preparation: The Maximum-Utility Decoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00160)
*Misha Yutushui,Theo Haas,Simon Trebst*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出最大效用解码器（MUD）框架，将基于测量的GHZ态制备中的解码问题转化为最小贝叶斯风险推断，通过最大化解码态的期望效用（而非仅优化二元逻辑恢复）实现最优解码。作者设计了可扩展的两阶段解码器：第一阶段将症候编码为MWPM边权重（使匹配感知规范选择，成本与裸MWPM相同），第二阶段用卷积神经网络微调以最大化期望效用。研究表明仅第一阶段在N=256×256规模上即可达到近最优性能，弥合了裸MWPM与最优解码阈值间87%的差距，确立了可针对具体实验操作需求定制效用函数的通用框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的测量制备宏观GHZ态协议中，传统解码器（如MWPM和MLD）优化的是二元逻辑恢复目标，但在二维几何下无法最大化GHZ态特有的连续长程序。这构成了性能瓶颈，需要一种以连续量子序为优化目标的解码范式。

Method: 将解码问题重新表述为最小贝叶斯风险推断，引入最大化解码态期望效用的MUD框架。构建了最优解码算法，并设计了可扩展的两阶段解码器：第一阶段将症候映射为MWPM边权重从而实现规范感知的匹配；第二阶段使用训练为最大化期望效用的卷积神经网络进行细化。通过重新定义效用函数可使框架适配不同实验需求。

Result: 第一阶段（规范感知MWPM）在N=256×256的最大研究尺度上已近最优表现，弥合了裸MWPM与最优解码阈值之间高达87%的性能差距，且计算成本与裸MWPM相当。两阶段方法以最优解码器的一小部分计算成本实现接近最优的性能。

Conclusion: 最大效用解码器（MUD）推广了MWPM和MLD，建立了可针对具体实验操作需求定制效用函数的通用解码框架，为实现测量制备宏观GHZ态提供了最优解码策略，对量子计量学、密码学和容错编码等量子技术具有基础性意义。

Abstract: The meticulous preparation of macroscopic Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger (GHZ) states provides a foundational resource for quantum technologies such as metrology, cryptography, and fault-tolerant codes. While state-of-the-art measurement-based protocols offer efficient low-depth execution, their performance can be bottlenecked by conventional decoders, such as minimum weight perfect matching (MWPM) or even maximum-likelihood decoding (MLD), which optimize for $binary$ logical recovery and fail to maximize the $continuous$ long-range order characteristic of a GHZ state for two-dimensional geometries. Here we overcome this limitation by framing the decoding problem as minimum Bayesian risk inference, introducing a general paradigm that maximizes the expected ${utility}$ of the decoded state. Implementing this maximum-utility approach, we construct an algorithm that achieves the highest possible per-shot decoded quantum order and thereby establish an optimal decoding strategy for measurement-based GHZ state preparation. To improve its computational efficiency, we design a scalable two-stage decoder, which first encodes the syndromes into the edge weights of MWPM and then refines the result with a convolutional neural network trained to maximize the expected utility, at a fraction of the cost of the optimal decoder. Remarkably, we find that the first stage alone$\unicode{x2014}$which makes the matching aware of the gauge choice at no cost beyond bare MWPM$\unicode{x2014}$already performs near-optimally up to the largest sizes we study, $N=256\times256$, closing up to $87\%$ of the gap between the bare-MWPM and optimal decoding thresholds. Generalizing MWPM and MLD, the maximum-utility decoder (MUD) establishes a versatile framework that can be explicitly tailored to the operational demands of specific experiments by redefining the utility function.

</details>


### [115] [Canonical quantization of the Pais-Uhlenbeck oscillator with a higher-derivative perturbation: a covariant phase space approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00108)
*Jie-qiang Wu,Jinan Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文运用协变相空间形式主义对Pais-Uhlenbeck振子进行微扰正则量子化，将加速度平方项视为微扰，通过构造低能解空间上的辛形式完成量子化，微扰计算得到与精确低能理论展开一致的能量谱和不等时对易子。该方法绕过了标准Ostrogradsky构造，自然解耦了Ostrogradsky幽灵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶导数理论在量子化时面临Ostrogradsky不稳定性问题（负能量幽灵态），标准的Ostrogradsky构造会引入幽灵自由度。作者希望将此前发展的微扰量子化方案推广到真正的高阶导数理论，从而避免Ostrogradsky幽灵的出现。

Method: 采用协变相空间形式主义（covariant phase space formalism），将Pais-Uhlenbeck振子中的加速度平方项视为微扰项，在低能解空间上构造辛形式，在此基础上进行微扰正则量子化，计算能量谱和不等时对易子。

Result: 微扰方法得到的能量谱和不等时对易子与精确低能理论的展开结果一致；该微扰途径绕过了标准的Ostrogradsky构造，实现了Ostrogradsky幽灵的自然解耦，从而将微扰量子化方案成功推广到真正的高阶导数理论。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了微扰量子化方案在高阶导数理论中的有效性，为处理包含高阶导数项的理论提供了一种避免幽灵态困扰的可行途径，是对先前微扰量子化框架的重要扩展。

Abstract: In this paper, we apply the covariant phase space formalism to the perturbative canonical quantization of the Pais-Uhlenbeck oscillator, with the acceleration-squared term treated as a perturbation. We quantize this model by constructing the symplectic form on the low-energy solution space. We then compute the energy spectrum and the unequal-time commutator in a perturbative way, and obtain the results that agree with the expansion of the exact low-energy theory. The perturbation method bypasses the standard Ostrogradsky construction and naturally decouples the Ostrogradsky ghost. This work extends our previous perturbative quantization scheme to genuine higher-derivative theories.

</details>


### [116] [Realization of a Quantum Topological Photon Pump](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00162)
*Qianao Yue,Bernardo Barrera,Martin Ritter,David M. Long,David A. Lane,Anushya Chandran,Alicia J. Kollár*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次实验观测了拓扑光子泵浦，利用transmon量子比特耦合微波腔，在量子区将腔从真空态泵浦至约7个光子，并在前几个循环中产生非经典腔态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑泵浦可以以仅由能带拓扑决定、不受控制场细节影响的量子化速率转移电荷或能量。基于此原理的光子泵浦能够实现对量子腔中非经典态的鲁棒制备，即使在控制存在缺陷的情况下也能工作，但此前从未被直接观测到。
