Method: 实验使用transmon量子比特耦合到微波腔，在量子区实现拓扑光子泵浦操作，从真空态泵浦至约7个光子，并监测产生的腔态统计特性。

Result: 首次实现了拓扑光子泵浦的直接实验观测，泵浦从真空态上升至约7个光子，并在前几个循环中生成了可证实的非经典腔态。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了拓扑光子泵浦在量子区的可行性，为利用拓扑原理鲁棒制备非经典量子态开辟了道路。

Abstract: Topological pumps transfer charge or energy at quantized rates determined solely by band topology, independent of the details of the control fields. Photon pumps operating on this principle could enable the robust preparation of non-classical states in a quantum cavity, even in the presence of control imperfections, but have not been directly observed. We present the first experimental observation of a topological photon pump, using a transmon qubit coupled to a microwave cavity. The pump operates in the quantum regime, pumping up from the vacuum state up to $\approx 7$ photons, and produces demonstrably non-classical cavity states for the first few cycles.

</details>


### [117] [Exact minimum measurement dependence for faithful local deterministic models of multipartite GHZ-Mermin correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00124)
*Aaron Alai*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文解决了Mermin态的忠实版本测量独立性违反代价问题，将其推广到13方，证明忠实性约束是"免费"的，并通过线性规划给出精确最优值序列F(3)=1/3, F(5)=2/5, F(7)=4/9, F(9)=8/17, F(11)=16/33, F(13)=32/65，均满足闭式定律F=R/[2(R+1)]，且最优隐变量密度具有闭式物理形式——制备态受挫稳定子哈密顿量的上下文化基态上的均匀测度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bell推导依赖于局部性、决定论和测量独立性三个假设。此前工作对单重态精确定价了测量独立性假设的代价，并在Kochen-Specker分析中为四组三体Mermin完美关联子定价了1/3的放弃比例，但Mermin态本身的忠实版本最优模型问题一直悬而未决。

Method: 通过归约定理证明忠实性约束不增加代价，使Hall的关联子仅阈值提升为忠实值；利用线性规划求最优解，并通过整数运算下界与显式构造之间的夹逼进行精确验证；对最优隐变量密度给出闭式物理构造，即受挫稳定子哈密顿量基态上的均匀测度，并在三个纠缠类别的簇态上做了样本外验证。

Result: 得到精确最优值F(3)=1/3, F(5)=2/5, F(7)=4/9, F(9)=8/17, F(11)=16/33, F(13)=32/65，每个值（至n=11）在下一个偶数规模重复；所有计算点服从F=R/[2(R+1)]，其中R=2^floor((n-1)/2)为Mermin违反比；证明组合下界在计算的每个核心上紧；并证明普适上界F≤1/2。

Conclusion: 最优隐变量密度有闭式物理形式，显示了量子关联的测量依赖解释所需付出的精确成本曲线。这些"地板"值为多方设备无关证书提供了防伪阈值，并构成了任何测量依赖的量子关联解释必须满足的精确需求曲线。所有定理的完整证明均在正文和附录中给出。

Abstract: Bell derivations rest on locality, determinism, and measurement independence. Hall [Phys. Rev. Lett. 105, 250404 (2010)] priced the third assumption exactly for the singlet state, and in the Kochen-Specker analysis of Phys. Rev. A 84, 022102 (2011) priced the four tripartite Mermin perfect correlators at a surrendered fraction of 1/3, leaving open the problem of an optimal model for the Mermin state itself. This paper solves the faithful version of that problem -- every full correlator reproduced and every proper-subset marginal vanishing -- and extends it to thirteen parties. A reduction theorem shows the faithfulness constraints are free, so Hall's correlator-only threshold is promoted to the faithful value, F(3) = 1/3; linear-programming optima, certified exactly by an integer-arithmetic squeeze between a proven lower bound and an explicit construction, then give F(5) = 2/5, F(7) = 4/9, F(9) = 8/17, F(11) = 16/33, and F(13) = 32/65, each value through n = 11 repeated at the following even size. All computed points obey the closed law F = R/[2(R+1)] with R = 2^floor((n-1)/2) the Mermin violation ratio, a proven combinatorial lower bound is tight on every computed core, and a universal ceiling F <= 1/2 shows the statistics never require total abandonment of measurement independence at any size. The optimal hidden-variable densities have a closed physical form: uniform measures on the contextual ground states of the prepared state's frustrated stabilizer Hamiltonian, a structure confirmed out of sample on cluster states in three entanglement classes. The floors constitute counterfeiting thresholds for multipartite device-independent certificates and an exact demand curve that any measurement-dependent account of quantum correlations must fund. Complete proofs of all theorems are given in the main text and appendices.

</details>


### [118] [A Quantum Reservoir for Neurodynamical Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00139)
*Annemarie Wolff,Kathleen Hamilton,Kahn Rhrissorrakrai,Laxmi Parida,Filippo Utro,Guillaume Dumas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子储层计算（QRC）在神经活动预测中的应用。基于横场Ising模型的量子储层结合异构量子测量和多项式岭回归，在标准基准任务上优于经典对应方法，并已在真实量子硬件上验证可行性。然而在模拟人类脑电（EEG）数据的并行储层架构上，量子储层虽未超越经典方法，但产生了稳定收敛的预测结果，为未来量子系统在临床时间序列预测中的应用建立了基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从短录音中预测神经活动仍是根本性挑战。经典储层计算在小数据场景下通常表现不佳，作者希望探究量子储层计算能否克服这一局限，从而为神经信号（尤其是EEG等生物信号）的时间序列预测提供新范式。

Method: 采用基于横场Ising模型的量子储层，结合异构量子测量（heterogeneous quantum measurements）和多项式岭回归（polynomial ridge regression）进行预测。在标准基准任务上与经典储层对比，并在真实量子硬件上运行同一任务以验证可行性。对EEG模拟数据采用并行储层架构（parallel reservoir architecture）进行评估。

Result: 在标准基准任务上，量子储层整体优于经典储层，预测精度强烈依赖储层参数。在量子硬件上成功运行了同一任务。在模拟EEG数据的并行储层架构测试中，量子储层未匹配经典储层性能，但产生了稳定、收敛的预测结果。

Conclusion: 尽管当前量子硬件和并行储层架构在复杂神经信号上尚未超越经典方法，但QRC可在近期量子设备上执行，并能对类EEG数据进行收敛预测。这项工作为未来面向临床时间序列预测的算法与硬件发展建立了实践基线。

Abstract: Forecasting neural activity from short recordings remains a fundamental challenge. Reservoir computing may offer an efficient paradigm for temporal prediction, however classical reservoirs typically underperform in small-data regimes. Here we investigate whether quantum reservoir computing (QRC) can help overcome this limitation. Building on recent advances, we introduce a quantum reservoir based on a transverse-field Ising model, combined with heterogeneous quantum measurements and polynomial ridge regression. On a standard benchmark task, results show that the quantum reservoir outperforms a classical counterpart overall, with prediction accuracy strongly dependent on reservoir parameters. We further demonstrate feasibility by running the same task on quantum hardware. To assess performance on biological signals, we evaluate QRC on simulated human electroencephalography (EEG) data with a parallel reservoir architecture. On this challenging task, the tested quantum reservoir did not match the performance of the classical one, but it produced stable, convergent predictions. This is a meaningful first step toward forecasting of biologically realistic neural data using a quantum reservoir. Overall, our findings indicate that although current quantum hardware and parallel reservoir architectures do not yet surpass classical methods on complex neural signals, QRC can be executed on near-term devices and does converge with realistic EEG-like data. This work establishes a practical baseline for future algorithmic and hardware developments aimed at clinical time-series forecasting with quantum systems.

</details>


### [119] [Phase transitions in quantum-circuit compilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00189)
*Andrea De Girolamo,Davide Rattacaso,Simone Notarnicola,Ilaria Siloi,Simone Montangero*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次将量子电路编译问题与多体自旋系统的热力学建立联系：电路不保真度充当能量函数，低温态对应编译电路。在平行门间存在串扰时，发现无序相到反铁磁砖墙相的相变（属Ising普适类），更大串扰下出现Z₃有序态。移除酉等价约束后这些相消失，表明电路的酉等价性是其涌现临界性的根源。电路Kolmogorov复杂度增强有序相的涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电路编译需要在高维搜索空间中寻找满足硬件约束（如最小化误差、保持酉等价性）的最优实现，这一问题本质上极其复杂。作者希望通过将其映射到统计力学框架，利用相变和临界现象的理论工具来理解编译过程的复杂性来源。

Method: 将量子电路编译过程映射为多体自旋系统的热力学问题：以电路不保真度作为能量函数，低温自旋态对应编译好的电路。以平行门间串扰为范式场景进行理论分析，研究不同串扰强度下的相变行为与普适类，并系统性地考察移除酉等价约束前后的相图差异，同时分析Kolmogorov复杂度对有序相涌现的影响。

Result: 发现无序相到反铁磁砖墙相的相变，符合Ising普适类；更大串扰下出现Z₃有序区，暗示串行化程度增加与Zₙ有序相涌现相关。移除酉等价约束后有序相消失，证明酉等价性是临界性的根源。Kolmogorov复杂度越高的电路，越容易涌现有序相。

Conclusion: 量子电路编译的复杂性本质上源于酉等价约束，该约束在编译问题中引入了新型的统计力学相变和临界现象。这一发现将编译问题置于更广泛的等价约束优化问题框架中，为理解此类问题的计算复杂性来源提供了新的物理视角，同时也为利用统计力学理论设计更优的编译算法提供了线索。

Abstract: Quantum-circuit compilation aims at finding an optimized realization of a target circuit under given constraints, e.g., the minimization of hardware-induced errors and the unitary-equivalence of the circuit. We connect the compilation process with the thermodynamics of a many-body spin system: circuit infidelity plays the role of the energy function and low-temperature states correspond to compiled circuits. In the paradigmatic case where crosstalk between parallel gates is present, we find a phase transition between a disordered phase and an antiferromagnetic brick-wall phase, compatible with the Ising universality class. At larger crosstalk, we observe a $\mathbb{Z}_3$-ordered regime, suggesting that increasingly serial compiled circuits are associated with emergent $\mathbb{Z}_n$-ordered phases. When the unitary-equivalence constraint is removed, these phases disappear, showing that the equivalence between circuits underlies the emergent criticality and constitutes a source of complexity in quantum-circuit compilation and, more generally, in equivalence-constrained optimization. Finally, we observe that the Kolmogorov complexity of the circuit enhances the emergence of ordered phases.

</details>


### [120] [Marked vertex search on disordered graphs with Rosenzweig-Porter phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00224)
*Sabyasachi Chakraborty,Tilen Cadez,Sonjoy Majumder,Rohit Kishan Ray*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了无序随机图上的标记顶点量子搜索问题。通过在Erdős-Rényi图上实现Rosenzweig-Porter随机矩阵模型，构建了具有可调遍历、非遍历扩展和局域化相的双重随机系统。研究发现搜索性能直接追踪底层量子相：遍历相虽然传输快但成功概率低，而局域化相成功概率高但搜索时间长。这建立了随机矩阵无序与连续时间量子行走搜索性能之间的直接定量联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子标记顶点搜索算法已被证明优于经典算法，但其在无序系统中的行为几乎未被探索。现有研究缺乏对无序如何影响量子搜索动力学的基本理解，需要填补这一空白。

Method: 1) 在Erdős-Rényi图上实现Rosenzweig-Porter模型，形成双重随机系统（ER图控制连接存在性，RP无序控制相互作用强度和位势）；2) 分析该双重随机系统的谱统计特性（Wigner-Dyson到Poisson交叉）；3) 推导有限尺寸局域化边界的解析估计；4) 在此无序图系综中研究标记顶点搜索问题，分析性能与量子相的关系。

Result: 1) RP系综的Wigner-Dyson-to-Poisson谱交叉在图约束下在稀疏到稠密范围内仍然存活；2) 推导出随图边概率p系统性移动的有限尺寸局域化边界，与共振杂化论证一致；3) 搜索性能直接追踪底层量子相；4) 反直觉地发现：遍历相支持快速传输但成功概率更低，而局域化相成功概率高但搜索时间显著更长。

Conclusion: 无序并非仅仅阻碍量子搜索，而是可以作为可调参数被利用。该工作建立了随机矩阵无序与连续时间量子行走搜索性能之间的直接定量联系，为量子搜索协议中的无序调控提供了新视角。

Abstract: Quantum marked vertex search algorithms are known to outperform their classical counterparts, yet their behavior in the presence of disorder remains largely unexplored. Here, we address this gap by studying marked vertex search on disordered random graphs. To introduce disorder, we implement the Rosenzweig-Porter (RP) model, a random matrix ensemble with tunable ergodic, non-ergodic extended, and localized phases, on Erdős-Rényi (ER) graphs. This produces a doubly random system where ER graph connectivity randomizes which interactions exist, while RP disorder controls their strength and `on-site' potentials, providing a two-parameter framework to study quantum dynamics on disordered networks. First, we show that the characteristic Wigner-Dyson-to-Poisson spectral crossover of the RP ensemble survives under graph constraints across the sparse-to-dense range, and we derive an analytical estimate for the finite-size localization boundary that shifts systematically with the graph edge probability $p$, consistent with a resonant-hybridization argument. Thereafter, using this disordered graph ensemble, we study the marked vertex search problem and find that search performance tracks the underlying quantum phase directly. Counterintuitively, the ergodic phase, despite supporting fast transport, yields lower success probability than the localized phase, which achieves high success probability at the cost of significantly longer search times. These results establish a direct and quantitative link between random matrix disorder on graphs and the performance of continuous-time quantum walk search, and suggest that disorder, rather than being merely an obstacle, can be exploited as a tunable parameter in quantum search protocols.

</details>


### [121] [Dissipation in Periodically Driven Quantum Systems: Partial Secularization and Thermodynamic Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00225)
*Luísa T. Tude,Carlos Ortega-Taberner,Roberta Zambrini,Gonzalo Manzano*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出在周期性驱动开放量子系统中，基于完全自发（secular）近似的Floquet-Born-Markov主方程可能导致稳态能量流的非物理预测。作者提出一种粗粒化（coarse-grained）主方程公式，能够在不牺牲完全正定动力学的前提下矫正这些问题，并通过与精确非马尔可夫模拟对比验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期性驱动的开放量子系统是量子热力学和量子控制的核心，通常用Floquet-Born-Markov主方程描述，但其中完全自发近似对稳态能量流的热力学预测可能产生非物理结果，这一问题尚未被充分重视和研究。

Method: 作者证明强自发近似会导致稳态能量流的非物理预测，并提出一种粗粒化主方程公式，该公式在保持完全正定动力学的同时能产生一致的（consistent）能量流。粗粒化时间具有清晰的物理含义：它定义了马尔可夫主方程能够描述驱动系统演化的时间分辨率。通过与精确非马尔可夫模拟对比（驱动二能级系统和三能级maser耦合冷热热库两个范例）验证方法的有效性和一致性。

Result: 粗粒化方法能够修正自发近似导致的非物理能量流问题，保持动力学完全正定性，并在精确非马尔可夫模拟的对比中展现出一致性和有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为在周期性驱动-耗散系统中正确应用自发近似提供了实用框架，并为评估GKSL形式主方程的精度提供了定量依据。

Abstract: Periodically driven open quantum systems are central to quantum thermodynamics and quantum control. These systems are typically described using Floquet-Born-Markov master equations, derived with the use of a full secular approximation, and whose thermodynamic implications are often overlooked. In this context, we show that such a strong secular approximation may lead to unphysical predictions for steady state energy currents. We then demonstrate that a coarse-grained formulation of the master equation can regularize these issues while yielding completely positive dynamics and consistent energy currents. The coarse-graining time has a clear physical interpretation, as it defines the temporal resolution at which a Markovian master equation can describe the evolution of the periodically driven system. We show the consistency and validity of our approach by comparing to an exact non-Markovian simulation in paradigmatic examples: a driven two-level system and a three-level maser coupled to hot and cold thermal reservoirs. Our work provides a practical framework for correctly applying the secular approximation in periodically driven-dissipative systems and for assessing the accuracy of master equations of the GKSL form.

</details>


### [122] [Fault-Tolerant Heisenberg-Limited Quantum Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00171)
*Lorcan O. Conlon,Yu-Xin Wang,Erfan Abbasgholinejad,Victor V. Albert,Michael J. Gullans,Alexey V. Gorshkov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出将容错量子计算的思想引入量子传感，针对一种特定噪声模型（相位翻转错误概率随量子比特数指数减小、比特翻转错误概率为p），证明使用N比特重复码可将海森堡标度的时间窗口从T∝1/p扩展到T∝1/p^((N+1)/2)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子传感器虽有望比经典传感器实现更高的灵敏度，但量子优势的实现条件非常苛刻，噪声、误差或系统标定不佳都会导致量子增强的丧失。作者希望借鉴容错量子计算的思想来克服这些限制，从而在更长时间的传感中保持量子优势。

Method: 作者考虑一个量子比特噪声模型，其中相位翻转错误的概率相对于比特翻转错误概率p呈指数级小。在此噪声结构下，他们采用N比特重复码，并结合容错量子传感协议，在总传感时间T内分析量子传感的标度行为。

Result: 对于N比特重复码，海森堡标度可在长达T∝1/p^((N+1)/2)的时间内实现，而无需容错传感协议时仅能达到T∝1/p。这表明容错方法可显著延长量子传感保持量子优势的时间窗口。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了将容错量子计算的理念应用于量子传感的可行性，在特定噪声结构下可大幅延长量子增强传感的有效时间，为设计对噪声更鲁棒的量子传感器提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Quantum sensors hold great promise for achieving better sensitivity in the measurement of physical quantities compared to their classical counterparts. However, the conditions under which quantum advantage in sensing can be achieved are rather restrictive, and most quantum enhancements in sensing are lost in the presence of noise, errors, or a poorly calibrated system. To overcome these limitations, we are motivated to import ideas from fault-tolerant quantum computing to quantum sensing. Specifically, we consider a qubit noise model where the probability of phase-flip errors is exponentially smaller (in qubit number) compared to the probability of bit-flip errors that occur with probability $p$. For this noise structure, we demonstrate that, given a total sensing time $T$, Heisenberg scaling can be attained for times up to $T\propto 1/p^{(N+1)/2}$ for a $N$-qubit repetition code, in contrast with $T\propto 1/p$ without using a fault-tolerant sensing protocol.

</details>


### [123] [Work Statistics of Autonomous Quantum Energy Pumps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00638)
*Yang Peng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对自主量子能量泵的终端解析功统计理论，通过将供能和受能系统建模为显式量子终端，直接由哈密顿量变化定义每个终端交换的能量。该框架可精确处理泵与终端的任意初态关联，解析周期性/准周期驱动系统中的输运功与泵内累积能量，并揭示了相关性能增强定向能量转移的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子泵浦理论通常通过相位导数定义电流，难以区分输运功与泵自身的能量累积，也无法处理任意初态关联或对功统计进行终端层面的精确刻画。作者希望建立一个更根本的、基于终端哈密顿量变化的功统计理论框架。

Method: 将供能和受能系统显式建模为量子终端（理想钟模型），从全空间算符出发，在泵希尔伯特空间上建立能量的精确表示；恢复周期性及准周期性驱动系统的常规相位导数电流；推导Floquet本征态中的有限周期功统计，建立涨落与Floquet量子几何的联系；对多终端电流互容性、两终端量子比特系统、相干腔基准等情形进行解析求解与数值对照。

Result: 框架成功区分输运功与泵内累积能量；在周期泵中给出Floquet本征态的有限周期功统计并建立涨落-几何联系；两终端量子比特展示噪声匹配现象（输运变锐利）；定义了正功方差间隙量化超出理想钟极限的涨落；相干腔基准证明随占据数增加均值电流精确对齐但功统计仍需更高阶收敛。

Conclusion: 该终端解析框架为自主量子泵浦的功统计提供了统一且精确的微观基础，揭示仅匹配平均电流不足以保证功统计准确性，为量子热力学与量子计量中的能量输运研究提供新工具。

Abstract: We develop a terminal-resolved theory of work statistics for autonomous quantum energy pumps. By modeling the systems that supply and receive energy as explicit quantum terminals, the energy exchanged with each terminal is defined directly from its Hamiltonian change. When the terminals are modeled by ideal clocks, these full-space observables admit exact representations on the pump Hilbert space and recover the conventional phase-derivative currents of periodically and quasiperiodically driven systems. The framework resolves transported work from energy accumulated in the pump. It also incorporates arbitrary initial pump--terminal correlations and identifies when correlations can enhance directional energy transfer under uncertain driving phases. For periodic pumps, we derive finite-cycle work statistics in Floquet eigenstates, relate their fluctuations to Floquet quantum geometry, and show that the long-time terminal currents become mutually compatible. An exactly solvable two-terminal qubit exhibits noise matching, in which transport becomes sharp through cancellation of common-mode terminal fluctuations even though the individual terminal energies remain noisy. Finally, for physical terminals beyond the ideal-clock limit, we introduce a positive work-variance gap that quantifies the fluctuations missed by a pump-only description. A coherent-cavity benchmark shows systematic convergence toward the ideal-clock regime with increasing occupation while demonstrating that agreement of the mean current alone does not guarantee accurate work statistics.

</details>


### [124] [On Identification of Heat and Work in Quantum Many-Body Systems with Local Operations and Classical Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00993)
*Hiroaki Matsueda*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过量子能量传送（QET）协议，基于LOCC优化条件提出了量子热力学中热与功的明确区分方案：优化LOCC时提取能量为功（daemonic ergotropy），未优化时系统中残留不可控能量为热，并推导了两种广义Clausius不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的量子热力学中，热和功分别归因于密度矩阵和哈密顿量的变化，但这种识别方式已被证明存在歧义。同时，涉及量子测量的量子热力学仍不完善。本文旨在解决这一基本问题，为量子多体系统中的热与功提供明确的定义。

Method: 考虑量子多体系统按照量子能量传送（QET）协议通过局域操作与经典通信（LOCC）提取能量。核心思想基于对LOCC优化条件的深刻洞察：当LOCC被优化时，QET的可提取能量成为daemonic ergotropy，可归为功；当LOCC未被优化时，系统中存在不可控的残留能量，经过多体相互作用的仔细处理后归为热。在此基础上推导了两种广义Clausius不等式，并通过一维Kitaev-like模型验证。

Result: 提出了基于LOCC优化条件的量子热与功的明确划分方案。热项包含由远程参与者通信产生的非局域关联，这种关联无法被子系统中的参与者直接观测，与传统热作为不可控能量的视角一致。推导了两种广义Clausius不等式并讨论其方向，用一维Kitaev-like模型验证了有效温度在该热力学中的物理意义。

Conclusion: 通过LOCC优化的视角为量子热力学中热与功的界定提供了新的、无歧义的框架，热量化的不可控性本质与非局域关联相关，且与经典热力学的Clausius不等式在广义层面保持一致。

Abstract: How we identify heat and work is a fundamental question in modern quantum thermodynamics. Usually, heat and work are attributed to changes in the density matrix and the Hamiltonian, respectively, during time-evolution processes in quantum systems. Recently, it has been recognized that this identification is ambiguous. Furthermore, quantum thermodynamics involving quantum measurement is still under development. Motivated by these on-going works, we consider a quantum many-body system from which we extract energy by local operations and classical communication (LOCC) according to the quantum energy teleportation (QET) protocol. The central idea to define heat and work unambiguously is based on a sharp insight into the optimization condition of LOCC. When LOCC is optimized, the extractable energy by QET becomes a daemonic ergotropy; thus, it can be attributed as work. On the other hand, when LOCC is not optimized, we have not squeezed out all the energy with the unitary operation. It means there is uncontrollable energy left in the system. The uncontrollable energy can be attributed as heat after careful treatment of many-body interactions. The heat term consists of nonlocal correlation due to communication between remote participants, and the correlation cannot be directly observed for the participant in the subsystem. Thus, this feature is consistent with the traditional perspective of heat as an uncontrollable energy. To deeply understand the nature of heat, we derive two types of generalized Clausius inequality in our effective quantum thermodynamics, and discuss the direction of the inequality. To justify our perspective, we examine a one-dimensional Kitaev-like model and discuss the physical meaning of effective temperature in our thermodynamics.

</details>


### [125] [Surface adsorbates suppress low-frequency noise for shallow nitrogen-vacancy centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01478)
*Zhiyang Yuan,David A. Fehr,Kalliope Zervas,Sorawis Sangtawesin,Lila V. H. Rodgers,Patryk Gumann,Michael E. Flatte,Nathalie P. de Leon*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过对比超真空（UHV）与大气环境下的浅层氮-空位（NV）中心，意外发现UHV条件下Hahn回波相干时间T2反而降低约4倍，揭示了表面吸附质对NV中心噪声环境的复杂且此前被低估的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浅层NV中心是极具前景的纳米尺度量子传感器，但其相干性受表面诱导噪声严重限制。表面吸附质被广泛认为是退相干的主要来源，但这一假设缺乏直接的实验验证。作者旨在通过UHV实验条件直接测试该假设。

Method: 在UHV条件下保持金刚石表面无吸附质，表征浅层单个NV中心，并与大气环境对比。结合单量子（SQ）和双量子（DQ）基下的Hahn回波测量分离不同噪声源贡献，并辅以T1测量（包括DQ T1）来区分不同频率范围的噪声。

Result: UHV中Hahn回波T2降低约4倍；SQ与DQ测量显示UHV中电噪声和磁噪声均增强；T1测量显示UHV中DQ T1增加，表明约100 MHz频段的电场噪声被抑制。低频噪声与UHV中表面电荷增加相一致，表面吸附质可在大气环境中补偿这些电荷。

Conclusion: 表面吸附质的移除会改变表面噪声谱，不同频率范围由不同微观机制主导。表面吸附质在塑造浅层NV中心噪声环境中的作用是复杂且此前被低估的，对纳米尺度量子传感具有重要启示。

Abstract: Shallow nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centers in diamond are promising nanoscale quantum sensors, yet their coherence is strongly limited by surface-induced noise. Surface adsorbates are widely believed to be a major source of decoherence. Here, we test this assumption by characterizing shallow single NV centers under ultrahigh vacuum (UHV) conditions, where the diamond surface is kept free of adsorbates, and comparing their behavior to ambient conditions. Surprisingly, we observe a ~4x reduction in the Hahn echo coherence time T2 in UHV. By combining Hahn echo measurements in the single-quantum (SQ) and double-quantum (DQ) bases, we separate contributions from different noise sources and find that both electric and magnetic noise are enhanced in UHV. In contrast, T1 measurements reveal an increased DQ T1 in UHV, indicating suppressed electric field noise in the ~100 MHz frequency regime. These results point to a modification of the surface noise spectrum upon adsorbate removal, with different frequency regimes arising from distinct microscopic mechanisms. Specifically, we find that the low frequency noise is consistent with increased surface charge in UHV that can be compensated by surface adsorbates in ambient conditions. Our findings highlight a complex and previously underappreciated role of surface adsorbates in shaping the noise environment of shallow NV centers, with important implications for nanoscale quantum sensing.

</details>


### [126] [No low-degree tests for quantum states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00265)
*Omar Alrabiah,Srinivasan Arunachalam,Sabee Grewal,John Wright*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了低阶相位态的量子测试问题，证明不存在高效的量子低阶测试器：任何测试器判断一个状态是否为d阶相位态（或远离所有此类状态）都需要Ω(⌊m/2⌋选择⌊(d-1)/2⌋)个拷贝。结果通过将量子测试码字相位态与对偶码的经典解码性质联系起来的通用框架获得，利用了高码率Reed-Muller码对随机错误的容错界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典设置中低阶多项式拥有高效的经典测试器，但量子设置中是否也存在类似的量子测试是未知的。作者希望探究量子低阶测试是否存在以及其样本复杂度下界。

Method: 建立了一个通用框架，将码字相位态的量子测试问题归结为对偶码的经典解码性质（特别是对随机错误的容错性）。利用高码率Reed-Muller码对随机错误的已知容错界限，推导出量子低阶测试所需的拷贝数下界。

Result: 证明了不存在高效的量子低阶测试器，任何测试器至少需要Ω(⌊m/2⌋C⌊(d-1)/2⌋)个拷贝。

Conclusion: 与经典情形形成鲜明对比，量子低阶测试不存在高效算法，其样本复杂度随维度参数增长，揭示了量子测试在低阶多项式结构性验证方面本质上比经典更困难。

Abstract: We study the problem of testing low-degree phase states, namely m-qudit quantum states of the form $q^{-m/2} \sum_{x \in \mathbb{F}_q^m} ω^{f(x)} |x>$, where $f$ is a degree-$d$ polynomial. In contrast to the classical setting, where low-degree polynomials admit highly efficient classical testers, it is not known whether analogous quantum tests exist. We show that no such quantum low-degree test exists: any tester requires $Ω(\binom{\lfloor m/2\rfloor}{\lfloor (d-1)/2 \rfloor})$ copies to determine whether a given state is a degree-$d$ phase state or is far from every such state. Our results follow from a general framework that relates quantum testing of codeword phase states to classical decoding properties of the dual code, which allows us to leverage known bounds on the tolerance of high-rate Reed--Muller codes to random errors.

</details>


### [127] [Phase-Space Methods for Many-Body Quantum Optics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00341)
*Edgar Guardiola-Navarrete,Silvia Cardenas-Lopez,Ana Asenjo-Garcia*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了用相空间方法（如截断维格纳近似TWA）处理多体量子光学问题的方法，推导了任意相空间表象下多体耗散演化的精确偏微分方程，讨论了耗散TWA的近似条件，并扩展到多时关联函数的计算，从而拓宽了相空间模拟在开放自旋系统中的适用范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体量子光学系统中，发射体通过共同的电磁储库相互作用，表现出丰富的非平衡行为并有望用于量子技术，但精确数值模拟因希尔伯特空间随系统尺寸指数增长而不可行。半经典相空间方法（如TWA）通过捕捉量子涨落的领头阶提供了高效替代方案，本文旨在系统梳理并推进该方向。

Method: 作者推导了任意相空间表象下支配多体耗散演化的精确偏微分方程，讨论了导出Mink和Fleischhauer提出的耗散TWA所需的近似条件，并发现P和Q分布在多体量子光学中通常不是最优选择。此外，将形式主义扩展至多时关联函数的计算。

Result: 确定了P和Q分布对多体量子光学问题的次优性；建立了任意相空间表象中多体耗散的精确演化方程；成功将相空间模拟扩展到多时关联函数，使开放自旋系统的模拟能够纳入相干性、光谱特性及集体辐射体的方向关联。

Conclusion: 这些发展为研究集体耗散驱动的奇特光源、驱动-耗散相变以及先进实验平台中多种多体现象提供了有价值的工具。

Abstract: Many-body quantum-optical systems, where a collection of emitters interacts through a common electromagnetic reservoir, exhibit rich out-of-equilibrium behavior and hold promise for applications in quantum technologies. However, exact numerical simulations of their dynamics quickly become unfeasible due to the exponential growth of the Hilbert space with system size. Semiclassical, phase-space approaches -- such as the Truncated Wigner approximation (TWA) -- provide computationally efficient alternatives by capturing leading-order quantum fluctuations. In this paper, we present a comprehensive overview of how to tackle problems in many-body quantum optics using phase-space methods. We derive the exact partial differential equation governing many-body dissipative evolution in any phase-space representation and discuss the approximations that yield the dissipative TWA proposed by Mink and Fleischhauer [SciPost Phys. 15, 233 (2023)]. We find that $P$ and $Q$ distributions are generally suboptimal for many-body quantum optics. Additionally, we extend the formalism to calculate multi-time correlation functions, thereby broadening the scope of phase-space simulations of open spin systems to include coherence and spectral properties, as well as directional correlations of collectively radiating emitters. These developments provide valuable tools for investigating exotic light sources driven by collective dissipation, driven-dissipative phase transitions, and a wealth of many-body phenomena arising in state-of-the-art experimental platforms.

</details>


### [128] [Validation and calibration of quantum hardware through the many-body quantum Mpemba effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01788)
*Francesco Campaioli,Marco Avesani,Oren Raz,Roderich Moessner,Gianluca Teza*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用工程化的多体弛豫过程来验证和校准量子硬件的流程。作者在两种独立开发的中性原子处理器上，首次在开放系统中实现多体量子Mpemba效应。通过初始态工程创建快、慢两种弛豫通道：快通道在硬件噪声掩盖目标动力学之前访问难以达到的混合态物理，慢通道则放大制备和控制中的隐藏缺陷。计算基上的测量可直接独立地基准化动力学可靠性，揭示每台处理器作为多体模拟器的真实工作窗口。互补响应可分离控制误差，并驱动自适应的、时间分辨的校准方案，为硬件开发者提供可操作修正，从而增强目标动力学的忠实复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可编程量子处理器在作为多体模拟器运行时，其实际工作窗口（即硬件噪声尚未破坏目标动力学的有效时间范围）难以直接评估。传统验证方法往往在理想条件下进行，无法反映真实运行时的器件缺陷；校准方案也缺乏对控制误差与退相干效应的精细区分。作者希望利用物理本身（即处理器要复现的目标动力学）来评判和校准硬件，从而建立一种可转移、通用的验证与校准工具。

Method: 作者利用精心设计的初始态工程在开放量子系统中实现多体量子Mpemba效应（即"热"态比"冷"态更快弛豫到平衡的异常现象）。在两个独立的中性原子量子处理器上，通过制备快弛豫和慢弛豫两种初态路径：快通道用于在噪声掩盖前提取混合态动力学信息，慢通道用于暴露并放大系统制备与控制中的隐藏缺陷。通过计算基测量直接基准化动力学可靠性，并利用互补的动力学响应将控制误差与噪声效应解耦，驱动一个自适应的时间分辨校准方案。

Result: 实验在两种独立开发的中性原子处理器上首次实现了开放系统中的多体量子Mpemba效应。快慢弛豫通道分别实现了对不可达混合态物理的访问与对隐藏缺陷的放大检测。互补响应成功分离了控制误差，自适应校准方案有效修正了硬件缺陷，提升了目标多体动力学的忠实复现程度，并明确了每台处理器作为多体模拟器的实际可靠运行窗口。

Conclusion: 多体弛豫动力学（尤其多体量子Mpemba效应）被确立为一种可转移、系统性的量子硬件验证与校准工具。该方法的核心优势在于：处理器由它自身旨在复现的动力学来评判，无需外部基准；它同时提供诊断（暴露缺陷）与治疗（指导校准）功能；且适用于不同架构的可编程量子处理器，具有通用性。

Abstract: We introduce a validation process that harnesses engineered many-body relaxation to control and calibrate quantum hardware. On two independently developed neutral-atom processors, we realize the many-body quantum Mpemba effect in an open system for the first time. Initial-state engineering creates fast and slow relaxation pathways: the fast pathway opens access to unreachable mixed-state physics before hardware noise obscures the target dynamics, whereas the slow pathway amplifies hidden imperfections in preparation and control. Computational-basis measurements directly and independently benchmark dynamical reliability, revealing each processor's actual operating window as a many-body simulator. The processors are thus judged by the very dynamics they are built to reproduce. Complementary responses disentangle control errors and drive an adaptive, time-resolved scheme that supplies hardware developers directly actionable corrections, enhancing faithful reproduction of the target dynamics. These results establish many-body relaxation as a transferable validation and calibration tool for programmable quantum processors.

</details>


### [129] [Hamiltonian Thresholds for Objective Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00365)
*Jie Gu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从微观哈密顿量层面推导了经典客观性所需的"见证"（witnessing）与单纯退相干（decoherence）之间的区分法则。通过精确QND乘积监测获得两边缘强达尔文窗口，并推导了冗余见证的可达块容量定律，最终用有限温度自旋相位浴展示了热探针能高效退相干但失去客观见证所需局部非对称性的物理分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子达尔文主义忽视了经典客观性的本质要求——环境不仅要使系统退相干，还必须"见证"（记录）特定的指针值。作者旨在从微观哈密顿量层面严格区分"退相干"与"见证"这两个概念，并建立相应的定量判据。

Method: 采用精确QND（量子非破坏）乘积监测模型，推导出"两边缘强达尔文窗口"——一边是充分见证跨越最优记录判别边缘，另一边是未观测的互补部分跨越残余相干边缘。在额外正则性假设下给出对数量级的补逆界。对受控相位监测，利用环境非对称性推导单一碎片信息界、冗余反转，并在高效i.i.d.流中建立冗余见证的可达块容量定律。最后用有限温度自旋相位浴模型数值验证物理分离。

Result: 获得认证的两边缘强达尔文窗口，建立了见证与退相干的定量区分标准；在受控相位系统中导出了单碎片信息界和冗余关系的反边界；在自旋相位浴模型中展示了热探针可以在保持高效退相干的同时丧失提供客观见证所需的局部非对称性。

Conclusion: 经典客观性不是简单的退相干所能保证的，它需要环境具备对特定指针值的"见证"能力。热环境在温度升高时可能仍然擅长退相干，却因非对称信息丢失而无法充当客观性的见证者，这揭示了量子到经典转变过程中的一个关键物理限制。

Abstract: Classical objectivity requires an environment to witness a pointer value, not merely to decohere it. We derive microscopic Hamiltonian laws for this distinction. Exact QND product monitoring yields a certified two-edge strong-Darwinism window: sufficient witnesses cross the optimal record-discrimination edge while retaining an unobserved complement that crosses the residual-coherence edge. Under additional regularity assumptions, the supplement gives converse bounds at the same logarithmic scale. For controlled-phase monitors, environmental asymmetry gives a single-fragment information bound, a redundancy converse, and, in efficient i.i.d. streams, an achievable block-capacity law for redundant witnesses. A finite-temperature spin-phase bath shows the physical separation: hot probes can decohere efficiently while losing the local asymmetry needed for objective witnesses.

</details>


### [130] [Optimality of Gaussian Entanglement of Formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01909)
*Gerardo Adesso*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了两模高斯态的纠缠形成（EOF）恰好等于其高斯限制下的值，解决了一个长期悬而未决的连续变量量子信息理论难题。关键结果是在一般纯两模态（包括非高斯态）中，纠缠与广义EPR可观测量之间存在尖锐的仿射关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠形成（EOF）是量子信息理论中度量量子纠缠的基本量，但精确计算任意高斯态的EOF一直是一个未解决的难题。此前仅知道高斯限制提供了上界，但无法确定是否达到，因此需要解决这一长期开放的数学问题。

Method: 作者通过建立一个尖锐的仿射（affine）关系，将纠缠与广义EPR可观测量联系起来。这一关系适用于任意纯两模态（包括非高斯态），以此为工具证明了任意两模高斯态的EOF与其高斯限制相等。随后将结果推广到双对称多模高斯态，并为任意非高斯态提供可测量的EOF下界。

Result: 成功证明了两模高斯态的EOF等于其高斯限制；结果推广至双对称多模高斯态；为任意非高斯态的EOF提供了可测量的下界。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了连续变量量子信息理论中长期悬而未决的问题，为高斯态纠缠的实际计算提供了严格的理论依据，同时通过下界为一般非高斯态的纠缠度量提供了实用工具。

Abstract: We prove that the entanglement of formation of every two-mode Gaussian state coincides with its Gaussian restriction, solving a longstanding open problem in continuous variable quantum information theory. The key result is a sharp affine relation between entanglement and a generalized Einstein-Podolsky-Rosen observable, valid for arbitrary pure two-mode states, including non-Gaussian ones. Our result extends to bisymmetric multimode Gaussian states, and also provides a measurable lower bound on the entanglement of formation of arbitrary non-Gaussian states.

</details>


### [131] [Sparse Quantum State Preparation with Sublinear T-Count](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00414)
*Jingquan Luo,Lvzhou Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究稀疏量子态制备的容错代价（以Clifford+T模型中的T门计数衡量），首次实现了亚线性于非零振幅数s的T门复杂度上界，并给出了相应的下界，显著缩小了已知上下界之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任意n量子比特态的T门复杂度已有最优刻画Θ(√(2^n log(1/ε))+log(1/ε))，但对于s-稀疏态（支持集大小至多s），此前最好的上界仍与s呈线性关系。在实际量子态制备中，稀疏态十分常见，因此需要回答：稀疏态的T门复杂度是否可能亚线性于s？本文旨在解决这一理论与实践中都重要的问题。

Method: 作者提出了一种"支持集感知的稀疏布尔函数综合定理"（support-aware synthesis theorem for sparse Boolean functions），利用该定理构造了稀疏态制备电路，实现了Õ(min{s, n^{3/4}√s}+√(s log(1/ε))+log(1/ε))的T门复杂度上界。同时通过参数化分析证明了Ω(min{s,√(ns)})的T门下界。

Result: 上界：任意n-比特s-稀疏态可在误差ε内用Õ(min{s, n^{3/4}√s}+√(s log(1/ε))+log(1/ε))个T门制备，当s足够大时首次实现亚线性于s的复杂度。下界：对所有0<ε≤1/6且2≤s≤2^{n/2}，稀疏态制备至少需要Ω(min{s,√(ns)})个T门，表明小支持集区域中线性依赖不可避免，并显著缩小了该参数范围内的上下界差距。

Conclusion: 本文为稀疏量子态制备的容错代价建立了更精确的刻画：在大支持集区域证明了下界Ω(√(ns))与上界Õ(n^{3/4}√s)之间的差距为n^{1/4}因子，而在小支持集区域则证实了线性依赖的不可避免性。所提出的支持集感知综合定理可能具有更广泛的独立应用价值。

Abstract: We study the fault-tolerant cost of preparing sparse quantum states, measured by $T$-count in the Clifford+$T$ model. Here an $n$-qubit state is called $s$-sparse if it is supported on at most $s$ computational-basis states. For arbitrary $n$-qubit states, the optimal $T$-count is $Θ(\sqrt{2^n\log(1/ε)}+\log(1/ε))$, but for $s$-sparse states the best previous upper bounds remained linear in $s$. We show that any $n$-qubit $s$-sparse state can be prepared up to error $ε$ using $\widetilde{O}(\min\{s,\ n^{3/4}\sqrt{s}\}+\sqrt{s\log(1/ε)}+\log(1/ε))$ $T$ gates, giving the first sublinear dependence on $s$ once the support is sufficiently large. Our approach is based on a support-aware synthesis theorem for sparse Boolean functions, which may be of independent interest. We also prove that, for every $0<ε\le 1/6$ and $2\le s\le 2^{n/2}$, sparse-state preparation requires $Ω(\min\{s,\sqrt{ns}\})$ $T$ gates, showing that linear dependence on $s$ is unavoidable in the small-support regime and substantially narrowing the gap between the known upper and lower bounds within this parameter range.

</details>


### [132] [Characterizing the nitrogen-vacancy center singlet transition and its phonon sideband for absorption-based room-temperature magnetometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02060)
*Tobias Probst,Florian Schall,Sebastian Heuft,Janina J. Schindler,Lukas Lindner,Sven Mägdefessel,Rüdiger Quay,Philipp Reineck,Alexander M. Zaitsev,Takeshi Ohshima,Matthias Weidemüller,Jan Jeske*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用腔增强光谱技术研究金刚石中氮空位（NV）中心在1042 nm单重态零声子线及其声子边带上的吸收基磁测量。通过归一化消除腔增强效应，获得准单程吸收和ODMR对比度，发现1042 nm处对比度最高，并测量了室温下的宽带单重态吸收截面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NV色心磁测量在实际应用中至关重要，传统基于荧光读出的方法受限于对比度和灵敏度。吸收型磁测量技术可通过腔增强吸收长度来提升对比度和灵敏度，因此需要系统研究NV中心的宽带单重态吸收特性，以优化腔增强磁测量方案。

Method: 采用腔增强光谱方法，在室温680-1050 nm光谱范围内研究泵浦激光和微波诱导的腔信号变化。通过归一化消除腔增强效应，提取准单程吸收值和ODMR对比度。提出一种新型方法，利用微波诱导信号变化测量宽带单重态吸收截面，该方法不受其他缺陷引起泵浦激光诱导信号变化的干扰。

Result: 在1042 nm处获得最高ODMR对比度，但在声子边带多个峰值处也发现高对比度点。腔增强ODMR对比度达到50-80%。测得1042 nm处吸收截面为0.89×10^-21 m²或2.9×10^-21 m²，具体取决于所采用的532 nm NV⁻吸收截面值。

Conclusion: 腔增强吸收型磁测量技术能够显著提升ODMR对比度，且声子边带也提供多个高对比度工作点。新提出的微波诱导方法可免干扰地定量测定室温下NV中心单重态跃迁及其声子边带的完整吸收截面。

Abstract: Magnetometry with nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centers in diamond has shown great promise in recent years. In particular, absorption-based magnetometry techniques, employing a cavity to enhance the absorption length, can improve the contrast and sensitivity compared to conventional techniques based on reading out the NV$^-$ triplet fluorescence. The absorption techniques rely on magnetic-field-dependent absorption at the NV$^-$ singlet zero phonon line at 1042$\,$nm and its phonon sideband. In a cavity-enhanced spectroscopy approach, we study pump-laser- and microwave-induced cavity signal changes at room temperature over a spectral range of 680-1050$\,$nm. Through normalization, we eliminate the cavity-enhancement effect and provide quasi-single-pass values for the absorption and optically detected magnetic resonance (ODMR) contrast. The highest contrast is found at 1042$\,$nm, but multiple points of high contrast are found at the peaks of the phonon sideband. Additionally, cavity-enhanced ODMR contrasts in the range of 50-80$\,\%$ are presented. We further measure the broadband singlet absorption cross section at room temperature with a novel method through microwave-induced signal changes. This method is insensitive to pump-laser-induced signal changes by other defects and quantifies the room-temperature absorption strength of the singlet transition and its entire phonon sideband. We determine the absorption cross section at 1042$\,$nm to be $σ^{\,\bigstar}_{1042}=(0.89\pm0.14)\cdot 10^{-21}\,\text{m}^2$ or $σ^{\,\blacktriangle}_{1042}=(2.9\pm0.5)\cdot 10^{-21}\,\text{m}^2$. depending on the employed 532$\,$nm NV$^-$ absorption cross section.

</details>


### [133] [Trading Imaginary Time for Randomness in Ground State Preparation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00443)
*Alvan Arulandu,John M. Martyn,Isaac L. Chuang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"twirled imaginary-time evolution (TITE)"方法，将虚时演化与随机时长的实时演化配对，实现基态制备成本的二次方降低。TITE 在理论上证明可将迹距离到基态的误差二次方抑制，从而将虚时减半（β→β/2）仍保持相同精度，使任意黑盒 ITE 实现的样本复杂度和经典计算成本获得二次方缩减。通过在非可积 Ising 链的含噪电路级模拟中验证了显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚时演化（ITE）是量子计算机上制备基态的基础方法，但因其非酉性质，现有实现在样本复杂度和经典成本上随目标虚时β呈指数增长，且态本身收敛慢于能量，导致任意基态可观测量估计成本更高。

Method: 提出 twirled imaginary-time evolution (TITE)：将 ITE 与按精心设计分布随机抽取时长的实时演化配对。通过随机化机制证明可将到基态的迹距离二次方抑制，从而可将约一半虚时替换为实时演化（β→β/2）而保持相同精度。因实时演化是酉的，不引入样本复杂度或经典计算的额外开销，适用于任何黑盒 ITE 实现（包括 Trotter 化和量子虚时演化）。

Result: 理论上证明 TITE 对迹距离的误差具有二次方抑制效果；在非可积 Ising 链的含噪电路级模拟中验证了相对于标准 ITE 的显著改进。

Conclusion: TITE 为任意基态制备的 ITE 实现提供了通用的二次方成本缩减方案，在保持精度的同时大幅降低样本复杂度和经典计算开销，且对含噪环境具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: Imaginary-time evolution (ITE) is a foundational method for ground state preparation on quantum computers. However, because ITE is non-unitary, existing implementations incur a sample complexity and/or classical cost that scales exponentially with the target imaginary time $β$. Moreover, the state itself converges slower than the energy, making accurate estimation of arbitrary ground state observables even more expensive. In this work, we improve upon standard ITE by introducing twirled imaginary-time evolution (TITE), which pairs ITE with real-time evolution applied for a random duration drawn from a carefully designed distribution. We prove that this randomization quadratically suppresses the trace distance to the ground state, and thus also the error of arbitrary observables, which allows roughly half of the imaginary time to be replaced with real-time evolution ($β\mapsto β/2$) while maintaining the same level of accuracy. Because real-time evolution is unitary and does not incur an overhead in sample complexity or classical computation, this affords a quadratic reduction in the cost of any black-box ITE implementation, including Trotterization and quantum imaginary-time evolution. We demonstrate the efficiency of our algorithm in noisy circuit-level simulations of a non-integrable Ising chain, showing substantial improvements over standard ITE.

</details>


### [134] [Adaptive Spectroscopy of Fast Two-Level-System Dynamics in Superconducting Qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02086)
*Fabrizio Berritta,David Pahl,Lukas Pahl,William P. Banner,Gabriel Cutter,Jan A. Krzywda,Spencer Weeden,Shravan Patel,Paul Buttles,Stanislav Eilhart,Michael Gingras,Bethany M. Niedzielski,Robert McDermott,Mollie E. Schwartz,Kyle Serniak,Max Hays,Jeffrey A. Grover,William D. Oliver*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文报道了利用亚秒级自适应光谱技术，在FPGA控制器上实现了对超导量子处理器中寄生两级系统（TLS）缺陷的快速动力学观测，发现TLS具有约数秒的特征切换时间和约0.9 MHz²/s的谱扩散系数，比传统非自适应光谱快约300倍，并揭示了TLS涨落与门级错误之间的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寄生两级系统（TLS）缺陷是超导量子处理器中能量弛豫和时序不稳定性的主要来源，但传统非自适应光谱方法需要数小时测量时间，无法观测较快的TLS动力学过程，从而限制了对TLS行为及其对量子门性能影响的理解。

Method: 采用基于FPGA的控制器实现亚秒级自适应光谱测量，在可调谐超导量子比特上以亚秒时间分辨率同时获取频率分辨和时间分辨的弛豫信息；通过随机基准测试（randomized benchmarking）关联TLS诱导的涨落与门级错误。

Result: 观察到TLS的电报式切换具有约数秒的特征时间尺度，谱扩散系数D约为0.9 MHz²/s，比传统非自适应光谱观测到的时间尺度快约300倍；在多个独立制备的器件和不同实验室中观察到相似的缺陷动力学；TLS涨落与门级错误存在相关性。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了一个此前无法触及的频率分辨TLS动力学新领域，重新定义了超导量子处理器中TLS感知表征与校准所相关的时间尺度，为理解和缓解TLS缺陷对量子处理器性能的影响提供了新的途径。

Abstract: Parasitic two-level-system (TLS) defects are a major source of energy relaxation and temporal instability in superconducting quantum processors. Our sub-second adaptive spectroscopy reveals telegraphic switching of TLSs with a characteristic timescale of a few seconds and spectral diffusion with diffusivity $D \approx 0.9~\mathrm{MHz}^2/\mathrm{s}$. These timescales are about $3 \times 10^2$ times faster than what is observed in conventional nonadaptive spectroscopy, which typically requires hours of measurement time. We resolve such fast dynamics on a field-programmable gate array (FPGA)-based controller that enables measurement of frequency- and time-resolved relaxations with sub-second temporal resolution in flux-tunable superconducting qubits. We observe similar defect dynamics across multiple qubits in independently fabricated devices measured in different laboratories. We correlate TLS-induced fluctuations with gate-level errors using randomized benchmarking. Our results reveal a previously inaccessible regime of frequency-resolved TLS dynamics and redefine the timescales relevant to TLS-aware characterization and calibration of superconducting quantum processors.

</details>


### [135] [Geometry-Informed Polynomial Time Quantum Approximation Schemes for Constrained Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01121)
*Chinonso Onah,Kristel Michielsen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了噪声量子采样器何时能产生具有性能保证的多项式时间端到端优化算法。基于约束增强型QAOA的有限深度和有限采样保证，作者证明了逆多项式最优集合理想概率结合独立采样、多项式时间可行性修复和评分，可产生精确命中FPRASq（全多项式随机近似方案）。该保证在实例相关窗口内对设备噪声具有鲁棒性。作者进一步提出Heavy-Hitter QAOA，在保留条件保证的同时将候选集保留和经典后处理成本降低一个问题规模的幂。IBM Eagle r3处理器上的硬件实验覆盖多达一百个逻辑变量的实例，匹配或改进了QOptlib参考回路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题在于：噪声量子采样器何时能提供具有端到端性能保证的多项式时间优化算法？这是一个基础性理论问题，涉及量子计算能否在噪声条件下对NP难优化问题提供可验证的优势。作者希望通过严格的理论框架来刻画噪声量子采样器与经典采样器在优化场景下的分离条件，并进而通过硬件实验验证其可行性。

Method: 本文采用理论分析与实验验证相结合的方法。理论上，构建在约束增强QAOA的有限深度和有限采样保证之上，通过逆多项式理想概率、独立采样、多项式时间可行性修复和评分四个要素构造精确命中FPRASq。分析了设备噪声窗口内外的不同保证性质，并提出NP-HQ算法适配Chen-Cotler-Huang-Li预言机模型以证明量子-经典分离。同时引入Heavy-Hitter QAOA以减少候选集和后处理成本。实验上，在IBM Eagle r3处理器上实现对一百个逻辑变量实例的测试。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 证明了在逆多项式理想概率条件下可构造精确命中FPRASq；(2) 该保证在实例相关噪声窗口内成立，有效电路深度与层数和问题规模乘积呈线性关系时，保持逆深度分数的最优质量会将采样复杂度增加一个问题规模幂次；(3) 在窗口之外，确定性修复保证可行性并提供实例相关近似保证；(4) 在NP-hard核容许优化承诺族上，经典多项式时间采样器重现逆多项式最优重叠将蕴含NP⊆BPP，表明分离在于采样分布生成；(5) 硬件实验在IBM Eagle r3上覆盖了100个逻辑变量实例，匹配或改进所有QOptlib参考回路。

Conclusion: 本文建立了噪声量子采样器用于端到端优化算法的严格理论框架，证明了在合理条件下可提供具有性能保证的FPRASq。关键结论是：量子与经典之间的分离不在于可行性修复或约束结构访问，而在于采样分布的生成本身。Heavy-Hitter QAOA在保留理论保证的同时提升了后处理效率，硬件实验也验证了方法的实际可行性。

Abstract: When does a noisy quantum sampler yield an end-to-end polynomial-time optimization algorithm with performance guarantees? Building on finite-depth and finite-shot guarantees for Constraint-Enhanced QAOA, we show that inverse-polynomial ideal probability on the optimal set, together with independent sampling, polynomial-time feasibility repair, and scoring, produces an exact-hit fully polynomial randomized approximation scheme, which we call an FPRASq.
  This guarantee survives device noise within an instance-dependent window. For effective circuit depth linear in the product of layer count and problem size, preserving an inverse-depth fraction of the ideal optimal mass increases the required shot complexity by one power of the problem size. Beyond this window, deterministic repair guarantees feasibility and provides an instance-dependent approximation guarantee whenever the induced objective inflation is controlled.
  The resulting NP-HQ algorithm fits the Chen-Cotler-Huang-Li oracle model. On any NP-hard kernel-admissible promise family, reproducing its inverse-polynomial optimal overlap with a polynomial-time classical sampler would imply that NP is contained in BPP, even with identical repair and perfect access to the constraint structure. Thus, the separation lies in generating the sampling distribution.
  We further introduce Heavy-Hitter QAOA, which preserves these conditional guarantees while reducing the retained candidate set and classical post-processing cost by one power of the problem size. Hardware experiments on IBM Eagle r3 processors cover instances with up to one hundred logical variables and match or improve every tested QOptlib reference tour.

</details>


### [136] [What is electric charge?: Charge as a local observable in relativistic quantum field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00468)
*Hideyasu Yamashita*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文质疑现代量子物理中"电荷是什么"这一基本问题尚未得到明确回答，指出总电荷算符Q的非局域性使其不是通常意义上的可观测物理量。虽然4-电流算符j被视为"局域电荷"的候选，但其严格定义在(3+1)维闵可夫斯基空间中难以实现，且即使能定义，其作为"局域电荷算符"的可解释性也存疑。作者回归Araki和Wyss(1964)的工作，提出"范围局域电荷"Q_ζ(P)的概念，在抽象C*-代数框架下进行讨论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子物理学未能清晰回答"什么是电荷"这一基本问题。总电荷算符Q虽然定义良好，但由于其非局域性，不是传统意义上可被局域实验装置测量的可观测量。这促使作者寻求电荷的"局域版本"定义。

Method: 作者分析了4-电流算符j作为局域电荷候选方案存在的问题：在(3+1)维闵可夫斯基空间中j的严格定义困难重重，且在(1+1)维中即便能够定义，其作为局域电荷算符的解释力也存疑。作者转而借鉴Araki和Wyss(1964)的早期工作，提出"范围局域电荷"Q_ζ(P)的概念，其中P为有限维投影，并在抽象C*-代数框架下展开论述。

Result: 作者论证了4-电流算符作为"局域电荷算符"的局限性，并提出了一种新的替代方案——"范围局域电荷"Q_ζ(P)，为局域电荷的概念提供了新的数学框架。

Conclusion: 作者认为，在抽象C*-代数框架下引入"范围局域电荷"Q_ζ(P)，为回答"什么是（局域）电荷"这一基本物理问题提供了新的思路，相较于4-电流算符方案更具概念上的合理性。

Abstract: It is rather surprising that modern quantum physics does not appear to have provided any clear answer to the simple question ``what is electric charge?''. Even when the total charge operator $Q$ is well-defined, the non-locality of $Q$ implies that it is not an observable in the usual sense, which can be measured by a (local) experimental apparatus. A candidate for the ``local version'' of charge operator is the 4-current operator $j=(j^μ)_{μ=0,1,2,3}$. However, it is known that the rigorous definition of $j$ is difficult in $(3+1)$-dimensional Minkowski space. Although it was found that the current can be defined in $(1+1)$-dimensions (Carey et al.), I argue that even when $j$ can be suitably defined, the interpretability of $j$ as the ``local charge operator'' is dubious. Instead I return to Araki and Wyss (1964), and propose the concept of ``scope-local charge'' $Q_ζ(P)$ for a ``scope'' $P$, expressed by a finite-dimensional projection. I work in an abstract $C^{*}$-algebraic setting.

</details>


### [137] [Cooperative Platoon Routing and Dispatching via Edge-Assisted Hybrid Quantum Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00524)
*Talha Azfar,Ruimin Ke*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种边缘辅助的闭环评估流水线，用于编队感知的车辆路径规划。通过路侧单元感知交通运动学并识别路段稳定性，仅在物理上适合近距离协调的路段激活编队奖励。将多车辆路径规划问题直接建模为QUBO形式，免去MILP线性化辅助变量。在Troy, NY的24小时SUMO微观仿真中实现18.5%的牵引能量减少，并在ibm_boston量子硬件上通过Linear-Chain QAOA实现66.7%的CNOT深度降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同编队行驶能降低CAV车队的能耗，但在不稳定的城市交通中，车辆需要在相容的时间于同一路段汇合并保持协调，使得路径规划问题变得困难。现有方法缺乏基于实时交通感知的闭环评估，且编队交互建模通常依赖MILP线性化辅助变量，计算代价高。

Method: 1) 路侧单元（RSU）通过视频估计局部交通运动学，分类路段流量稳定性，并仅在不稳定但物理上适合近距离协调的路段激活编队奖励。2) 将多车辆编队路径规划问题直接表示为QUBO模型，配对编队交互以原生二次Ising项表达，避免MILP线性化变量。3) 使用24小时SUMO微观仿真（Troy, NY）评估框架，并在IBM量子硬件（ibm_boston）上使用Linear-Chain QAOA进行基准测试。

Result: SUMO仿真显示相对于非协同基线可实现18.5%的车队牵引能量需求降低。在25个活跃量子比特的基准实例上，Linear-Chain QAOA相比dense QAOA将两量子比特CNOT深度降低66.7%，在p=2时以38.6%的可行性概率和14.2%的最优概率采样到精确经典基态。

Conclusion: 边缘感知与浅层量子优化相结合，可作为闭环CAV编队调度中的有效组成部分，展示了物理感知的路段筛选与QUBO原生编队建模在降低能耗和量子计算开销方面的协同优势。

Abstract: Cooperative platooning can reduce the energy use of Connected and Autonomous Vehicle (CAV) fleets, but the routing problem becomes difficult when vehicles must meet on the same road segments at compatible times while moving through unstable urban traffic. This paper develops an edge-assisted, closed-loop evaluation pipeline for platooning-aware vehicle routing. Roadside Units estimate local traffic kinematics from video, classify segment-level flow stability, and activate platooning rewards only on road segments where close-gap coordination is physically appropriate. The resulting multi-vehicle routing problem is written directly as a Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO) model, so pairwise platooning interactions are represented as native quadratic Ising terms instead of requiring auxiliary MILP linearization variables. We evaluate the framework using a 24-hour microscopic SUMO simulation of Troy, NY, together with localized IBM Quantum hardware benchmarks. The SUMO study shows an $18.5\%$ reduction in fleet tractive-energy demand relative to a non-cooperative baseline. On 25-active-qubit benchmark instances executed on $\texttt{ibm_boston}$, Linear-Chain QAOA reduces two-qubit CNOT depth by $66.7\%$ compared with dense QAOA and samples the exact classical ground state with $P_{\text{feas}} = 38.6\%$ and $P_{\text{opt}} = 14.2\%$ at $p=2$. These results suggest that edge perception and shallow quantum optimization can work together as a useful component of closed-loop CAV platoon dispatching.

</details>


### [138] [Quantum computer-based simulation of Stark many-body localization in a 1D Fermi-Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02245)
*Abdul Kalam,Prasenjit Deb,Akitada Sakurai,Tapan Mishra,V. S. Prasannaa,B. P. Das*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在IBM超导量子计算机上使用12量子比特的一维Fermi-Hubbard模型，通过自旋分辨Jordan-Wigner变换、SWAP网络和张量网络电路优化等编译技术，模拟了Stark多体局域化（MBL）动力学，并观测到从热化到局域化的交叉行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体局域化（MBL）是描述孤立量子多体系统非遍历性的重要动力学现象，与热化相反，它导致初始状态的长期记忆和纠缠的缓慢增长。作者希望利用当前噪声中等规模量子（NISQ）硬件来研究这一现象，推动量子模拟在真实的量子计算设备上的实现。

Method: 作者使用12量子比特对应一维Fermi-Hubbard模型，采用自旋分辨的Jordan-Wigner变换进行编码，结合SWAP网络和基于张量网络的量子电路优化例程（叠加在标准电路优化流水线上）进行编译，实现了两量子比特门数量减少88%和电路深度减少87%。利用Trotter化量子电路模拟实时动力学，研究不同外加势场倾斜强度下的系统行为。

Result: 研究表明，在弱倾斜场下系统表现出热化动力学，在强倾斜场下（即使演化时间较短）系统表现出明显的局域化行为，实现了从热化到Stark局域化的交叉观测。结果与精确模拟进行了基准对比验证。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了在当前的噪声超导量子硬件上，通过先进的电路编译优化策略，可以有效模拟Stark多体局域化这一非平衡量子多体现象，为在NISQ设备上进行更大规模的有意义量子模拟提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Many-body localization (MBL) is a dynamical phenomenon that describes the non-ergodicity of isolated quantum many-body systems. In contrast to thermalization, this phenomenon leads to a long-lived memory of initial states of local systems and slow growth of entanglement. In this work, we study Stark MBL in a 12-qubit correlated fermionic system described by the one-dimensional Fermi-Hubbard model using Hamiltonian simulation on an IBM superconducting qubit quantum computer. To enable such a computation on current-day noisy hardware, we combine a series of compilation steps, including the use of the spin-resolved Jordan-Wigner transformation, employing SWAP networks, and integrating a tensor-network-based quantum circuit optimization routine on top of a standard circuit optimization pipeline. As a result, there is approximately an 88$\%$ and 87$\%$ reduction in two-qubit gate count and circuit depth, respectively. Through such simulations of the real-time dynamics using Trotterized quantum circuits, we exhibit a crossover from thermalizing dynamics of the system at a weak tilt of the field to a strongly localized behavior at large tilt with short evolution times. We also benchmark our obtained results with respect to those from exact simulations.

</details>


### [139] [Multiparameter quantum estimation in a photon system induced by gravitational redshift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00550)
*Wei Ye,Hui Cao,Songtao Zhang,Xiang Zhu,Huan Zhang,Ying Xia,Shixun You,Daisheng Zhang,Shoukang Chang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了引力红移作用下光子系统在幅值阻尼和Ohmic类退相通道中的多参数量子估计问题，发现量子Cramér-Rao界在双参数估计中不够紧，并通过半定规划数值计算了Holevo界和Nagaoka界，证明Nagaoka界最为紧致；在三参数估计中，强耦合与sub-Ohmic区域分别展现出更高的估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子穿过弯曲时空时引力红移会显著扭曲波包，因此有必要在此背景下研究参数估计。已有研究仅涉及单参数量子Cramér-Rao界，多参数场景尚未被探索，作者希望填补这一空白。

Method: 在幅值阻尼和Ohmic类退相两种噪声通道下，对遭受引力红移的光子系统进行两参数（初始相位和权重参数）及三参数（额外包含引力红移强度）量子估计。由于量子Cramér-Rao界不够紧致，采用半定规划数值求解Holevo Cramér-Rao界和Nagaoka界作为更紧的误差界。

Result: 1) 在两种噪声通道下，量子Cramér-Rao界对双参数估计均无法提供紧致误差界；2) Nagaoka界在所有考虑的界中最紧，符合多参数量子估计的一般层级结构；3) 幅值阻尼通道下，强耦合区域的三参数估计精度显著优于弱耦合区域；4) Ohmic类退相通道下，sub-Ohmic区域始终比Ohmic和super-Ohmic区域提供更高的估计精度。

Conclusion: 多参数量子估计框架适用于引力红移背景下光子系统的参数估计分析，且选择适当的紧误差界（如Nagaoka界）和耦合参数区域（强耦合或sub-Ohmic）可以显著提升估计精度。

Abstract: As photons propagate through curved spacetime, gravitational effects become unavoidable. In particular, gravitational redshift can induce significant distortion in photon wave packets, making it es?sential to investigate parameter estimation within this context. While previous research has focused on single-parameter estimation using the quantum Cramer-Rao bound, the multiparameter scenario remains largely unexplored. In this work, we investigate multiparameter quantum estimation for a photon system subject to gravitational redshift under both amplitude-damping and Ohmic-like dephasing channels. Our analysis reveals that the quantum Cramer-Rao bound fails to provide a tight error bound for the two-parameter estimation involving the initial phase and weight parameters inboth types of noisy channels. To overcome this limitation, we numerically compute two tighter error bounds, i.e., the Holevo Cramer-Rao bound and the Nagaoka bound, when utilizing a semidefinite program. We demonstrate that the Nagaoka bound yields the tightest error bound among all considered bounds, consistent with the general hierarchy of multiparameter quantum estimation. Furthermore, for the three-parameter estimation, including the initial weight parameter, the phase parameter, and the strength of gravitational redshift, we observe significantly enhanced estimation precision in the strong-coupling regime compared to the weak-coupling regime under the amplitude-damping channel. Similarly, in the Ohmic-like dephasing channel, the sub-Ohmic regime consistently affords higher precision than the Ohmic and super-Ohmic regimes.

</details>


### [140] [Spread complexity as a probe in generalized and long-range Aubry-Andre-Harper models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02451)
*Triyas Sapui,Tanoy Kanti Konar,Subinay Dasgupta,Aditi Sen De*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了广义及长程Aubry-Andre-Harper(AAH)模型中量子淬火的传播复杂度(spread complexity)，发现其长时间平均的传播复杂度在准周期势跨越与初始本征态能量相关的迁移率边时呈现非解析行为，从而可精确识别迁移率边转变。作者还推导了动量矩和相应Lanczos系数的解析表达式，揭示了不同模型中Lanczos系数的定性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索传播复杂度作为一种诊断工具，用于识别广义AAH模型中存在能量依赖的迁移率边（mobility edges）时的量子相变，特别是在量子淬火动力学背景下，弥补传统AAH模型研究中缺乏迁移率边信息的不足。

Method: (1) 对广义AAH模型（含迁移率边）和长程AAH模型（含LR跳跃）进行量子淬火后的传播复杂度分析；(2) 利用Krylov空间方法计算传播复杂度；(3) 通过本征态局域密度态(LDOS)的扩展性验证所得行为；(4) 针对淬火从零到强准周期势的极限情形，解析推导复杂度动量矩及Lanczos系数。

Result: (1) 在广义AAH模型中，长时间平均传播复杂度在淬火后的准周期势跨越与初始本征态能量对应的迁移率边时出现非解析变化，可准确标记迁移率边转变；(2) Lanczos系数呈现初始平台后随Krylov基指数衰减的行为，不同于无迁移率边的常规AAH模型中近乎常数的表现；(3) 对长程跳跃，从局域相到扩展相的淬火中系数随Krylov基指数衰减，而反向淬火则与AAH结果一致。

Conclusion: 传播复杂度可以有效区分广义AAH模型中的迁移率边转变，并提供了一种新的量子混沌/局域化诊断工具。Lanczos系数的行为模式取决于是否存在迁移率边，这为研究具有能量依赖迁移率边的系统中量子淬火动力学提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: We investigate the spread complexity of quantum quenches in generalized and long-range Aubry-Andre-Harper (AAH) models, encompassing regimes with and without mobility edges. In particular, in the generalized AAH models supporting energy-dependent mobility edges, we demonstrate that the long-time averaged spread complexity exhibits nonanalytic behavior when the post-quench quasiperiodic potential crosses the mobility edge associated with the energy of the initial eigenstate, thereby accurately identifying the mobility-edge transition. Such a behavior is supported by the spreading of local density of states. We further derive analytical expressions for the moments and the corresponding Lanczos coefficients for quenches between the limits of vanishing and strong quasiperiodic potentials. The Lanczos coefficients display qualitatively distinct behavior depending on the presence of mobility edges - they exhibit an initial plateau followed by a decay with the Krylov basis index, in contrast to the nearly constant behavior of the conventional AAH model without mobility edges. For LR hopping, the coefficients decay with the Krylov basis index for quenches from the localized to the extended phase, while they coincide with the AAH results for quenches in the opposite direction.

</details>


### [141] [Adaptive operator-generated subspaces for effective many-body Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00560)
*Ginanjar Utama,Hermawan Kresno Dipojono*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应Clifford代数子空间本征求解器（A-CASE），一种由算子生成、基于单参考的Rayleigh-Ritz方法。它从共享的Pauli期望值库重建重叠、哈密顿量、可观测量和响应矩阵，并通过自适应增长评分来避免对称性泄漏或近线性相关。在H4/STO-3G/CAS(4e,4o)基准上达到与确定性FCI一致至3.1×10^-15 Ha的精度；论文全面对比了A-CASE与ADAPT-VQE、ADAPT-GCIM在固定预算、迭代匹配等条件下的误差表现与测量代价，并引入了分组bootstrap传播有限射击统计波动的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子结构与嵌入工作流最终归结为有效的多体哈密顿量，但量子本征求解研究往往从手工构建的qubit模型出发，两者之间存在衔接鸿沟。作者希望建立一条从交换（AB-initio）哈密顿量直接到能量、关联和响应的可执行路径，同时提供一个能够自适应增长子空间、抑制对称性泄漏并保持数值稳定性的算子生成方法。

Method: A-CASE采用单参考、算子生成的Rayleigh-Ritz框架：所有矩阵（重叠、哈密顿量、可观测量、响应）均从共享的Pauli期望值库重建；自适应增长评分基于重叠感知的局部矩阵束（pencil），并检测和拒绝对称性泄漏或近似线性相关向量；通过严格的FCIDUMP适配器定义活性空间边界。论文在H4线性链STO-3G基组CAS(4e,4o)上系统比较了A-CASE与ADAPT-VQE（固定参考与两算子参考）、ADAPT-GCIM在固定矢量预算、匹配契约（state preparation次数与Pauli词测量量）条件下的误差，并采用分组bootstrap将有限射击波动传播至阈值化、对角化、根匹配、谱权重、磁化率和展宽的各个环节。

Result: 在H4/STO-3G/CAS(4e,4o)中，A-CASE映射扇区与独立行列式FCI一致到3.1×10^-15 Ha。九矢量预算下固定参考A-CASE误差为3.019 mHa，改用两算子ADAPT-VQE态作参考后降至0.342 mHa（但不暗示总代价优势）。匹配契约下，固定参考路线仅需1次态制备而ADAPT-VQE需90次，但前者测量的Pauli词约多一个数量级。固定角度ADAPT-GCIM在最近尺寸匹配和迭代匹配处分别给出13.364 mHa与10.674 mHa误差。在更广泛的基准阶梯上，固定Krylov基通常更准确且更窄但条件数显著更差。bootstrap给出的误差带明确是启发性、条件性的，而非有限样本置信证书。

Conclusion: A-CASE建立了从互换哈密顿量到能量、关联和响应的可执行计算路径，验证了算子生成子空间方法的可行性及其与自适应变分方法之间的精度-代价权衡。作者明确声明该方法不以材料精度、有利标度或量子优势为宣称目标，其不确定性量化是启发式的而非严格的统计置信区间。

Abstract: Electronic-structure and embedding workflows terminate in effective many-body Hamiltonians, whereas quantum eigensolver studies often start from hand-built qubit models. We present the Adaptive Clifford-Algebra Subspace Eigensolver (A-CASE), a single-reference, operator-generated Rayleigh--Ritz method. Overlap, Hamiltonian, observable, and response matrices are reconstructed from one shared Pauli-expectation bank, while adaptive growth scores overlap-aware local pencils and rejects symmetry leakage or near-linear dependence. A strict FCIDUMP adapter supplies the active-space boundary. For linear H$_4$ in STO-3G with CAS(4e,4o), the mapped sector agrees with independent determinant FCI to $3.1\times10^{-15}$ Ha. At a nine-vector budget A-CASE has a $3.019$ mHa error; replacing the determinant reference by a two-operator ADAPT-VQE state reduces it to $0.342$ mHa, without implying a matched total-cost advantage. Under a matched contract, the fixed-reference route uses one state preparation versus ADAPT-VQE's ninety but measures roughly an order of magnitude more Pauli words. Exact fixed-angle ADAPT-GCIM gives $13.364$ mHa at the nearest size match and $10.674$ mHa at the iteration match, with its transition-pair burden reported separately. Across a broader benchmark ladder, fixed Krylov bases are generally more accurate and often narrower but substantially less well conditioned. A grouped bootstrap propagates finite-shot variability through thresholding, diagonalization, root matching, spectral weights, susceptibility, and broadening; its bands are explicitly heuristic and conditional, not finite-sample confidence certificates. The work establishes an executable path from an interchange Hamiltonian to energies, correlations, and response, without claiming materials accuracy, favorable scaling, or quantum advantage.

</details>


### [142] [Thermalization of open quantum systems with pseudomodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02517)
*R. Kevin Kessing,Thibaut Lacroix,Susana F. Huelga,Martin B. Plenio*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了伪模（pseudomode）方法中系统热化与细致平衡条件的关系，分析了在何种参数条件下伪模模型满足细致平衡并热化到系统Gibbs态，提出了"弱细致平衡"的概念及其频率范围限制，并研究了Hermitian与非Hermitian伪模组合以构建平坦有效温度分布的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伪模方法可以精确描述量子系统与浴的任意强耦合，但一般情形下系统不会热化到Gibbs态。即使在渐近弱耦合下，这一现象也与常见热力学直觉冲突。因此需要明确何时伪模模型满足细致平衡并热化到Gibbs态，以指导需要热力学一致性伪模模型的构建。

Method: 通过分析伪模模型中细致平衡（detailed balance）成立的条件，考察不同参数选择如何实现或破坏细致平衡；引入"弱细致平衡"概念，即仅在与系统相关的有限频率范围内成立的细致平衡；并研究了Hermitian与非Hermitian伪模组合以实现平坦有效温度分布的策略。

Result: 建立了伪模模型满足细致平衡和热化到系统Gibbs态的具体判据；证明了特定参数选择只能在有限频率范围内实现"弱细致平衡"；展示了Hermitian与非Hermitian伪模的组合可以产生平坦的有效温度分布。

Conclusion: 研究建立的准则和判据为构建需要热力学一致性的伪模模型提供了重要指导，特别是在系统与浴相互作用非微扰或需要精确描述动力学与热力学性质的场景中。

Abstract: Pseudomode approaches allow for an exact and unapproximated description of a quantum system interacting arbitrarily strongly with a bath. In general, a system coupled to pseudomodes will not thermalize to the system's Gibbs state: This is to be expected when the system-bath coupling is non-perturbative, but conflicts with common thermodynamic intuition when the system-bath coupling is asymptotically weak. We explore under which circumstances pseudomode models satisfy detailed balance (and subsequently thermalize to the system Gibbs state) and how specific choices of parameters can force "weak" detailed balance that is restricted to a limited frequency range. A combination of Hermitian and non-Hermitian pseudomodes that yields a flat effective-temperature profile is also considered. The results and criteria established here are relevant for the construction of pseudomode models in contexts where thermodynamic consistency is required.

</details>


### [143] [Breakdown of the optical saturation regime in molecular single-photon emitters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00596)
*Hugo Levy-Falk,Daniele De Bernardis,Elena Fanella,Louise Morlaës,Costanza Toninelli*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文报道了蒽纳米晶体中单个二苯并三联苯（DBT）分子在强连续波驱动下出现的"反常饱和"现象：荧光在高共振泵浦功率下被强烈抑制，线宽超出二能级系统预测而展宽。通过排除激光加热和系间窜越，作者提出强度依赖的激发态吸收（ESA）至短寿命暗态的模型，可定量复现荧光抑制和线宽展宽。研究还表明基质质量可缓解淬灭，脉冲激发方案有利于实现完全粒子数反转，对量子纳米光子学和分子光力学有直接意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固态单有机分子（如DBT）被公认为确定性单光子源，通常被建模为有效二能级系统（TLS）。然而在强连续波驱动下，DBT分子在蒽纳米晶体中偏离了二能级系统的预期行为，出现反常饱和现象，需要深入理解其物理机制。

Method: 采用相干与不相干激发对比实验，独立标定温度（通过声子诱导退相），排除激光加热和系间窜越因素；提出强度依赖的激发态吸收（ESA）至短寿命暗态的模型，定量拟合荧光抑制与线宽展宽数据；并考察基质质量的影响及脉冲激发方案。

Result: 所提出的ESA模型可定量复现实验观察到的荧光抑制和线宽展宽；基质质量对淬灭有缓解作用；脉冲激发方案被证明是达成完全粒子数反转的策略性选择。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了强驱动下DBT分子偏离二能级描述的本质机制（激发态吸收至暗态），为量子纳米光子学和分子光力学应用中优化单光子源性能提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Solid-state single organic molecules, such as dibenzoterrylene (DBT) in organic matrices, are prominent deterministic single-photon sources, usually modeled as effective two-level systems (TLS). We show that single DBT molecules in anthracene nanocrystals, under strong continuous-wave driving, depart from this picture: instead of the expected saturation, fluorescence is strongly suppressed at high resonant pump power, while the linewidth broadens beyond the TLS prediction -- an \emph{anomalous saturation} regime. Comparing coherent and incoherent excitation and independently calibrating temperature via phonon-induced dephasing rules out laser-induced heating and intersystem crossing. Instead, a model of intensity-dependent excited-state absorption (ESA) toward a short-lived dark state quantitatively reproduces both the fluorescence suppression and the linewidth broadening. We further show that matrix quality mitigates this quenching, and that pulsed excitation schemes are strategic toward full population inversion, with direct implications for quantum nanophotonics and molecular optomechanics.

</details>


### [144] [Coordinate space representation for quantum simulation of scalar field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00670)
*Gaetan Bardy,Matthieu Saubanere,Adrian Tanasa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了基于坐标空间谐振子基的φ^4模型量子模拟公式，展示了哈密顿量具有有效的带状对角结构，允许受控截断，并通过二元和一元玻色子-量子比特映射估算了量子计算资源需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子场论的量子模拟中，计算成本既取决于量子算法也取决于哈密顿量的表示方式。探索坐标空间谐振子基表示φ^4模型，以期望通过有效局域性降低量子模拟所需资源。

Method: 在坐标空间中推导格点φ^4哈密顿量的谐振子基表示，分析单体矩阵和相互作用张量的带状结构；通过数值对角化验证低能谱与标准动量空间表示的一致性；采用二元（binary）和一元（unary）玻色子-量子比特映射估算量子计算资源。

Result: 坐标空间表示中的单体和两体项均表现出有效的带状对角结构，允许在保持低能谱的情况下进行受控截断；该表示在广泛的参数范围内比标准表示减少量子模拟所需资源。

Conclusion: 利用有效局域性，坐标空间谐振子基表示可以显著降低φ^4模型量子模拟的资源需求，是一种有前景的替代方案。

Abstract: Quantum computing provides a promising framework for the simulation of quantum field theories, where the computational cost depends both on the quantum algorithm employed and on the representation of the Hamiltonian. We investigate a formulation of the $φ^4$ model based on the harmonic-oscillator basis in coordinate space. We derive the lattice $φ^4$ Hamiltonian in this representation and analyze the structure of the resulting one-body matrix and interaction tensor. We show that both exhibit an effective band-diagonal structure, allowing controlled truncations of the Hamiltonian while preserving the low-energy spectrum. We validate this formulation by comparing low-energy observables obtained from numerical diagonalization with those computed in the standard harmonic-oscillator momentum-space representation. Finally, we estimate the resources required to encode the Hamiltonian on a quantum computer using both binary and unary boson-to-qubit mappings. By exploiting effective locality, the coordinate-space representation reduces the resources required for quantum simulation over a broad range of parameters.

</details>


### [145] [Finite-Syndrome Compression of Quantum Fisher Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02333)
*Jianqi Sheng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子传感中有限可读误差记录（syndrome）的压缩问题，证明了精细与粗粒化SLD量子Fisher信息损失之间的精确恒等式，将最优有限综合征设计等价于算子值聚类问题，并给出了零损失的条件、量子码精确恢复的最小可读综合征数（Knill-Laflamme不相容图的色数）以及若干具体噪声模型的最优闭合解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可读的误差记录能保护量子传感，避免物理上不同的噪声轨迹被不可逆地混合。然而，有限探测器或辅助系统只能保留有限多个综合征值，缺乏一个通用准则来判断哪些记录可以合并而不损失计量信息。此前没有系统的理论框架来处理这一有限分辨率下的信息压缩问题。

Method: 本文将问题形式化为固定细粒度经典-量子记录在参数无关压缩至最多M个标志下的优化问题。核心方法是证明一个精确恒等式：损失的对数对称导数量子Fisher信息（SLD QFI）等于状态加权的细/粗SLD得分之间平方距离之和。由此将最优有限综合征设计转为算子值聚类问题，并导出零损失的支持分解公共SLD条件。此外，利用Knill-Laflamme不相容图的色数刻画量子码精确恢复的最小可读综合征数，并针对比特随机酉噪声和平面随机Pauli模型推导出有限划分公式和精确最优值。

Result: 得到了SLD QFI损失的精确恒等式，建立了零损失的支持分解公共SLD条件；将最优有限综合征设计等价为算子值聚类问题；对量子码精确恢复给出了图论刻画（色数）；对比特随机酉噪声得到有限划分公式；对平面随机Pauli模型在均匀误差轴假设下得到精确最优值F_M*=[M sin(π/M)/π]^2，亏损为π^2/(3M^2)+O(M^{-4})。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了有限综合征分辨率的信息论代价，明确了被动噪声-擦除解释所需的侧信息假设，为量子传感中的有限探测器设计提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Readable error records can protect quantum sensing because they prevent physically distinct noise trajectories from being irreversibly mixed. A finite detector or ancilla, however, can retain only finitely many syndrome values, and a general criterion for deciding which records may be merged without losing metrological information is absent. We formulate the problem for a fixed fine-grained classical-quantum record and parameter-independent compression into at most $M$ flags. We prove an exact identity expressing the lost symmetric-logarithmic-derivative quantum Fisher information (SLD QFI) as a sum of state-weighted squared distances between fine and coarse SLD scores. Consequently, optimal finite-syndrome design is exactly an operator-valued clustering problem, and zero loss is characterized by a support-resolved common-SLD condition. We extend the identity to the full multiparameter SLD QFI matrix and distinguish local QFI preservation from recovery of an entire statistical model. For exact recovery of a quantum code, we separately show that, when each fine error is individually correctable, the minimum number of readable syndromes is the chromatic number of a Knill-Laflamme incompatibility graph. For qubit random-unitary noise we obtain a finite partition formula. A planar random-Pauli model admits a conditional-variance representation and, for a uniform error axis, the exact optimum $F_M^{\star}=[M\sin(π/M)/π]^2$, with deficit $π^2/(3M^2)+O(M^{-4})$. These results identify the information-theoretic cost of finite syndrome resolution while making explicit the side-information assumptions required for any passive noise-to-erasure interpretation.

</details>


### [146] [Non-Hermitian dynamics in a driven-dissipative and spatially correlated light-matter system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00706)
*Ruiyang Huang,Eliott Beraud,Antoine Glicenstein,Simon Bernon*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个统一的非厄米理论框架来研究驱动-耗散量子系统的动力学，并通过对^87Rb玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体在近共振光物质相互作用下的物质波衍射实验数据进行了验证，揭示了相干驱动与耗散之间的相互作用，并观察到随驱动增强而衰减率降低的反常现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯量子态是理想化的表征，在真实系统中不可避免地会受到环境耗散连接的影响而退化。现有微扰方法在近共振光物质相互作用区域失效，因此需要建立一套统一的、能够在非微扰区域精确描述驱动-耗散量子系统动力学的理论框架。

Method: 从驱动-耗散系统的一般主方程描述出发，推导出一个依赖于控制参数（如光物质相互作用的振幅Ω和失谐Δ）的有效复势V_eff(Ω,Δ)。实验上，利用周期性的激发态工程驱动近共振的^87Rb玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体，将空间调制的V_eff映射到动量空间进行精确量化。理论结果与主方程的数值模拟及非厄米哈密顿量H_eff=p^2/2m+V_eff的模态分析相结合进行解释。

Result: 实验结果与主方程的数值模拟一致，展示了相干驱动与耗散之间的相互作用，并发现一个显著特征：随着驱动增强，衰减率反而降低。该动力学通过非厄米哈密顿量的模态分析得到了直观且定性的解释。

Conclusion: 该工作建立的统一理论框架不仅能够实现精确的数值模拟，还通过模态分析提供了直观的物理解释，为非厄米量子动力学和驱动-耗散系统的研究提供了新的理论工具与实验验证。

Abstract: Pure quantum states, as described in quantum mechanics textbooks, are ideal representations inevitably deteriorated in real systems by any dissipative connection to the environment. In this work we derive a unified theoretical frame to study the non-Hermitian dynamics of a driven-dissipative quantum system and validate it by comparison to experimental data of matter-wave diffraction. To highlight the role of dissipation, we perform the comparison in a near-resonant regime of light-matter interaction, where perturbative approaches fail. Here, we show that the developed formalism enables both exact simulations and an intuitive interpretation based on modal analysis. The theoretical analysis is based on the general master equation description of driven-dissipative systems, from which we derive an effective complex potential $V_\mathrm{eff}(Ω,Δ)$ that depends on control parameters such as the amplitude and detuning of the light matter interaction. Experimentally, we drive a $^{87}$Rb BEC near resonance using periodic excited-state engineering, mapping the spatially modulated $V_{\rm eff}[Ω,Δ(x)]$ into momentum space for precise quantification. The experimental results agree with numerical simulations of the master equation and demonstrate the interplay between coherent drive and dissipation, with an exceptional signature: a reduced decay rate with increasing drive. Such dynamics is then interpreted by a modal analysis of the non-Hermitian Hamiltonian $H_\mathrm{eff}=p^2/2m+V_\mathrm{eff}$ which provide an intuitive and qualitative explanation for such driven-dissipative system.

</details>


### [147] [Quantum Speedups for Testing Similar Means](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00741)
*Chengshen Gao,Yongzhen Xu,Shenggen Zheng,Lvzhou Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子算法在分布性质检验中的应用，具体关注m个分布（m≥3）是否具有相似均值的问题。在查询模型和采样模型下分别设计出复杂度为Õ(1/ε)和Õ(√m/ε)的量子算法，相比经典算法实现二次加速；并建立了相应模型下的量子下界，证明算法在ε依赖方面（对数因子内）已达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布性质检验是信息论、学习理论和统计学中的核心问题。虽然已知量子算法对单个分布或一对分布的性质检验有显著加速，但尚不清楚量子算法是否能对多个分布（m≥3）的性质检验提供加速。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在查询模型中，算法可以自主选择对哪个分布进行采样；在采样模型中，分布被均匀随机选择。作者针对两种模型分别设计了量子算法：查询模型复杂度为Õ(1/ε)，采样模型复杂度为Õ(√m/ε)。同时建立了量子下界：查询模型为Ω(1/ε)，采样模型为Ω(m^{1/3}+m^{1/4}/ε)。

Result: 查询模型下量子算法复杂度为Õ(1/ε)，采样模型下为Õ(√m/ε)，均相比经典算法实现二次加速。量子下界分别为Ω(1/ε)和Ω(m^{1/3}+m^{1/4}/ε)，证明算法在ε的依赖关系上（对数因子内）是最优的。

Conclusion: 量子算法在多个分布的性质检验中同样能够提供二次加速，所设计的算法在ε依赖方面已达到最优。该工作为量子算法在多元分布性质检验领域提供了重要的理论保证。

Abstract: Property testing of distributions is a central topic in information theory, learning theory, and statistics. While quantum algorithms are known to offer significant speedups for property testing of a single distribution or a pair of distributions, it is unclear whether quantum algorithms provide speedups for property testing of $m$ ($m\geq 3$) distributions. This work focuses on quantum algorithms for testing whether $m$ distributions have similar means or are $ε$-far from mean similarity under two models. In the query model, the algorithm can choose which distribution to sample from, whereas in the sampling model, the distributions are selected uniformly. We design quantum algorithms with complexities $\tilde{O}(1/ε)$ (the $\tilde{O}$ notation hides poly-logarithmic factors) and $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{m}/ε)$ in the query and sampling models, respectively, achieving quadratic speedups over the classical counterparts. We further establish quantum lower bounds of $Ω\left(1/ε\right)$ and $Ω\rbra{m^{1/3}+\frac{m^{1/4}}ε}$ for the query model and the sampling model, demonstrating the optimality of our quantum algorithms in terms of the dependence on $ε$ up to logarithmic factors.

</details>


### [148] [From minimal informationally complete measurements to orthocentric simplices and back again](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00809)
*Piotr Bereza,Wojciech Słomczyński,Anna Szymusiak*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了最小s-紧信息完备测量（MICs）与正交心单纯形（orthocentric simplices）之间的数学对应关系，证明了三者等价：s-紧MIC、其测量向量经适当重标度后形成以原点为垂心的锐角正交心单纯形、以及这类单纯形恰好是位似自对偶的。此外，论文完整刻画了s-紧类的测量方向空间，其模空间为Δ°_{d+1}×{±1}，并证明每个以原点为垂心的锐角正交心单纯形都可在态空间中锚定，生成一类包含恰好一个紧IC测量（模整体重标度）的最小s-紧IC测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子断层成像中，最小编码完备测量（MICs）的统计性质已被充分研究，但其几何结构一直未被揭示。作者希望建立量子信息论中"紧性"（tightness）概念与经典欧几里得几何之间的桥梁，从而更深入地理解这些测量的内在几何本质。

Method: 论文通过建立s-紧MIC测量向量与正交心单纯形顶点之间的对应关系展开分析。核心方法是证明一个三向等价定理：s-紧MIC当且仅当其测量向量经重标度后构成以原点为垂心的锐角正交心单纯形，且这类单纯形恰好是位似自对偶的。进一步，作者通过方向配置空间的刻画，利用交叉比条件约束测量方向之间的夹角（须为钝角），并证明方向模空间由"骨架"（一个概率向量）和方向类别共同决定，即Δ°_{d+1}×{±1}。

Result: 论文完成了s-紧MIC的完整几何表征：其测量方向之间的夹角必须为钝角且满足交叉比条件；方向配置的模空间为Δ°_{d+1}×{±1}，即由一个概率向量（骨架）和一个方向类别（±1）共同参数化。反方向上，证明了每个以原点为垂心的锐角正交心单纯形都可在量子态空间中锚定，生成一类最小s-紧IC测量，其中恰好包含一个（模整体重标度）紧IC测量。

Conclusion: 本文将量子测量理论中的"紧性"概念与经典欧几里得几何中的正交心单纯形理论建立起了深刻的联系，证明s-紧MIC类完全由其测量方向刻画，并为该类的模空间提供了简洁的参数化描述。这一几何对称性为量子断层成像与欧氏几何之间架起了桥梁，也为紧IC测量和形态测量（morphophoric measurements）提供了统一的几何视角。

Abstract: The reconstruction of unknown quantum states via minimal informationally complete measurements (MICs) is a cornerstone of quantum tomography. Although the statistical properties of these measurements are well-understood, their geometric structure has remained elusive. In this work, we establish a correspondence between the class of minimal $s$-tight informationally complete measurements, encompassing, among others, tight IC and morphophoric measurements, and the classical geometry of orthocentric simplices. In particular, we prove a three-way equivalence: a MIC is $s$-tight if and only if its measurement vectors, upon suitable rescaling, form the vertices of an acute orthocentric simplex with the orthocentre at the origin, and such simplices are precisely the homothetically self-dual ones. This geometric manifestation of operational ''tightness'' provides a bridge between the physical world and Euclidean geometry. Furthermore, the $s$-tight class is fully characterised by its measurement directions: the angles between them must be obtuse and satisfy a cross-ratio condition. We determine the space of admissible direction configurations: modulo rotations, every such configuration is encoded by a single probability vector, the ''skeleton'' of the measurement, together with an orientation class, so that the moduli space of $s$-tight MIC directions is $Δ^{\circ}_{d+1}\times\{\pm 1\}$. Conversely, every acute orthocentric simplex with the orthocentre at the origin can be anchored in the state space, generating a class of minimal $s$-tight IC measurements that contains exactly one tight IC measurement up to overall rescaling.

</details>


### [149] [Measurement-Based Loss Tolerance in Graph-GKP Codes through Syndrome-Resolved Pauli-Frame Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00830)
*Seid Koudia,Symeon Chatzinotas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的因果框架，将图码的多种物理表示与GKP码的模拟呼吸位移信息结合起来，用于纯损耗加量子极限放大的测量基损耗容忍。通过局部GKP恢复生成刷新逻辑块、连续伴随记录和置信度评分，低置信度结果被转换为定位删除，实现从玻色子数据直接生成可用性模式，并推导出解码器条件的支路限制、带符号结果重建、泡利帧更新、递归级联和伴随分辨的逻辑融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图码提供逻辑可观测量的多种物理表示，而GKP码保留玻色子位移噪声的模拟信息，两者在测量基损耗容忍下尚未有统一的理论框架来协同利用。

Method: 建立因果框架统一图码与GKP码机制：每个局部GKP恢复生成刷新逻辑块、连续伴随记录和置信度评分；低置信度结果转换为定位于擦除；接受和拒绝的伴随均贡献于图分支上的伴随分辨后验，决定可访问的逻辑表示与输出的逻辑泡利帧；推导了解码器条件支路限制、带符号结果重建、泡利帧更新、图-GKP模块递归级联及伴随分辨逻辑融合。

Result: 在多个压缩水平下进行数值模拟，识别出任务依赖的损耗容忍行为和方晶格及六角GKP晶格的有限深度伪阈值。

Conclusion: 图-GKP接口为容错MBQC、融合基计算和全光子中继器提供了统一的因果控制层。

Abstract: Graph codes offer multiple physical representatives of logical observables, while Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill (GKP) codes retain analog information about bosonic displacement noise. We develop a causal framework that unifies these mechanisms for measurement-based loss tolerance under pure loss followed by quantum-limited amplification. In this framework, each local GKP recovery produces a refreshed logical block, a continuous syndrome record, and a confidence score for the inferred Pauli class. Low-confidence outcomes are deliberately converted into located erasures, so the availability pattern is generated directly from the bosonic data rather than sampled independently. Both accepted and rejected syndromes contribute to a syndrome-resolved posterior over the graph branch, which determines accessible logical representatives and the outgoing logical Pauli frame. We derive decoder-conditioned branch restriction, signed-outcome reconstruction, Pauli-frame updating, recursive concatenation of graph-GKP modules, and syndrome-resolved logical fusion. Numerical simulations across several squeezing levels identify task-dependent loss-tolerance behavior and finite-depth pseudothresholds for square and hexagonal GKP lattices. The resulting graph-GKP interface provides a unified causal control layer for fault-tolerant MBQC, fusion-based computation, and all-photonic repeaters.

</details>


### [150] [Estimating spacetime fluctuation strength in SU(1,1) and SU(2) interferometers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00907)
*B. Sharmila*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文评估了SU(1,1)干涉仪相对于传统SU(2)干涉仪在估计时空随机涨落强度和关联长度方面的优势，通过计算量子Fisher信息和经典Fisher信息进行系统比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精度激光干涉仪常用于探测随机时空涨落以理解引力的基本性质，传统上使用SU(2)干涉仪。作者受Zheng等人工作启发，探究SU(1,1)干涉仪是否比SU(2)干涉仪在此类实验中具有性能优势。

Method: 分别计算SU(1,1)和SU(2)两类干涉仪中，用于估计时空涨落强度和关联长度的量子Fisher信息，并针对不同实验相关测量方案计算相应的经典Fisher信息，比较不同参数区间的信息度量。

Result: 系统比较了两种干涉仪在不同参数区间的Fisher信息表现，评估了SU(1,1)干涉仪在估计涨落强度或关联长度时是否以及何时优于SU(2)干涉仪，涵盖当前和未来可能的实验配置。

Conclusion: 研究结果可用于指导在哪些条件下选择SU(1,1)干涉仪替代传统SU(2)干涉仪以获得更好的时空涨落参数估计性能。

Abstract: High-precision laser interferometers are commonly used to search for signatures of random spacetime fluctuations, in an attempt to understand the fundamental nature of gravity. Conventionally, $SU(2)$ interferometers have been used in such experimental investigations. Motivated by [K. Zheng \textit{et al.}, Photon. Res. \textbf{8}, 1653 (2020)], I assess if there is any advantage to be gained by using an $SU(1,1)$ interferometer instead of the $SU(2)$ interferometer. To this end, I compute the quantum Fisher information for estimating the strength and the correlation length of the spacetime fluctuations, and the corresponding classical Fisher information considering different experimentally relevant measurement schemes, in both types of interferometers. I compare the information metrics corresponding to different parameter regimes that are relevant to both current and possible future experimental setups. This helps me assess if and when the $SU(1,1)$ interferometer offers any advantage over the $SU(2)$ interferometer for estimating either the fluctuation strength or correlation length of the spacetime fluctuations.

</details>


### [151] [Parallel Repetition in the Two-Player Quantum Cloning Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00930)
*Eli Coe Naig,Stephen A. Fenner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究双玩家量子克隆游戏中的并行重复问题，该游戏是由量子位置验证启发的单配性纠缠游戏。作者证明了Colisson等人对n个副本的值界（(3/4)^n到cos^{2n}(π/8)）对两个副本的情况均非紧致，给出了显式的挑战相关策略达到(5+√17)/16>9/16，证明了无限制游戏下强并行重复失败；同时通过块Gram矩阵论证给出上界(11+√65)/32<cos^4(π/8)，并严格改进所有n的先前并行重复上界。对于每个n，挑战无关策略的最优值为(3/4)^n。
