<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子位置验证是一种重要的量子密码学原语，而量子克隆游戏作为其背后的单配性纠缠游戏，理解其并行重复行为对于评估量子位置验证协议的安全性至关重要。此前Colisson等人给出的上下界对于n个副本存在间隙，需要确定这两个界是否紧致，以深化对游戏值的理解。

Method: 作者采用了两种互补的证明技术：(1) 通过构造显式的挑战相关（challenge-dependent）策略来建立下界，证明(3/4)^n下界不紧致；(2) 利用块Gram矩阵（block Gram matrix）论证来推导上界，证明cos^{2n}(π/8)上界也不紧致，并将该技术推广到任意n以严格改进先前的并行重复上界。对于挑战无关策略，通过完整的分析确定了其精确最优值。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 对两个副本，显式策略达到(5+√17)/16≈0.695>0.5625，证明强并行重复失败；(2) 上界(11+√65)/32≈0.596<0.729（cos^4(π/8)），两界均非紧致；(3) 块Gram矩阵方法对每个n严格改进了先前上界；(4) 挑战无关策略的最优值精确为(3/4)^n。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了并行重复量子克隆游戏中的关键开放性间隙问题，证明了先前两个界对两副本情况均非最优，并展示了强并行重复在无限制量子克隆游戏中的失败。块Gram矩阵技术提供了对所有n的改进上界，而挑战无关策略的精确刻画进一步揭示了游戏中挑战相关性和并行重复行为之间的微妙关系，为量子位置验证的安全性分析提供了更精细的工具。

Abstract: We study parallel repetition in the two-player quantum cloning game, a monogamy-of-entanglement game motivated by quantum position verification. Colisson Palais, Escolà-Farràs, and Speelman bounded the value of $n$ copies between $(3/4)^n$ and $\cos^{2n}(π/8)$. For two copies, we prove that neither bound is tight. An explicit challenge-dependent strategy achieves value $(5+\sqrt{17})/16>9/16$, so strong parallel repetition fails for the unrestricted game. A block Gram matrix argument gives the upper bound $(11+\sqrt{65})/32<\cos^4(π/8)$ and strictly improves the previous parallel-repetition upper bound for every $n$. For every $n$, challenge-independent strategies have optimal value $(3/4)^n$.

</details>


### [152] [Revisiting Thermal Scalability for Large-Scale Superconducting Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00990)
*Shaswot Shresthamali,Ilkwon Byun,Teruo Tanimoto,Yoshinori Uzawa,Kunihiro Inomata,Tsuyoshi Yamamoto,Koji Inoue*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析了超导量子系统中读出放大链带来的热可扩展性瓶颈，通过完整建模三种产热机制（放大器耗散、偏置线路被动传导和焦耳热），证明放大器相关热量是现代大型低温量子系统的主导热瓶颈，并评估了各种技术方案及面向1万量子比特单冰箱系统的演进路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统级热扩展性模型对读出放大链的产热机制覆盖不全（仅部分或不包含放大器耗散、被动传导和焦耳热），导致瓶颈误判和可扩展性高估，需要更完整的热估算模型来准确识别热瓶颈。

Method: 改进先前的系统级热估算模型，完整纳入现代低温量子系统中读出放大器模块的主要热源，包括有源耗散、被动传导和焦耳热；同时探索多种技术选项及其权衡，评估更大制冷平台和光学方案等前瞻性系统配置。

Result: 分析表明放大器相关热量成为主导热瓶颈，从根本上改变了现代大规模低温系统的热景观；通过技术选项比较找到了降低关键热负载并提升可扩展性的配置；评估了面向单冰箱1万量子比特低温系统的前瞻性路径。

Conclusion: 读出放大器模块的热负载是大规模超导量子系统热扩展性的关键制约因素，需要结合多种技术方案和更大制冷平台来克服这一瓶颈，以实现单冰箱万级量子比特的目标。

Abstract: The readout amplification chain imposes a critical thermal scalability bottleneck in large-scale superconducting quantum systems. This happens through three mechanisms: amplifier dissipation, passive conduction through bias wiring and Joule heating within that same wiring. These terms are absent or only partially represented in several prior system-level thermal-scalability models, leading to bottleneck misidentification and scalability overestimation. In this work, we improve upon previous system-level heat estimation models by fully accounting for the major heat sources in modern cryogenic quantum systems including the active dissipation, passive conduction, and Joule heating in the readout amplifier module. Our analysis demonstrates that amplifier-associated heat emerges as the dominant thermal bottleneck that fundamentally alters the thermal landscape of modern large-scale cryogenic systems. We explore various technology options and their tradeoffs to identify configurations that reduce this critical heat load and improve scalability. Finally, we evaluate forward-looking system configurations, including larger refrigeration platforms and optical approaches, and analyze forward-looking pathways toward single-fridge 10k-qubit cryogenic systems.

</details>


### [153] [Complete Existence Classification of Seven-Partite Absolutely Maximally Entangled States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01011)
*Fei Shi,Xiande Zhang,Qi Zhao,Lvzhou Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了七量子比特绝对最大纠缠态（AME(7,d)）存在的充分必要条件为局域维度 d≥3。通过构造循环二次相位态覆盖所有奇数维度，并发展二元-奇数维耦合构造覆盖所有模4余2的维度，结合已知的2的幂次情况与乘积性质，完成了对所有 d≥3 的完整证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前AME(7,d)态的存在性仅在d为素数幂（除2外）或可由已知存在维度乘积表示时得到证明，而AME(7,2)已被证明不存在，因此需要建立所有d≥3时的存在性，填补这一空白。

Method: 作者提出两种新的构造方法：(1) 为每个奇数局域维度构造循环二次相位态；(2) 为每个模4余2的维度发展耦合的二元-奇数维构造。结合已知的2的幂次情形以及AME态的乘积性质，覆盖了所有d≥3的维度。

Result: 成功证明AME(7,d)态存在当且仅当d≥3，使该问题得到完整解决。

Conclusion: AME(7,d)态的完整刻画已完成：局部维度d=2时不存在，所有d≥3时均存在。

Abstract: We prove that an absolutely maximally entangled state of seven qudits exists if and only if the local dimension satisfies $d\geq 3$. Prior to this work, to the best of our knowledge, $\text{AME}(7,d)$ states were known to exist only when $d$ is a prime power other than $2$, or when $d$ can be expressed as a product of dimensions for which existence was already known. Since it has been proved that no $\text{AME}(7,2)$ state exists, it remains to establish existence for all $d\geq 3$. We construct cyclic quadratic-phase states for every odd local dimension and develop a coupled binary--odd-dimensional construction for every dimension congruent to $2$ modulo $4$. Together with the known power-of-two cases and the product property of AME states, these constructions cover every local dimension $d\geq 3$.

</details>


### [154] [Twenty-one characterizations of reversible quantum channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01088)
*Nan Li,Yuan Li,Hanyu Yang,Shunlong Luo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了有限维希尔伯特空间上的可逆量子信道（特别关注输入与输出维度不同的情况），给出了二十一种等价的代数、几何和信息论刻画，并首次推导出Choi态的谱分解、直和与张量积三种等价表示形式，为量子纠错、量子隐形传态和量子热力学等应用提供了统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可逆量子信道在量子动力学和量子信息处理中扮演基础性角色，但由于其重要性和广泛应用，从多角度对可逆量子信道进行刻画是非常有价值的。已有刻画分散在不同文献或语境中，缺乏统一的系统性框架，特别是当输入与输出维度不同时，相关研究尚不充分。

Method: 本文在有限维希尔伯特空间上研究可逆量子信道，重点处理输入与输出维度不同的情形。作者从代数、几何和信息论三个视角系统地整理并证明了二十一种等价刻画，包括已有结果和文献中隐含或在不同语境中的结果，并新推导了Choi态表征，证明了可逆量子信道的Choi态具有谱分解、直和与张量积三种等价形式。

Result: 系统性地提出了可逆量子信道的二十一种等价刻画，涵盖了代数、几何和信息理论等不同视角；明确区分了已知结果与本文新澄清或新推导的结果；建立了Choi态表征定理，证明可逆量子信道的Choi态具有谱表示、直和表示和张量积表示三种等价形式。

Conclusion: 这二十一种等价刻画为可逆量子信道建立了全面的理论框架，为量子信道的结构特性和信息论性质提供了多样化的见解，并为量子信息处理中的实际应用（如量子纠错、量子隐形传态和量子热力学）提供了便利的工具和理论基础。

Abstract: Reversible quantum channels play a fundamental role in quantum dynamics and quantum information processing. A quantum channel is reversible if there exists another quantum channel acting as its left inverse. Due to their intrinsic significance and wide applications, it is desirable to characterize reversible quantum channels from diverse perspectives. In this work, we study reversible quantum channels on finite-dimensional Hilbert spaces, with particular emphasis on the case of different input and output dimensions. We systematically present twenty-one equivalent characterizations of reversible quantum channels from algebraic, geometrical, and information-theoretical perspectives. Among these characterizations, some are well known, while others, implicit in the literature or formulated in other contexts, are clarified here; the Choi-state characterization is derived in this work. Specifically, we prove that the Choi states of reversible quantum channels admit three equivalent forms: the spectral, direct-sum, and tensor-product representations. These twenty-one equivalent characterizations establish a comprehensive framework for reversible quantum channels, provide diverse insights into the structural and information-theoretic properties of quantum channels, and facilitate applications of reversibility in quantum information processing such as quantum error correction, quantum teleportation, and quantum thermodynamics.

</details>


### [155] [Fault-tolerant quantum computing with a microwave Cat Bus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01111)
*Yanyan Chen,Xinyang Yu,Yueyang Min,Zhihao Zhang,Shuaifan Cao,Xiaopeng Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种免穿梭（shuttling-free）的中性原子量子计算架构，利用微波腔中自稳的玻色猫态（Cat Bus）实现远程Rydberg原子的共振纠缠，原生支持高度并行的一对多CZ^n门并抑制串扰。该架构相比基于原子重排的方案在10^5数据比特下将症状提取周期时间降低180倍，错误阈值从0.55%提升至0.80%，为高速容错中性原子量子计算提供了新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错中性原子量子计算机的可扩展性受限于光镊穿梭（shuttling）的延迟，导致量子算法编译中量子比特开销与电路深度之间存在严苛的权衡。传统架构中原子搬运操作成为性能瓶颈，亟需一种无需物理移动原子即可实现全连接的硬件高效方案。

Method: 提出Cat Bus架构：微波腔模式自稳为玻色猫态作为量子总线，实现远程Rydberg原子的共振纠缠，原生支持一对多CZ^n门。从底层相互作用和物理约束推导cat-atom错误通道；开发硬件感知调度方案，利用原生CZ^n门构建最小深度的症状提取电路；使用超图积（HGP）码进行基准测试。

Result: 在N=10^5数据比特下，症状提取周期时间相比原子重排架构降低180倍；匹配双量子比特退极化噪声下错误阈值从0.55%提升至0.72%；硬件推导误差模型下阈值达0.80%，对应阈值协同度C_th=7.8×10^4，与Rydberg耦合微波腔系统的实验可访问参数兼容。

Conclusion: Cat Bus通过避免原子输运，为高速容错中性原子量子计算提供了一条可行路径，在保持全连接的同时实现高度并行化操作、指数抑制串扰以及显著提升的错误阈值。

Abstract: The scalability of fault-tolerant neutral-atom quantum computers is constrained by the latency of shuttling with optical tweezers, imposing a stringent trade-off between qubit overhead and circuit depth in quantum algorithm compilation. Here we propose a hardware-efficient, shuttling-free architecture that achieves all-to-all connectivity. Remote Rydberg atoms are resonantly entangled through a microwave ``Cat Bus''---a cavity mode autonomously stabilized in a bosonic cat state. The Cat Bus natively supports the highly parallelized execution of one-to-many $\mathrm{CZ}^n$ gates with exponentially suppressed crosstalk. We derive the resulting cat--atom error channel from the underlying interactions and physical constraints. For fault-tolerant operation, we develop a hardware-aware scheduling scheme that exploits the native cat--atom $\mathrm{CZ}^{n}$ gate to construct a syndrome-extraction circuit with minimum depth. We benchmark the architecture using hypergraph-product (HGP) codes and estimate a 180-fold reduction in syndrome-extraction cycle time at $N=10^5$ data qubits compared with an atom-rearrangement-based architecture. Under matched two-qubit depolarizing noise, the corresponding error threshold increases from $0.55\%$ to $0.72\%$. Under the hardware-derived error model, we obtain a threshold of $0.80\%$, corresponding to a threshold cooperativity of $C_{\mathrm{th}}=7.8 \times 10^4$, compatible with experimentally accessible parameters for Rydberg-coupled microwave-cavity systems. By avoiding atom transport, the Cat Bus provides a route towards high-speed, fault-tolerant neutral-atom quantum computation.

</details>


### [156] [Optimal Strategies for Multi-parameter Quantum Metrology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01114)
*Linxuan Li,Zihao Hu,Longyun Chen,Yuxiang Yang,Haidong Yuan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个通用且高效的计算框架，用于在量子多参数传感中联合优化探针态、控制操作和测量，覆盖平行、顺序及不定因果顺序等多种策略族，并提供精确的半定规划公式来计算Holevo、Nagaoka-Hayashi和量子Cramér-Rao等精度界，同时支持资源约束和有限内存顺序策略的优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多参数量子传感中同时估计多个参数至关重要，但各参数的最优估计策略往往互不兼容，无法同时达到所有参数的极限精度。目前缺乏一个通用框架来系统性地比较不同策略族（平行、顺序、不定因果顺序）的可达到精度，并在此框架下考虑实际资源约束。

Method: 开发了一个联合优化计算框架，同时优化探针态、控制操作和测量。为Holevo界、Nagaoka-Hayashi界和量子Cramér-Rao界提供了精确的半定规划（SDP）公式。框架可处理平行、顺序和不定因果顺序等不同策略族，能直接纳入如能量预算等资源约束，并对顺序策略提出了考虑受限辅助内存维度的有限内存优化方法，将协议分解为初始探针制备和中间控制操作。

Result: 在多参数磁力测量和频率估计中识别了各类策略族内的最优协议，揭示了多参数条件下不同策略族可达到性能之间的严格层级关系。框架有效纳入了能量预算等资源约束，并展示了有限内存优化方法用于设计资源受限的顺序传感方案。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个多功能计算工具，能够确定复杂多参数设置中的基本精度极限，并设计最优量子传感协议，为实验上实际可行的多参数量子传感提供了系统化设计路径。

Abstract: Estimating multiple unknown parameters simultaneously is essential for practical quantum sensing. However, it faces a fundamental challenge: the optimal strategy for estimating one parameter is often incompatible with that for another, making it impossible to simultaneously achieve the ultimate precision limits for all parameters. Here we develop a general and efficient computational framework that jointly optimizes probe states, control operations, and measurements across different strategy families, including parallel, sequential, and those with indefinite causal order. Our approach provides exact semidefinite-program formulations for several precision bounds, including the Holevo, Nagaoka-Hayashi, and quantum Cramér-Rao bounds. We demonstrate the capabilities of the framework in multiparameter magnetometry and frequency estimation, identifying optimal protocols within each class and revealing a strict hierarchy among the achievable performances of different classes in the multiparameter regime. The framework also directly incorporates resource constraints, such as energy budgets, enabling systematic investigation of experimentally realistic sensing scenarios. Furthermore, we develop a finite-memory optimization method for sequential strategies with restricted ancillary-memory dimension. By decomposing the protocol into initial probe preparation and intermediate control operations, this method provides a practical route to designing resource-constrained sequential sensing schemes. Our work establishes a versatile computational tool for determining fundamental precision limits and designing optimal quantum-sensing protocols in complex multiparameter settings.

</details>


### [157] [Maximal Rényi Relative Entropy for $α>2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01124)
*Roberto Rubboli*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文完成了量子Rényi相对熵中最大扩展的完整刻画，证明对于α>2，最大扩展由α-z Rényi相对熵（z=α-1）给出，并将其应用于催化辅助的吉布斯保持操作中相干性生成能力的完整表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子相对熵在量子信息理论中扮演基础性角色。经典Rényi相对熵（除线性组合外）是最一般的相对熵类，因此自然动机是寻找其最小和最大量子扩展。此前最小扩展已被完整刻画，但最大扩展仅在α∈[0,2]范围内被识别为几何Rényi相对熵，α>2的情况仍是开放问题。

Method: 通过严格的数学证明，作者证明了对于α>2，最大量子扩展恰好由α-z Rényi相对熵在z=α-1时的形式给出，从而补全了此前缺失的参数区间，完成了该性质的完整特征化。

Result: 证明了α>2时最大扩展为z=α-1的α-z Rényi相对熵；作为应用，完全刻画了在催化辅助下，吉布斯保持操作能否将能量非相干态转化为能量相干态的条件，从而完整表征了这类操作的相干性生成能力。

Conclusion: 本文补全了量子Rényi相对熵最大扩展的完整描述，架起了量子信息论中Rényi相对熵理论与热力学相干性操作之间的桥梁，为催化辅助热力学转换提供了完整判据。

Abstract: Quantum relative entropies play a fundamental role in quantum information theory. In the classical setting, Rényi relative entropies constitute, up to linear combinations, the most general class of relative entropies, naturally motivating the search for their minimal and maximal quantum extensions. The minimal extension is known to be the reverse sandwiched Rényi relative entropy for $α\in[0,1/2)$ and the sandwiched Rényi relative entropy for $α\geq 1/2$. In contrast, the maximal extension had previously been identified only for $α\in[0,2]$, where it is given by the geometric Rényi relative entropy. In this work, we complete this characterization by proving that for $α>2$, the maximal extension is given by the $α$-$z$ Rényi relative entropy with $z=α-1$. As an application, we determine when an energy-incoherent state can be transformed into an energy-coherent state by a Gibbs-preserving operation assisted by an uncorrelated catalyst, thereby fully characterizing the coherence-generating power of this class of operations in the catalytic setting.

</details>


### [158] [Constant-depth adaptive preparation of Dicke and symmetric states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01144)
*Rui Luo,Junjie Chen,Xiongfeng Ma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种精确的恒定深度自适应制备协议,用于制备任意的Dicke-(n,k)态及更一般的置换对称态。通过引入均匀子集叠加态作为基本构建块,结合经典前馈测量,实现了恒定量子深度下指数级抑制失败概率的Dicke态制备,并进一步通过提升框架将酉Dicke态制备电路推广到任意对称态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dicke态及置换对称态的高效制备对于量子计量学、量子网络和集体量子信息处理至关重要。尽管现有技术已能利用测量和经典前馈实现低深度制备,但如何以更少的辅助量子比特和更高的成功概率实现恒定深度制备仍是一个关键挑战。

Method: 本文采用两阶段策略:(1)首先构造均匀子集叠加态作为原始构件,采用恒定深度自适应电路,使用O(k²log²n)个辅助量子比特,成功概率至少1/k;(2)通过并行重复在不增加量子深度的情况下将失败概率指数级压制。随后建立一个通用的提升框架,将洁净的酉Dicke态制备电路相干地组合起来,以仅多项式辅助量子比特开销制备任意对称态。

Result: 得到了一个精确的、概率性的、恒定深度Dicke态制备协议,使用O(n²+k²log²n+knlognloglogn)个辅助量子比特;结合最近的恒定深度酉Dicke态构造,实现了任意n量子比特对称态的精确恒定深度制备协议,辅助量子比特开销为O(n³√logn)。

Conclusion: 本文证明了利用测量和经典前馈可以有效降低对称态制备的量子深度至恒定水平,同时保持精确性,且辅助量子比特开销仅为多项式规模。均匀子集叠加态本身也具有独立的研究价值(如Johnson图的均匀顶点态、量子行走和拓扑数据分析中的紧凑均匀子集态),为其进一步应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Efficient preparation of Dicke states and, more generally, permutation-symmetric states is important for quantum metrology, quantum networking, and collective quantum information processing. Measurements and classical feedforward enable low-depth preparations of these states, with a cost of ancillary qubits. In this work, we introduce an exact constant-depth adaptive preparation protocol for arbitrary Dicke-$(n,k)$ states and further symmetric states. We first provide a protocol preparing the uniform subset superposition state, as a primitive, using constant-depth adaptive circuit with $O(k^2\log^2 n)$ ancillary qubits and success probability at least $1/k$. This yields an exact, probabilistic, constant-depth Dicke-state preparation protocol using $O\left(n^2+k^2\log^2 n+kn\log n\log\log n\right)$ ancillary qubits. Parallel repetition suppresses the failure probability exponentially without increasing the quantum depth. Moreover, the uniform subset superposition state is also of independent interest as the uniform vertex state of the Johnson graph and as the compact uniform subset state appearing in quantum-walk and topological-data-analysis algorithms. We further establish a general lifting framework that coherently combines clean unitary Dicke-state preparation circuits to prepare arbitrary symmetric states with only polynomial ancillary overhead. Combined with recent constant-depth unitary Dicke-state constructions, this gives an exact constant-depth preparation protocol for arbitrary $n$-qubit symmetric states using $O(n^3\sqrt{\log n})$ ancillary qubits.

</details>


### [159] [Perfect Discrimination of Non-Orthogonal Quantum States via Adaptive Post-Measurement Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01167)
*Hanwool Lee,Teiko Heinosaari*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子态辨识问题，表明在测量之前获得一比特经典信息无法帮助完美区分成对非正交量子态，但当这比特信息在测量之后由接收方查询获得时，反而可能实现完美区分。作者提出了一个查询框架，并给出了一种寻找最优策略的通用方法，该方法可归结为标准的最小误差辨识问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统直觉认为，提前获得信息至少与事后获得信息同等有价值。但本文挑战这一直觉，探究经典信息到达时机（测量前vs测量后）对量子态辨识能力的影响，发现事后信息在某些情况下更有价值，从而打破常规认知。

Method: 提出了一个"查询"框架：发送方在响应接收方的请求时提供经典信息，该请求可以依赖于测量结果。在此框架中给出了寻找最优策略的通用方法，并证明该问题可转化为辅助系综（auxiliary ensemble）上的标准最小误差态辨识问题。

Result: 证明在测量后到达的一比特经典信息可以比测量前到达的相同信息更有用；某些成对非正交量子态在允许查询依赖测量结果的情况下可以被完美区分，而之前的已知结论是仅靠测量前一比特信息不可能完美区分。

Conclusion: 信息的价值不仅取决于其内容，还取决于其到达时机以及接收者能否根据测量调整查询策略。事后查询信息为量子态辨识提供了新的能力维度，且最优策略可通过已知的最小误差辨识技术求解。

Abstract: A set of pairwise non-orthogonal quantum states cannot be perfectly discriminated, and this remains true even when one bit of classical partial information is available prior to the measurement. Contrary to the usual intuition that earlier information is at least as valuable as later information, we show that the same bit can be more useful when it arrives after the measurement. We present a framework for state discrimination in which the sender provides classical information in response to a request from the receiver, which we refer to as a query. In some cases, pairwise non-orthogonal states can be perfectly discriminated when the query is allowed to depend on the measurement outcome. We give a general method for finding the optimal strategy in this setting, which turns out to be the standard minimum-error discrimination problem for an auxiliary ensemble.

</details>


### [160] [Hybrid Quantum Neural Networks: Theory, Implementations, and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01194)
*Léo Monbroussou,Maniraman Periyasamy,Viacheslav Kuzmin,Pavel Sekatski,Viktoria Patapovich,Asel Sagingalieva,Alexey Melnikov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了混合量子神经网络（HQNN），系统梳理了其理论基础、代表性架构、实现挑战与已有性能报告，为机器学习和量子机器学习两个社区提供了结构化的领域全景，并指出了未来的研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子机器学习领域发展迅速，但架构、基准和硬件假设的多样化使得评估各种提案的实用性、识别真正优势所在、以及指导实践者如何使用这些模型变得困难。因此需要一个系统化的综述来整合当前状态。

Method: 该综述通过以下方式完成：总结混合量子神经网络的主要理论和方法论基础；调查已开发的最有前景的架构；检验其实现挑战和已报告的性能表现。

Result: 研究发现：近期基准测试表明规模化的优势尚未被证实；但理论工作已识别出量子模型具有可证明优势的任务；混合方法在实践问题中使用紧凑的量子组件和显著更少的可训练参数取得了有希望的结果。

Conclusion: 该综述为混合量子神经网络领域提供了结构化的视角，整合了不同视角，有助于识别未来研究和应用驱动开发的有前景路径。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence has been transformed by deep neural networks, yet the search for new learning architectures continues. Quantum machine learning offers one such direction, and hybrid quantum neural networks, which combine classical neural-network components with quantum information processing units, have emerged as a practical framework for near-term quantum technologies. However, the rapid development of the field across diverse architectures, benchmarks and hardware assumptions makes it difficult to assess the utility of various proposals, identify where genuine advantages may arise, and determine how practitioners can use these models. While recent benchmarks caution that such gains have not yet been demonstrated at scale, theoretical work has identified tasks on which quantum models hold provable advantages, and hybrid approaches have delivered promising results on practical problems using deliberately compact quantum components and substantially fewer trainable parameters. Here, we review hybrid quantum neural networks for the machine-learning and quantum-machine-learning communities. We summarize their main theoretical and methodological foundations, survey some of the most promising architectures developed so far, and examine their implementation challenges and reported performance. By consolidating these perspectives, this review provides a structured view of the state of the field and helps identify promising paths for future research and application-driven development.

</details>


### [161] [Quantum sequential parameter testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01248)
*Simon Morelli,Ricard Ravell Rodríguez,John Calsamiglia,Ramón Muñoz-Tapia,Gael Sentís,Michalis Skotiniotis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出顺序参数测试（sequential parameter testing）框架，在连续参数空间中通过逐步排除足够遥远的竞争值来以目标误差容限确定未知参数，并引入"双峰检验"（twin-peaks test）作为顺序似然比检验的计算高效类比。该框架应用于量子比特的相位和纯度测试，数值结果表明自适应投影测量在相位测试中可与集体协变测量达到相同的平均样本成本，局部测量在纯度测试中最优且顺序测试显著节省样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顺序假设检验在离散假设集上已成熟，但将其扩展到连续参数空间存在额外挑战且此前基本未被探索。作者旨在填补这一空白，建立适用于连续参数的顺序测试框架，以满足资源高效的认证任务需求。

Method: 作者定义了顺序参数测试框架，通过设定误差容限并排除足够遥远的竞争参数值来判定未知参数；引入"双峰检验"作为对顺序似然比检验的自然且计算高效的类比；并将该框架分别应用于量子比特的相位测试和纯度测试两类任务进行分析。

Result: 对于相位测试，数值结果表明自适应投影测量达到与固定副本数集体协变测量相同的平均样本成本；对于纯度测试，局部测量被证明是最优的，且顺序参数测试相比固定样本量协议带来显著的平均样本节省。

Conclusion: 参数测试被确立为一个操作上有意义的框架，为涉及连续参数的高资源效率认证任务提供了系统化的方法论支撑。

Abstract: Sequential strategies in hypothesis testing use a variable number of measurement rounds, allowing a decision to be made as soon as the observed data provide a prescribed level of error tolerance. Although sequential testing is well established for a discrete set of hypotheses, extending this framework to a continuous parameter space poses additional challenges and has remained largely unexplored. In this article, we introduce sequential \textit{parameter testing}, a framework for determining an unknown parameter up to a prescribed tolerance by ruling out sufficiently distant competing values with a target error tolerance. We clarify its operational distinction from conventional parameter estimation and develop sequential tests for continuous families of hypotheses, introducing the \textit{twin-peaks test} as a natural and computationally efficient analog of sequential likelihood-ratio testing. We apply the framework to two paradigmatic quantum tasks: testing the phase and the purity of a qubit. For phase testing, we show numerically that adaptive projective measurements achieve the same average sample cost as collective covariant measurements with a fixed number of copies. For purity testing, local measurements are optimal, and sequential parameter testing yields significant average sample savings over fixed sample-size protocols. Our results establish parameter testing as an operationally meaningful framework for resource-efficient certification tasks involving continuous parameters.

</details>


### [162] [Sharp Quantum Capacity Thresholds: Exponential Strong Converses for Degradable and Antidegradable Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01308)
*Tulja Varun Kondra,Raphael Brinster,Hermann Kampermann,Dagmar Bruß,Nikolai Wyderka*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明了任何有限维可退化（degradable）和反退化（antidegradable）量子信道在噪声信道容量之上都满足指数级强逆（exponential strong converse），即编码方案的保真度随信道使用次数呈指数衰减。这首次为量子擦除信道在全参数范围内建立了针对所有编码方案的指数强逆，并通过接收端后处理证明了指数强逆界的保持性，从而为任意有限维信道提供了可高效计算的半定规划界，改进了Pauli信道的界，并为非可退化的多级振幅阻尼信道族给出了精确的指数强逆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信道的量子容量是量子信息可靠传输的最大速率，但对于一般信道需要优化任意多次信道使用。可退化和反退化信道是两类核心例外，其容量可由单字母相干信息给出。然而，在此前的研究中，当允许固定的非最大误差时，这些信道是否能在容量之上通信仍是开放问题。解决这一问题对理解量子信道容量的鲁棒性和强逆性质具有重要意义。

Method: 论文通过证明有限维可退化和反退化信道在容量以上速率具有指数衰减的保真度，从而建立指数级强逆。作者利用信道结构特性（可退化/反退化），结合量子信息论中的保真度衰减技术，并通过证明指数强逆界在接收端后处理下的保持性，进一步推广到一般信道。对于一般有限维信道，推导出可高效计算的半定规划（SDP）界；针对Pauli信道和多级振幅阻尼信道，分别给出改进的和精确的指数强逆结果。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 对所有有限维可退化和反退化信道，在容量以上任意速率，所有编码方案的保真度随信道使用次数指数衰减；(2) 首次给出了量子擦除信道在整个参数范围内的全编码指数强逆，此前仅对几乎所有编码成立；(3) 证明指数强逆界在接收端后处理下保持，由此为任意有限维信道提供了可高效计算的SDP界；(4) 得到了Pauli信道的改进界；(5) 为非可退化的多级振幅阻尼信道族建立了精确的指数强逆。

Conclusion: 该工作彻底解决了可退化和反退化信道在固定非最大误差容忍条件下容量之上的通信问题，证明了这些信道的强逆性质。指数强逆在接收端后处理下的保持性为一般信道的强逆研究提供了新工具，并展示了其在实际信道（如Pauli信道、振幅阻尼信道）中的广泛应用。这一结果深化了对量子信道容量极限和量子通信可靠性的理解。

Abstract: The quantum capacity of a noisy channel quantifies the maximum rate at which quantum information can be transmitted reliably. For general channels, its evaluation requires an optimization over arbitrarily many channel uses. Degradable channels form a central exception: their capacity is given by the single-letter coherent information, while antidegradable channels have zero capacity. Nevertheless, even for these fundamental classes, it has remained open whether communication above capacity becomes possible when a fixed non-maximal error is tolerated. Here we resolve this problem by proving an exponential strong converse for every finite-dimensional degradable and antidegradable channel: at any rate above capacity, the fidelity of every coding scheme decays exponentially with the number of channel uses. As an immediate consequence, we establish the first all-code exponential strong converse for the quantum erasure channel throughout its full parameter range, strengthening previous results that applied only to almost all codes. We also show that exponential strong-converse bounds are preserved under receiver post-processing. This yields efficiently computable semidefinite-programming bounds for arbitrary finite-dimensional channels, improved bounds for Pauli channels, and an exact exponential strong converse for a nondegradable multilevel amplitude-damping family.

</details>


### [163] [Compiler Framework for 3D Neutral-Atom Quantum Computers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01316)
*Chen Huang,Zhemin Zhang,Zhao Zhang,Xudong Lv,Zhiding Liang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: Piqasso是首个面向三维中性原子量子计算机的编译器，通过将原子在垂直方向分层布局（存储、纠缠、读出），利用垂直跳变路由和多AOD调度，相较于现有平面编译器可将原子输运距离降低2.1倍，执行速度提升最高7.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中性原子量子计算机已能在三维镊子阵列中排列原子，但现有编译器均假设平面几何布局，未能利用垂直维度来提升编译效率和执行性能。

Method: Piqasso包含三个核心组件：(1) 解析式放置算法，满足轴向间隙光学约束并将存储、纠缠、读出堆叠为不同层；(2) 路由器通过短垂直跳变将门操作搭档原子带到一起，利用平面外绕行规避平面内交叉冲突；(3) 多声光偏转器(AOD)调度器跨焦平面并行化原子输运。

Result: 在34个电路基准上，相较于最先进的平面编译器，Piqasso将原子输运距离降低2.1倍，执行速度最高提升7.3倍，移动保真度提升2.2倍，序列化输运轮数减少1.8倍，且所有性能优势随规模扩大而进一步增强。

Conclusion: 三维编译器能够显著释放中性原子量子计算垂直维度的潜力，并随着系统规模扩大展现出更明显的性能优势。

Abstract: Neutral-atom quantum computers can now arrange atoms in three-dimensional tweezer arrays, yet every existing compiler assumes a flat geometry. We present Piqasso, a compiler that exploits the vertical axis by stacking storage, entanglement, and readout into distinct layers. Its pipeline pairs an analytical placement respecting axial-clearance optics with a router that brings gate partners together via short vertical hops---bypassing in-plane crossing conflicts through out-of-plane detours---and a multi-AOD scheduler that parallelizes transport across focal planes. On 34 circuits, Piqasso reduces atom transport distance by 2.1$\times$ over a state-of-the-art planar compiler, yielding up to 7.3$\times$ faster execution, 2.2$\times$ higher movement fidelity, and 1.8$\times$ fewer serialized transport rounds, with all gains widening at scale.

</details>


### [164] [Analytic Qubit Separation between POVMs and Projective Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01317)
*Lin Zhu,Ranyiliu Chen,Xin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次通过完全解析的Bell泛函分离了局部维度为2时的qubit POVM与投影测量两种测量模型，并给出了严格的解析下界差距大于1/1000。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义测量（POVM）可以通过扩充希尔伯特空间后以投影方式实现，但这一膨胀过程会改变可用的局域维度。作者希望在保持局域维度为2的前提下，证明POVM与投影测量两种测量模型在Bell非局域性上存在本质区别。

Method: 构造了一个有理系数的Bell泛函，其最优值在qubit POVM下可达2√2+1/100（通过一个具有有理矩阵元素的三输出qubit POVM实现）；同时证明所有qubit投影策略被2√2+√5/250+√2/32400所界，从而给出解析的间隙。采用Lean语言提供分离定理的机器可验证证书，并利用精确的level-3非交换sum-of-squares证书证明该显式qubit策略达到无限制有限维张量积量子最优值。

Result: 实现了qubit POVM与qubit投影测量在任意共享两比特态上的首个完全解析Bell泛函分离，间隙大于1/1000。同时通过非交换sum-of-squares证书验证了显式策略的量子最优性。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在固定局域维度为2的条件下，以完全解析的方式证明了广义测量（POVM）相对于投影测量在Bell实验中的优势，并通过Lean和sum-of-squares证书保证了结论的严格性。

Abstract: Generalized measurements can be implemented projectively after enlarging the Hilbert space, but this dilation changes the available local dimension. We construct a Bell functional with rational coefficients that separates the two measurement models at local dimension two. An explicit three-outcome qubit positive-operator-valued measure with rational matrix entries attains $2\sqrt2+1/100$. On the other hand, all qubit-projective strategies are bounded by $2\sqrt2+\sqrt5/250+\sqrt2/32400$, giving a fully analytic certified gap greater than $1/1000$. To our knowledge, this is the first fully analytic Bell-functional separation between qubit POVMs and qubit projective measurements over arbitrary shared two-qubit states. Lean certificate for the separation theorem is provided for completeness. Separately, an exact level-3 noncommutative sum-of-squares certificate proves that the explicit qubit strategy attains the unrestricted finite-dimensional tensor-product quantum optimum.

</details>


### [165] [Expressive Power and Limitations of Multi-photon Quantum Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01365)
*Zeyu Xiao,Weixu Shi,Yizhi Wang,Lingling Lao,Junjie Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文定量分析了多光子量子神经网络（MPQNN）中光子数增加对表达力（expressivity）的影响。研究发现，在固定可观测量情况下，表达力的增强存在一个随模式数线性增长的阈值，阈值以下光子数增加可多项式提升表达力，阈值以上则无影响；而在可训练可观测量情况下，增加光子数始终能多项式提升表达力。数值模拟验证了理论结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多光子量子神经网络通过增加输入光子数有望提升模型表达能力，但此前缺乏对光子数增加如何精确影响表达力以及该增强是否有上限的定量理解。

Method: 通过推导两种情形下近似误差的上界来定量估计MPQNN的表达力：(1) 固定可观测量情形；(2) 可训练可观测量情形。并辅以数值模拟进行验证。

Result: 固定可观测量情形下存在一个与模式数线性相关的阈值：低于阈值时，增加光子数可多项式提升表达力；高于阈值时，增加光子数不再影响表达力。可训练可观测量情形下，增加光子数始终能多项式提升表达力。

Conclusion: 该工作阐明了多光子量子特征在QNN中的性能提升及其局限性，为在量子机器学习中利用多光子优势提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Quantum neural networks (QNNs) have shown promise in leveraging quantum computation for machine learning tasks. Utilizing multiple identical photons as input, multi-photon quantum neural networks (MPQNNs) have the potential to enhance the expressivity through increasing the photon number. However, how precisely the expressivity of an MPQNN is affected by an increase in photon number, and whether it can be infinitely enhanced by increasing the photon number, remains unexplored. In this work, we quantitatively estimate the expressivity of this model by deriving upper bounds on approximation error in two cases. In the case of a fixed observable, there exists a threshold that scales linearly with the mode number. Below the threshold, the expressivity of an MPQNN can be enhanced polynomially by increasing the photon number. Above the threshold, however, increasing the photon number does not affect the expressivity. In the case of a trainable observable, the expressivity can always be enhanced polynomially by increasing the photon number. These findings are then validated by numerical simulations. Our work elucidates the performance enhancement of multi-photon quantum feature in QNNs, as well as its limitations, offering guidance for leveraging multi-photon advantages in quantum machine learning.

</details>


### [166] [Automated Construction and Verification of Unextendible Product Bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01438)
*Zicheng Han,Wanchen Zhang,Fei Shi,Xiande Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于SAT求解器的框架来构造不可扩展乘积基（UPB）。通过定义O_N-瓦片分解并证明瓦片到UPB的定理，将搜索UPB的问题编码为布尔可满足性问题，并利用SAT求解器生成显式实例。同时实现了基于局部正交图和未饱和子空间的UPB验证算法。通过该框架，在三方和四方系统中获得了多种尺寸的UPB，包括C^3⊗C^3⊗C^3中的尺寸13到23。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UPB在量子信息理论中具有重要应用（如完全纠缠子空间、束缚纠缠、局域不可区分性），而UPB的存在性与其基数（尺寸）密切相关。因此，确定给定多体系统中特定尺寸的UPB是否存在是该领域的核心问题之一。

Method: 1) 基于N维超立方体的分解定义O_N-瓦片分解；2) 证明瓦片到UPB定理：每个O_N-瓦片分解通过基于瓦片傅里叶乘积基和全局停止态"stopper state"的构造诱导出一个UPB；3) 将搜索此类分解编码为布尔可满足性问题（SAT）并用SAT求解器生成显式实例；4) 实现基于局部正交图和未饱和子空间的UPB验证算法，用于判定任意有限乘积态集合是否构成UPB。

Result: 在三方和四方系统中获得了多种尺寸的UPB，包括在C^3⊗C^3⊗C^3中尺寸13到23的UPB。小维度实例还可作为递归构造的种子UPB，在更大的多方系统中产生进一步的例子。

Conclusion: 该SAT辅助框架为系统性地搜索和构造特定尺寸UPB提供了一种有效方法，并且所获得的小维度UPB实例可以通过递归构造扩展到更大的多方系统，具有可扩展性。

Abstract: Unextendible product bases (UPBs) are important structures in quantum information theory, with applications to completely entangled subspaces, bound entanglement, and local indistinguishability. Since many properties and applications of UPBs are closely related to their cardinalities, one of the central problems in the study of UPBs is to determine whether UPBs of prescribed sizes exist in a given multipartite system. In this paper, we introduce a SAT-assisted framework based on decompositions of the \(N\)-dimensional hypercube. We define \(O_N\)-tile decompositions and prove a tile-to-UPB theorem: every \(O_N\)-tile decomposition induces a UPB through a construction based on tile-wise Fourier product bases and a global stopper state. We then encode the search for such decompositions as a Boolean satisfiability (SAT) problem and use SAT solvers to generate explicit instances. In terms of verification, we also implement a UPB verification algorithm based on local orthogonality graphs and unsaturated subspaces. The algorithm can be used to determine whether an arbitrary finite set of product states forms a UPB. Using this framework, we obtain UPBs of several sizes in some tripartite and quadripartite systems, including sizes \(13,14,\ldots,23\) in \(\mathbb C^3\otimes\mathbb C^3\otimes\mathbb C^3\). Moreover, the small-dimensional instances obtained here can serve as seed UPBs for recursive constructions, leading to further examples in larger multipartite systems.

</details>


### [167] [Spatial-Order Hierarchy of Time-Dependent Exchange-Correlation Potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01483)
*Naoki Negishi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在精确含时密度泛函理论中，基于相对于含时Hartree-Fock(TDHF)参考的约化密度矩阵非对角展开，建立了交换关联势中相互作用分量与动能关联分量之间的可表示性约束，并揭示了两者之间的层级结构关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确含时密度泛函理论将交换关联势分解为相互作用分量和动能关联分量，但这两者之间的结构关系尚不清楚。理解这一关系对于构建非绝热泛函至关重要。

Method: 基于含时Hartree-Fock(TDHF)参考的约化密度矩阵非对角展开，建立可表示性约束。分析非零线性项与高阶非对角结构对电流密度和动能关联分量的影响。在一维两电子关联淬火体系中，通过保留精确相互作用分量同时将动能分量设为零来验证。

Result: 非零线性项产生非HF电流密度而动能关联分量仍保持HF可表示；高阶非对角结构激活动能分量。在关联淬火体系中，仅保留相互作用分量会抑制精确演化的密度展宽。

Conclusion: 建立了密度运动方程的层级结构，为非绝热泛函构造提供了精确约束条件。

Abstract: Exact time-dependent density-functional theory separates the exchange-correlation potential into interaction and kinetic-correlation components, but the structural relation between them remains unknown. We establish a representability constraint based on the off-diagonal expansion of the one-electron reduced density matrix relative to a time-dependent Hartree-Fock (TDHF) reference. A non-zero linear term generates a non-HF current density while the kinetic-correlation component remains HF representable; higher-order off-diagonal structure activates the kinetic component. In a one-dimensional two-electron correlation quench, retaining the exact interaction component while setting the kinetic component to zero suppresses the density broadening of the exact evolution. These results establish a hierarchy of density equations of motion and provide an exact constraint for non-adiabatic functional construction.

</details>


### [168] [Non-Hermitian photon number filtering using N00N state Bloch oscillations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01484)
*Sean Crowe,Joanna Ptasinski,Melvin Pascoguin,Stefan Evans*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究利用线性增长有效折射率波导阵列中的模拟非对称损耗轮廓，实现N=1和N=2光子N00N态的布洛赫振荡，通过控制输入相位进行动态切换，分别达到80%的双光子输出和90%的单光子输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在光子计算和网络中，高效的量子态制备至关重要。N00N态是量子计量和量子信息处理中的关键资源，但如何选择性地输出特定光子数态并实现动态可控是一个挑战。本文探索利用布洛赫振荡与损耗工程的结合，为光子电路中的态制备提供新路径。

Method: 采用线性增长有效折射率的波导阵列，通过模拟非对称损耗轮廓实现布洛赫振荡；以等概率输入的N=1和N=2 N00N态为初始态；在半个布洛赫周期附近引流部分波导能量，选择性输出特定光子数态；通过调谐输入相位实现输出态的切换。

Result: 通过调谐输入相位，可以实现动态切换：2光子主导输出占80%，1光子主导输出占90%，验证了该系统对光子数态的选择性输出能力。

Conclusion: 该方案为光子电路中的态制备提供了改进路径，有望在光子计算和量子网络应用中发挥作用。

Abstract: We explore Bloch oscillations of $N=1$ and $N=2$ photonic N00N states, using an array of linearly growing effective index waveguides with a simulated asymmetric loss profile. Starting with equal probability input $N=1$ and $N=2$ N00N states, and siphoning off a portion of the waveguides near the half Bloch period, we selectively output specific photon number states. Tuning the input phase, we achieve dynamic switching between 2-photon dominated (80\% of the output) and 1-photon dominated (90\% of the output) cases. This offers a path to improved state preparation in photonic circuits used in computing and networking.

</details>


### [169] [Limits of heralded photonic Bell-state generation in the presence of loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01549)
*Kevin J. Randles,Manuel H. Muñoz-Arias,Mohan Sarovar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析了五种用于生成备用（event-ready）光子贝尔态的方案，在从集总损耗模型到实际集成频域实现的误差模型层级下，对比了它们的前兆概率、保真度和误差鲁棒性，为集成光子学中的量子资源态生成提供设计指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量纠缠光子态是量子信息科学应用的基础，而贝尔态作为最基础的小型纠缠态，其生成质量直接决定了上层应用（如团簇态、中继图态）的性能基线。因此，理解种子态生成在现实误差机制下的表现至关重要，特别是在集成光子学实验中。

Method: 本文构建了一个逐层递进的误差模型层级：(1) 解析可处理的集总损耗模型；(2) 包含多光子发射误差的实际前兆单光子源模型；(3) 具有架构依赖损耗的集成频率箱实现模型。在此框架下，系统对比了五种前兆贝尔态生成方案的前兆概率、保真度和误差鲁棒性。

Result: 研究发现：基于高阶多光子干涉的方案在低损耗实现中具有更高的保真度鲁棒性；而当概率性单光子源或有损分束器主导资源成本时，低光子数方案可能更优。在频率箱架构中，有源分束器的损耗会显著影响最优资源态生成策略的选择。

Conclusion: 本文结果为集成离散变量量子光子学提供了设计指导，对频率箱架构尤为重要——在该架构下有源分束器损耗是决定最优资源态生成策略的关键因素。

Abstract: High-quality entangled states of photons underlie quantum information science (QIS) applications across communication, sensing, and computing. In many discrete-variable photonic QIS architectures, large application-ready states (e.g., cluster states, repeater graph states) are constructed via fusion measurements on small entangled seed states, of which Bell states are the fundamental example. The quality of seed-state generation therefore sets a baseline for application performance, making it crucial to understand this process under realistic error mechanisms, particularly in integrated photonics experiments. In this work, we analyze five heralded schemes for generating event-ready photonic Bell states, contrasting their heralding probabilities, fidelities, and error robustness. We develop a hierarchy of error models, progressing from an analytically tractable lumped-loss model to realistic heralded single-photon sources with multiphoton emission errors and finally to integrated frequency-bin implementations with architecture-dependent loss. Across these models, we find that schemes based on higher-order multiphoton interference provide superior fidelity robustness in low-loss implementations, while lower-photon-number schemes can be preferable when probabilistic sources or lossy beamsplitters dominate the resource cost. Our results provide design guidance for integrated discrete-variable quantum photonics, with particular relevance for frequency-bin architectures where active beamsplitter loss can determine the optimal resource-state-generation strategy.

</details>


### [170] [Near-optimal quantum metrology with few-qubit measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01617)
*Liang Mao,Senrui Chen,Hsin-Yuan Huang,John Preskill,Sisi Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类仅使用少量比特测量的量子计量学协议，显著降低了多比特态多参数估计所需的量子资源开销。对于任意纯态，该协议可逼近量子Cramér-Rao界，样本复杂度开销随比特数线性增长；对典型Haar随机态，开销可降至常数。其核心建立在量子态认证与计量学之间的普适联系上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的多量子比特态多参数计量协议通常需要高度复杂的量子测量，导致大量的量子资源消耗。作者旨在设计一种仅需少量量子比特测量的协议，从而在保持计量精度的同时大幅降低资源成本。

Method: 作者引入一族仅使用少量量子比特测量的计量学协议。其关键思想是建立量子态认证协议与计量学问题之间的普适联系：认证协议的精度决定了计量学协议的开销。对于任意纯态，该协议可逼近量子Cramér-Rao界，样本复杂度开销与量子比特数呈线性关系；对于典型的Haar随机态，该开销可进一步降低为常数。

Result: 所提出的协议对任意纯态能达到接近量子Cramér-Rao界的最优精度，样本复杂度开销随量子比特数线性增长（与待估计参数数量无关）；对典型Haar随机态，开销降至常数。文章还通过哈密顿量从基态估计的实例进行了验证。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了量子态认证与量子计量学之间的普适联系，提出了资源高效的多参数量子计量协议，在保证接近理论最优精度的前提下显著降低了测量复杂度，为大规模量子计量应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Quantum metrology, which addresses parameter estimation in quantum systems, has broad applications across science and technology. Conventional metrology protocols for multi-qubit states in the multi-parameter regime typically require highly complex quantum measurements, leading to substantial quantum-resource costs. In this work, we introduce a family of metrology protocols that use only few-qubit measurements, thereby significantly reducing the required resources. For arbitrary pure states, one of our protocols approaches the quantum Cramér-Rao bound up to an overhead in sample complexity that scales linearly with the number of qubits, irrespective of the number of parameters to be estimated. For typical Haar-random states, this overhead can be reduced to a constant. Our results build on recent advances in quantum state certification protocols with few-qubit measurements: we establish a universal connection between certification and metrology in which the precision of the certification protocol determines the metrological overhead. We also illustrate our approach through an example of Hamiltonian estimation from ground states.

</details>


### [171] [Ring Optimized M-APSK Modulation for Discrete Modulated CV-QKD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01723)
*Seonguk Kim,Jun Heo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向离散调制连续变量量子密钥分发(CV-QKD)的多环M-APSK星座优化方法，通过优化环半径比和环概率提升有限尺寸密钥率，使16-APSK的最大传输距离相比传统二项式APSK方案延长约15%，且对小尺寸APSK星座优化增益更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统APSK结构采用固定环间距和预设环概率，限制了离散调制CV-QKD系统在有限尺寸条件下的密钥率性能，需要一种优化方法来提升传输性能。

Method: 提出多环M-APSK星座优化方法，核心在于优化环半径比(ring radius ratio)和环概率(ring probability)；利用基于Gram矩阵的方法计算平均态τ的非零谱，并通过保真度(fidelity)将优化的离散平均态与高斯平均态进行对比评估。

Result: 所提优化结构使16-APSK的最大传输距离相比传统二项式APSK结构延长约15%；优化增益在小尺寸APSK星座中更为显著，因为此时平均态与高斯调制之间的结构差距更大。

Conclusion: 通过优化APSK星座的环参数，可以有效提升离散调制CV-QKD的有限尺寸密钥率性能，特别是对小尺寸星座而言，优化带来的增益更为明显。

Abstract: This paper proposes a multi ring M-APSK constellation optimization method for discrete-modulated continuous variable quantum key distribution. Unlike conventional APSK structures with fixed ring spacing and predefined ring probabilities, the proposed method optimizes the ring radius ratio and ring probability to improve the finite-size secret key rate. A method based on the Gram matrix is used to calculate the nonzero spectrum of the average state $τ$, and fidelity is used to compare the optimized discrete average state with the Gaussian average state. The results show that the proposed structure extends the maximum transmission distance of 16-APSK by approximately 15% compared with the conventional binomial APSK structure. The optimization gain is larger for small size APSK constellations, where the average state has a larger structural gap from Gaussian modulation.

</details>


### [172] [Robust Nonclassical magnon pair generation and Cauchy-Schwarz inequality violation in a hybrid electromagnonic system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01741)
*Sabur A Barbhuiya,Ajeet Kumar,Amarendra K Sarma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个混合量子系统，其中超导量子比特通过公共腔模介导两个Kittel磁振子模式间的相互作用，发现两个磁振子驱动源之间的相对相位可以作为主动补偿旋钮，即使耦合不对称也能恢复磁振子阻塞所需的相消干涉，从而实现磁振子阻塞和强违反Cauchy-Schwarz不等式，为相位控制的磁振子量子信息处理提供了可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁振子系统有望实现差异巨大的量子平台间的耦合，磁振子阻塞可以产生非经典磁振子态，但在实际中依赖于破坏性干涉，这种干涉容易被制造引起的耦合不对称所破坏，亟需解决这一实际问题。

Method: 研究了超导量子比特耦合到公共腔模、并介导两个Kittel磁振子模式间相互作用的混合系统。通过调节两个磁振子驱动之间的相对相位作为补偿旋钮来恢复干涉，推导了补偿给定耦合不对称的相位解析条件，并与精确数值模拟进行验证。

Result: 调节驱动相位可以同时产生磁振子阻塞和强违反经典Cauchy-Schwarz不等式；推导的相位补偿解析条件与精确数值模拟一致；驱动相位作为一个控制旋钮可在经典与量子 regime 之间切换系统的行为。

Conclusion: 驱动相位可作为一种主动补偿机制修复耦合不对称引起的干涉破坏，实现相位控制的磁振子量子信息处理。

Abstract: Magnonic systems are a promising resource for quantum technologies because of their ability to couple significantly disparate quantum platforms and the generation of nonclassical magnon states through magnon blockade has attracted growing attention. In practice, however magnon blockade relies on a destructive interference that is easily spoiled by fabrication-induced coupling asymmetries. Here we show that the relative phase between two magnon drives acts as an active compensation knob that restores this interference even when the couplings are mismatched. We study two Kittel magnon modes in a hybrid system in which a superconducting qubit coupled to a common cavity mode mediates the inter-mode interaction and we find that tuning the drive phase produces both magnon blockade and a strong violation of the classical Cauchy--Schwarz inequality. We derive the analytic condition for the phase that compensates a given coupling asymmetry and confirm it against exact numerical simulations. The drive phase thereby serves as a control knob that switches the system between classical and quantum regimes. Our results enable phase-controlled magnonic quantum information processing.

</details>


### [173] [Noise-robust discrimination of incoherent point sources with spatial-mode demultiplexing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01765)
*Jian-Qiang Liu,Chao-Ning Hu,Jun Xin,Xiao-Ming Lu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文理论预测并实验证明，仅使用两个最低阶厄米-高斯模式的降维空间模式解复用（SPADE）测量，在区分单光源和双非相干点光源时，对背景噪声表现出优异的鲁棒性。SPADE在所有源分离度下均优于直接成像（DI），即使在每像素背景信号比为0.11时也能逼近量子极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实应用（如天文观测和量子传感）中，背景噪声不可避免，而传统直接成像在弱信号背景下区分单光源与双非相干点光源的能力受限。现有SPADE方案虽已展现出超越直接成像的潜力，但其在含背景噪声环境下的理论框架和实验验证尚不充分，亟需一种对噪声鲁棒的实际检测方案。

Method: 建立包含均匀背景噪声的理论框架，推导出解析的Chernoff指数表达式；实验上仅使用两个最低阶厄米-高斯模式实现降维SPADE测量，并对单源与双非相干点光源的二分类假设检验进行性能评估，同时与直接成像（DI）进行对比。

Result: 理论结果表明SPADE在所有源分离度下一致优于直接成像；实验验证显示，在每像素背景信号光子比为0.11时，SPADE的假设检验性能仍接近量子极限。该优势源于SPADE将源信息集中在最少检测模式中，从而减少累积的背景噪声影响。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种在背景噪声不可避免场景下（如天文观测和量子传感）具有实用价值的检测方案，证明了降维SPADE测量在低信噪比条件下的优越性和可行性。

Abstract: We theoretically predict and experimentally demonstrate that a reduced spatial-mode demultiplexing (SPADE) measurement using only the two lowest-order Hermite-Gaussian modes exhibits remarkable robustness against background noise in discriminating between a single source and two incoherent point sources. We establish a theoretical framework incorporating uniform background noise and derive an analytical Chernoff exponent expression, showing that SPADE consistently outperforms direct imaging (DI) across all source separations. Experimental results confirm that SPADE-based hypothesis testing approaches the quantum limit even when the background-to-signal photon ratio per pixel is 0.11. This advantage stems from SPADE's ability to concentrate source information into minimal detection modes, reducing the cumulative background noise impact. Our findings provide a practical detection scheme for applications where background noise is inevitable, such as astronomical observations and quantum sensing.

</details>


### [174] [The Sample Complexity of Fidelity Estimation to a Known Rank-$r$ Reference State Is $\widetildeΘ(r^2/\varepsilon^2)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01770)
*Gye Jin Lee,Sunghyeon Jo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文解决了估计根Uhlmann保真度样本复杂度的公开问题，证明其复杂度为S(r,ε)=Θ̃(r²/ε²)（含对数因子上闭区间），填补了先前下界Ω(r/ε²)和上界O(r²/ε²)之间的间隙。证明结合了精确谱矩匹配、径向偏置双重相关Wishart模型和Cauchy恒等式，并通过直接和嵌入与二项稀疏化获得了最优的1/ε²依赖。此外还证明了量子谱估计在恒定精度下的近二次下界Ω̃(r²)，确立了近二次障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前估计两个量子态之间根Uhlmann保真度的样本复杂度存在已知下界Ω(r/ε²)和上界O(r²/ε²)之间的显著间隙，这一公开问题由Wang提出。闭合这一间隙对于理解量子态层析的统计极限、优化量子信息处理任务中的资源分配具有重要意义，同时也是量子谱估计复杂度研究中的关键一环。

Method: 证明方法综合多种技术：(1) 精确谱矩匹配用于构造hard实例族；(2) 引入径向偏置的双重相关Wishart模型分析统计行为；(3) 应用Cauchy恒等式将态不可区分性问题约化为加权随机排列的长圈估计；(4) 通过直接和嵌入与二项稀疏化技巧获得最优的1/ε²依赖关系。下界在2r维系统上构造（σ为固定r维子空间上的最大混合态），针对不与σ交换的hard态族建立。

Result: 本文完全确定了估计根Uhlmann保真度的样本复杂度为S(r,ε)=Θ̃(r²/ε²)（对所有0<ε≤ε₀），其中ε₀为普适常数，从而在多项式意义上精确闭合了此前的复杂度间隙。同时证明了量子谱估计在恒定精度下的近二次下界Ω̃(r²)，结合已有的O(r²(log log r/log r)²)上界，确定了该场景下样本复杂度的多项式阶。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了根Uhlmann保真度估计的样本复杂度公开问题，确立了二次依赖于参考态秩、二次反比于误差的精确复杂度；同时为量子谱估计建立了近二次下界障碍，说明在恒定精度下谱估计本质上需要与r²成正比的样本量，这为量子态估计与量子计量学领域设定了新的理论边界。

Abstract: We settle the sample complexity of estimating the root Uhlmann fidelity $F(ρ,σ)=\operatorname{tr}\sqrt{\sqrtσρ\sqrtσ}$ between an unknown state $ρ$ and a known rank-$r$ reference state $σ$. Writing $S(r,\varepsilon)$ for the sample complexity at additive error $\varepsilon$, we resolve the open problem posed by Wang by closing, up to logarithmic factors, the gap between the previously known bounds $Ω(r/\varepsilon^2)$ and $O(r^2/\varepsilon^2)$. We prove $S(r,\varepsilon)=\widetildeΘ(r^2/\varepsilon^2)$ for all $0<\varepsilon\le\varepsilon_0$, where $\varepsilon_0>0$ is a universal constant. The lower bound already holds on a $2r$-dimensional system when $σ$ is maximally mixed on a fixed $r$-dimensional subspace, and for a hard family of states that do not commute with $σ$. The proof combines exact spectral moment matching, a radially size-biased doubly correlated Wishart model, and the Cauchy identity, reducing state indistinguishability to a long-cycle estimate for a weighted random permutation. A direct-sum embedding and binomial thinning yield the optimal $1/\varepsilon^2$ dependence. We also prove a near-quadratic lower bound $\widetildeΩ(r^2)$ for quantum spectrum estimation at constant accuracy. Combined with the recent $O(r^2(\log\log r/\log r)^2)$ upper bound, this determines the polynomial order of the sample complexity in this regime and establishes a near-quadratic barrier.

</details>


### [175] [Symmetry-Projected Compatible Multiparameter Quantum Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01831)
*G. R. Jin,Z. Y. Zhou,W. Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个通用的对称投影框架用于多参数量子传感，通过对编码生成元进行子空间保持与子空间变化分解，消除了量子Fisher信息矩阵（QFIM）及均值对称对数导数（SLD）对易矩阵中的所有跨扇区元素。在宇称保护的集体SU(2)自旋系统中，横向QFIM可通过自旋涨落直接认证，最优轴与反压缩正交分量对齐。应用于耗散单向扭转系统时，发现高度混合的瞬态可在宽时间窗口内表现出近平衡的海森堡尺度QFIM分量，同时稳态保持各向同性的N²/3横向QFIM缩放，但横向参数对的弱兼容性呈现尖锐的宇称依赖性。该对称保护消除了横向-纵向参数对的Uhlmann曲率，使得多参数量子Cramér-Rao界能够在渐近极限下同时饱和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多参数量子传感中，量子Fisher信息矩阵的精确计算和参数可同时估计性的判定往往复杂且困难。作者旨在通过对称性投影的方法，建立通用框架来简化多参数传感的分析，揭示对称性在参数估计兼容性、量子Cramér-Rao界饱和等方面的关键作用。

Method: 提出对称投影框架，将编码生成元分解为子空间保持（subspace-preserving）和子空间变化（subspace-changing）两部分。当子空间变化的生成元在被占据子空间内表现为标量时，对应的QFIM块简化为四倍对称化协方差矩阵。该框架应用于宇称保护的SU(2)自旋系统和耗散单向扭转系统，分析QFIM结构、Uhlmann曲率及参数估计兼容性。

Result: 在耗散单向扭转系统中，高度混合瞬态可在宽时间窗口内呈现近平衡的海森堡尺度QFIM分量（横向-纵向参数对(θ_y,θ_z)）；稳态保持各向同性横向QFIM（缩放到N²/3），但横向参数对(θ_x,θ_y)的弱兼容性表现出尖锐的宇称依赖性——奇数N失败而偶数N恢复；对称保护消除了横向-纵向参数对的Uhlmann曲率，使多参数量子Cramér-Rao界可在渐近极限下同时饱和。

Conclusion: 对称投影框架为多参数量子传感提供了统一的理论工具，揭示了对称性对QFIM结构、参数兼容性及估计精度的深远影响。该框架不仅简化了多参数传感的分析，还指明了通过对称性保护实现多参数量子Cramér-Rao界同时饱和的可行路径。

Abstract: We establish a general symmetry-projection framework for multiparameter quantum sensing. Decomposing encoding generators into subspace-preserving and subspace-changing components relative to a symmetry sector identically eliminates all cross-sector elements of the quantum Fisher information matrix (QFIM) and the mean symmetric logarithmic derivative (SLD) commutator matrix. When projected subspace-changing generators act as a scalar within the occupied subspace, the corresponding QFIM block reduces to four times the symmetrized covariance matrix, regardless of probe state purity. For parity-protected collective $\mathrm{SU}(2)$ spin systems, this renders the transverse QFIM directly certifiable via spin fluctuations, with the optimal axis aligned with the anti-squeezed quadrature. Applied to a dissipative one-axis-twisting system, our framework reveals that highly mixed transient states can exhibit nearly balanced, Heisenberg-scaled QFIM components for transverse--longitudinal parameter pairs $(θ_y,θ_z)$ over a broad time window. Furthermore, while the steady state retains an isotropic transverse QFIM scaling as $N^2/3$, weak compatibility for transverse parameter pairs $(θ_x,θ_y)$ exhibits a sharp parity dependence---failing for odd $N$ but restored for even $N$. The resulting symmetry protection eliminates the Uhlmann curvature for transverse--longitudinal pairs, enabling simultaneous saturation of the multi-parameter quantum Cramér-Rao bound in the asymptotic limit.

</details>


### [176] [Reinforcement-Learned Electric-Field Sensing with Asymmetrically Blockaded Rydberg Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01832)
*Shi-Qiang Qiao,Xue-Ke Song,Shi-Lei Su,Liu Ye,Dong Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于非对称阻塞效应的强化学习优化的里德伯静电计，在里德伯原子阵列中实现高灵敏度电场传感。通过微波缀饰诱导非对称阻塞抑制目标原子间相互作用，同时保持中心控制原子与目标原子间的耦合在Förster共振附近可调。经典Fisher信息随原子数近二次方标度并接近海森堡极限，强化学习设计的复合脉冲比单π脉冲将量子Fisher信息提升一个数量级。还建立了紧凑的六原子球形结构用于矢量电测量，可从校准轴向布居提取场方向，弱偏置场消除偶极-偶极诱导的符号和魔角歧义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维修正里德伯电场传感在量子传感领域具有重要应用价值，但现有方案面临原子间相互作用干扰、灵敏度受限、矢量测量困难等挑战。作者旨在通过非对称阻塞效应与强化学习优化相结合，实现高灵敏度、高精度的三维电场传感方案。

Method: 该方法利用微波缀饰诱导非对称阻塞：一方面抑制目标原子间的相互作用，另一方面使中心控制原子与目标原子的耦合在Förster共振附近保持场可调。可检测原子布居信号与场调节的阻塞半径相关联，通过态选择性读出实现电场传感。采用强化学习设计复合脉冲以增强量子Fisher信息，并构造六原子球面构型实现矢量测量，通过弱偏置场消除偶极-偶极诱导的歧义。

Result: 在平面原子阵列中，经典Fisher信息随原子数呈近二次方标度并趋近海森堡极限；强化学习复合脉冲将量子Fisher信息提升约一个数量级（相比单π脉冲）；六原子球面构型实现了矢量电测量，数值测试表明该方案对Rabi频率偏差、位置误差、残余目标间耦合及投影噪声具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种实验上可行的实现高精度三维里德伯电场传感的方案，兼具高灵敏度和矢量测量能力，为量子增强的电场传感技术提供了新的路径。

Abstract: We present a reinforcement learning-optimized Rydberg electrometer based on the asymmetric blockade effect and achieve high-sensitivity electric field sensing in Rydberg arrays. Microwave dressing induces asymmetric blockade to suppress interactions between target atoms, while keeping the coupling between the central control atom and target atoms field-tunable near Förster resonance. The field-regulated blockade radius affects the detectable atomic population signals, thereby enabling electric field sensing via state-selective readout. In planar atomic arrays, classical Fisher information exhibits near-quadratic scaling with atom number and approaches the Heisenberg limit. Reinforcement learning-designed composite pulses greatly enhance quantum Fisher information by up to one order of magnitude compared with single $π$ pulses. We further establish a compact six-atom spherical configuration for vector electrometry, in which field orientation is extracted from calibrated axial populations, and weak bias fields eliminate dipole-dipole-induced sign and magic-angle ambiguities. Numerical tests against Rabi frequency deviation, positional error, residual inter-target coupling and projection noise demonstrate the reliability of this scheme. This work provides an experimentally viable approach to realize high-precision three-dimensional Rydberg electric field sensing.

</details>


### [177] [The Pangaea Architecture: Fault-Tolerant Heterogeneous Topological Codes via a Quantum Bus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01887)
*Sheir Yarkoni,Chen Scheim,Daniel Hakshuri,Nadav Katz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: Pangaea是一种基于量子总线（quantum bus）的容错量子计算架构，通过辅助规范码条带（gauge-code strip）在远程拓扑码补丁之间介导逻辑操作，实现了三维推广的晶格手术，大幅降低物理量子比特开销（由O(d²N_L)降至O(dN_L)），在50逻辑量子比特规模下相比平面表面码架构节省高达10倍物理量子比特。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面晶格手术（planar lattice surgery）在布线上存在路由瓶颈，传统二维架构需要O(d²N_L)的物理量子比特来实现多量子比特交互，随着逻辑量子比特数量增长，物理资源开销和布线复杂度急剧上升，亟需一种能够突破二维限制、降低物理开销的容错量子计算架构。

Method: 引入量子总线作为辅助规范码条带，通过其测量重构联合逻辑算符，同时保持最近邻物理连通性。该架构支持异质拓扑码（如surface-color码）的联合奇偶校验测量，实现本征的多量子比特泡利操作，可视为三维化的晶格手术推广。通过伪阈值（pseudo-threshold）模拟验证了基于测量的远程CNOT原语的容错性，并据此构造了基于量子总线的本征异质15-to-1魔术态蒸馏模块。

Result: 仅需O(dN_L)个物理量子比特即可实现N_L个距离为d的逻辑量子比特的多量子比特交互，而传统二维架构需要O(d²N_L)。在50逻辑量子比特规模下，Pangaea在匹配逻辑错误率条件下相比平面表面码架构使用最多减少10倍的物理量子比特。伪阈值模拟验证了surface-surface和surface-color联合奇偶校验测量的容错性。

Conclusion: Pangaea确立了一种可扩展的三维容错量子计算架构，通过量子总线解决了平面晶格手术的路由瓶颈，是未来大型容错量子计算机的有前景的架构方案。

Abstract: We introduce Pangaea, a fault-tolerant quantum architecture that uses a quantum bus to mediate logical operations between remote patches of two-dimensional topological codes. The bus is an auxiliary gauge-code strip whose measurements reconstruct joint logical operators while preserving nearest-neighbor physical connectivity. Enabling native heterogeneous topological codes and multi-qubit Pauli operations, the quantum bus can be interpreted as a three-dimensional generalization of lattice surgery. We require only $O(dN_L)$ physical qubits to implement multi-qubit interactions for $N_L$ distance-$d$ logical qubits, compared to $O(d^2N_L)$ of traditional two-dimensional architectures. At the 50-logical-qubit scale, Pangaea uses up to $10\times$ fewer physical qubits than planar surface-code architectures at matched logical error rates. We verify fault-tolerance of long-range measurement-based CNOT primitives for both surface--surface and surface--color joint parity measurements using pseudo-threshold simulations. We use this protocol to construct a native heterogeneous 15-to-1 magic-state distillation module using the quantum bus. These results establish Pangaea as a scalable architecture for three-dimensional fault-tolerant quantum computing that resolves the routing bottleneck of planar lattice surgery.

</details>


### [178] [Oraqle: An Empirical Analysis of Qubit Readout and Discriminators in Quantum Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01939)
*Emmanouil Giortamis,Aleksandra Świerkowska,Sandra Stankovic,Felix Gust,Benjamin Lienhard,Pramod Bhatotia*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了Oraqle，一个端到端基准测试框架，用于评估量子比特态读出及其对量子纠错（QEC）性能的影响。研究发现：测量时长可大幅缩短而逻辑错误率几乎不受影响；判别器复杂度对QEC性能影响甚微（残差误差源于器件物理而非模型）；读出对逻辑错误率的影响取决于硬件在QEC领域中的位置，且该影响窗口随器件成熟而扩大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错（QEC）是通往容错量子计算最有前景的路径，其运行于测量-解码-纠错的连续循环中。读出是解码器唯一的地面真值来源，但同时也是堆栈中最慢、最容易出错的操作，其特性随量子比特变化且随时间漂移。尽管读出角色如此核心，但目前缺乏从读出特征、读出长度及其与ML判别器协同设计的角度来深入研究QEC性能的工作。

Method: 提出的Oraqle是一个端到端基准测试框架，在真实实验提取的量子比特态读出数据集、最先进的ML判别器、多种QEC码以及覆盖当前到未来器件的硬件机制下，系统地评估量子比特态读出及其对QEC性能的影响。

Result: 研究揭示了三个非对称性发现：(1) 测量时长可显著缩短而对逻辑错误率几乎无惩罚；(2) 判别器复杂度对QEC性能影响甚微，因为残差误差被写入器件物理而非模型中；(3) 量子比特态读出对逻辑错误率的影响取决于硬件在QEC领域中的位置，且该影响窗口随器件成熟而扩大。

Conclusion: 读出特性在QEC性能中起着关键但此前未充分研究的作用；通过合理减少测量时长和简化判别器复杂度可在不牺牲QEC性能的前提下优化系统；随着量子硬件成熟，读出优化的重要性将进一步凸显。

Abstract: Quantum error correction (QEC) is the most promising route toward fault-tolerant quantum computing and, thus, useful quantum computers. QEC operates as a continuous measure-decode-correct cycle: ancilla qubits are read out, a decoder infers errors from the resulting syndromes, and corrections are applied before the next round begins. Within this loop, readout occupies a uniquely critical role, as it is the sole source of ground truth available to the decoder. Yet readout is also the slowest and most error-prone operation in the stack, with characteristics that vary across qubits and drift over time; This complexity propagates directly to the classical control hardware, and in particular to the FPGA-hosted machine-learning (ML) discriminator that must classify each analog signal into a binary syndrome outcome. Despite this central role, QEC performance has not yet been studied in depth from the perspective of readout characteristics, readout length, and their co-design with an ML discriminator.
  We introduce Oraqle, an end-to-end benchmarking framework that evaluates qubit-state readout and its impact on QEC performance across real experimentally extracted qubit-state-readout datasets, state-of-the-art ML discriminators, multiple QEC codes, and hardware regimes spanning current to projected devices. Our study reveals three asymmetric findings: The measurement duration can be significantly reduced with nearly no penalty to the logical error rate; The discriminator complexity barely affects the QEC performance, as residual errors are written into device physics rather than the model; and the impact of qubit-state readout on the logical error rate is conditional on where the hardware sits in the QEC landscape, a window that widens as devices mature.

</details>


### [179] [Zero-G: A Pre-Decoder-Aware Decoder for Quantum Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02030)
*Peter Wegmann,Theofilos Augoustis,Aleksandra Świerkowska,Emmanouil Giortamis,Pramod Bhatotia*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: Zero-G是一种专为配合预解码器设计的随机近似最小权重完美匹配（MWPM）强解码器，通过利用预解码产生的稀疏残余综合征，动态地在延迟与精度之间权衡，在不牺牲精度的情况下实现10倍延迟提升，并可在FPGA和CPU上异构部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算要求经典解码器的速度与底层硬件匹配，以避免指数级积压。预解码器虽用于分层解码以简化局部错误，但在实践中，现有强解码器是针对密集综合征设计的，无法利用预解码后残余综合征的稀疏性，导致加速效果甚微。

Method: 提出Zero-G解码器：一种随机近似MWPM解码器，专门利用预解码产生的稀疏残余综合征，通过动态调整延迟与精度的权衡，替代传统全有或全无的运行时-精度折中；同时将硬件控制与解码核心解耦，实现FPGA和CPU上的异构部署而无需维护分开的实现。

Result: Zero-G在匹配精度的条件下，相比现有强解码器实现了10倍延迟改进，最坏情况下在码距高达d=15时解码延迟低于350纳秒；单个128核CPU可扩展至640个逻辑量子比特，单个AMD Versal V80 FPGA可扩展至32个逻辑量子比特。

Conclusion: Zero-G证明了专门为预解码器设计的强解码器能够有效利用稀疏残余综合征，显著提升解码速度，并通过软硬件解耦实现跨平台部署，为大规模容错量子计算提供了实用的实时解码方案。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computing requires classical decoders that keep pace with the underlying hardware, translating syndrome measurements into corrections fast enough to avoid an exponential backlog. To meet this real-time constraint, pre-decoders have emerged as part of a hierarchical decoding approach to resolve simple, local errors before passing a sparser residual syndrome to a strong decoder. While pre-decoding should, in theory, speed up the strong decoder, in practice, the speedup is only marginal, since existing strong decoders are designed to decode dense syndromes and cannot exploit the sparsity provided by pre-decoders. To address this, we present Zero-G, a strong decoder designed for use alongside pre-decoders. As a stochastic approximate minimum-weight perfect matching (MWPM) decoder, Zero-G exploits sparse residual syndromes, dynamically trading latency for accuracy rather than relying on an all-or-nothing runtime-accuracy trade-off. By decoupling hardware control from the decoding core itself, we enable heterogeneous deployment across both FPGAs and CPUs without maintaining separate implementations. Zero-G achieves a $10\times$ latency improvement over existing strong decoders at matching accuracy, with worst-case sub-350ns decoding at code distances up to d=15, while scaling to 640 logical qubits on a single 128-core CPU and 32 logical qubits on a single AMD Versal V80 FPGA.

</details>


### [180] [Convergence monitoring of quantum Gibbs samplers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02038)
*Nikolaos Louloudis,Ruben Ibarrondo,Mikel Sanz,Mario Berta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种低成本的哈密顿量无关收敛判据，用于监测全量子马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（QMC）采样器在吉布斯态采样过程中的收敛性。该方法利用量子吉布斯采样器中固有的弱测量记录，通过检测热平衡下系统与环境间净能量流为零时的能量交换平衡条件来判定收敛，仅依赖采样器自身生成的数据即可实现统计诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全量子马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛方法虽已实现高效吉布斯态采样，但严格的混合时间最坏情况边界对经典上不可解系统难以获得。经典蒙特卡洛经验表明相关可观测量可能快速收敛，因此需要一种在多项式开销内高效诊断收敛的实用判据，以替代无法获得的严格理论边界。

Method: 提出基于弱测量的收敛监测判据：利用量子吉布斯采样器及其量子比特高效变体中固有的弱测量，从弱测量记录中提取（准）频率分布；在热平衡下系统与环境间净能量流为零，能量交换统计满足平衡条件；据此构造哈密顿量无关的停止判据，仅使用采样器已生成的数据。

Result: 提供了统计分析和数值/解析研究，分析了该判据的性能、假设条件和局限性，验证了基于能量交换平衡条件的收敛判据的有效性。

Conclusion: 该收敛监测方法是一种实用且低成本的工具，能够在不依赖系统哈密顿量先验知识的情况下，仅通过采样过程中的弱测量数据诊断吉布斯采样器的收敛性，为量子蒙特卡洛方法的实际应用提供了重要支撑。

Abstract: Recent progress in fully quantum Markov chain Monte Carlo methods enables efficient Gibbs-state sampling on quantum computers [Chen et al., Nature 646, 561 (2025)]. Although rigorous worst-case bounds on mixing times remain largely inaccessible for classically intractable systems, experience from classical Monte Carlo suggests that convergence of relevant observables may nevertheless be rapid. This raises the practical question of how to diagnose convergence efficiently, i.e., with at most polynomial overhead. We propose a low-cost criterion for convergence monitoring that exploits the weak measurements inherent in quantum Gibbs samplers and their qubit-efficient variants [Ding et al., arXiv:2508.05703 (2025)]. Our approach is based on the observation that, at thermal equilibrium, the net energy flow between system and environment vanishes and energy-exchange statistics satisfy a balance condition. This condition appears in the distribution of (quasi-)frequencies extracted from the weak-measurement record and we use it to construct a Hamiltonian-agnostic stopping criterion based solely on data already generated by the sampler. We provide a statistical analysis, along with numerical and analytical studies to understand its performance, assumptions, and limitations.

</details>


### [181] [Towards Tensor-Network SAT-Solvers for Quantum-Classical Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02041)
*Benjamin Zec,Lukas Schmidbauer,Maja Franz,Wolfgang Mauerer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了张量网络基态搜索作为HPC/QC集成系统中量子任务的一个替代（surrogate）方法，对比了Max-3-SAT问题的原生高阶Ising表征与二次化QUBO-to-QI两种编码在DMRG方法下的性能，发现二次化不是中性变换，会显著降低解质量，且经典模拟退火在测试实例上匹配或优于DMRG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集成HPC/QC系统不能仅被视为调度量子内核的机制，还需要支持可观测性（无法仅靠QPU实现）、回退执行以及成本感知的量子任务是否被经典替代的决策。由于经典最坏情况复杂度是指数级的，这些替代机制必须利用近似或问题结构来缓解。因此需要研究何种经典模拟方案可以作为合适的量子任务替代品。

Method: 将Max-3-SAT问题分别编码为两种形式：原生多项式无约束优化到高阶Ising（native HOI），以及二次化的QUBO到二次Ising（quadratised QI）。两种编码均通过矩阵乘积算子（MPO）表示，并使用密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）方法进行优化，以模拟退火（SA）作为经典性能基线进行对比。

Result: 二次化并非中性变换：辅助变量和成对耦合显著降低了解质量。相对于原生高阶Ising表征，二次化表征下DMRG的解质量明显下降。在所有测试实例中，经典模拟退火在解质量上匹配或优于DMRG。

Conclusion: 由于SAT衍生问题的解是经典乘积态，DMRG的量子优势在此类问题中无法体现。HPC/QC运行时中的替代（surrogate）选择必须考虑编码方式和实例特征，本研究为回退策略与架构协同设计提供了实证基础。

Abstract: Integrated HPC/QC systems aim to combine classical high-performance computing with quantum processors, but cannot be reduced to mechanisms for dispatching quantum kernels. An integrated architecture must support aspects such as observability, which cannot be implemented using QPUs alone, as well as fallback execution and cost-aware decisions on whether to replace quantum tasks with classical surrogates. Such mechanisms must be approximate or benefit from problem structure to soften the inescapable exponential classical worst-case complexity. In this work, we study tensor-network ground-state search, as such a surrogate, for optimisation problems. This combines key quantum primitives with advanced classical simulation. It provides initial empirical indicators for surrogate selection criteria, and exposes end-to-end toolchain effects that may be missed when transformation steps are studied in isolation. We compare a native polynomial unconstrained optimisation to-higher-order-Ising and a quadratised quadratic unconstrained binary optimization to-quadratic-Ising formulation for Max-3-SAT. Both are encoded as matrix product operator and optimised using density matrix renormalisation group approaches, with simulated annealing (SA) as classical performance baseline. Our results show that quadratisation is not a neutral transformation step: auxiliary variables and pairwise couplings substantially degrade solution quality relative to the native higher-order representation, while SA matches or outperforms DMRG across all tested instances. Since the optima of Boolean satisfiability (SAT)-derived problems are classical product states, DMRGs advantages dont materialise here. These findings suggest that surrogate selection in HPC/QC runtimes must be encoding- and instance-aware and provide empirical groundwork for informed decisions on fallback strategies and architecture co-design.

</details>


### [182] [Adaptive Reconstruction of Bosonic Quantum States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02049)
*Vasilisa Usova,Phila Rembold,Ian Yang,Marco Rossignolo,Simone Montangero,Samuele Tosatto,Gerhard Kirchmair*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应重建技术，用于估计玻色子量子态族（而非单一目标态）的保真度，同时从少量测量中重建Wigner函数。该方法结合物理信息参数模型与贝叶斯推理、bootstrap和主动学习，在电路量子电动力学平台上对薛定谔猫态（α∈[1,3]）进行了验证，并在闭环量子最优控制实验中展示了其实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻色子量子系统提供硬件高效的量子信息处理平台，但由于其巨大的希尔伯特空间和高昂的态层析测量成本，表征这些系统极具挑战。现有方法仅能估计相对于单一目标态的保真度，无法处理物理等效但存在相空间平移、旋转或其他变换的状态族，因此需要一种能够针对状态族进行测量高效保真度估计的方法。

Method: 该方法将物理信息参数模型与贝叶斯推理、bootstrap和主动学习相结合，通过迭代选择最具信息量的相空间采样点来估计针对一族玻色子态的保真度并重建Wigner函数。研究中在电路量子电动力学平台上实施，以薛定谔猫态（振幅α∈[1,3]）为基准，并与现有Wigner函数采样协议进行实验对比。

Result: 重建方法在几分钟内产生可复现的保真度估计，即使使用不匹配的先验，对相空间中的显著位移和旋转仍保持鲁棒性，并且对微妙的状态缺陷敏感。与现有Wigner函数采样协议的实验比较表明，自适应采样在针对猫态族的测量高效保真度估计方面具有优势。

Conclusion: 该方法展示了自适应采样策略在玻色子量子态表征中的显著优势，不仅实现了测量高效的保真度估计，还将重建保真度成功纳入闭环量子最优控制实验的品质因数中，证明了该方法在玻色子量子态自主优化中的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Bosonic quantum systems provide a hardware-efficient platform for quantum information processing but remain challenging to characterise due to their large Hilbert space and the high measurement cost of state tomography. Existing approaches estimate the fidelity with respect to a single target state, making them unsuitable for applications in which physically equivalent states differ by phase space translations, rotations, or other transformations. Here, we introduce an adaptive reconstruction technique that estimates the fidelity with respect to a family of bosonic states while reconstructing the underlying Wigner function from a small number of measurements. The method combines a physics-informed parametric model with Bayesian inference, bootstrap, and active learning to iteratively select the most informative phase space sampling points. We implement the approach on a circuit quantum electrodynamics platform and benchmark it on Schrödinger cat states with amplitudes $α\in[1,3]$. The reconstruction yields reproducible fidelity estimates within a few minutes, remains robust to substantial displacements and rotations in phase space despite using a mismatched prior, and is sensitive to subtle state imperfections. We further compare the adaptive strategy with existing Wigner function sampling protocols experimentally, demonstrating the advantage of adaptive sampling for measurement-efficient fidelity estimation with respect to a family of cat states. Finally, we incorporate the reconstructed fidelity into the figure of merit used in a proof-of-principle closed-loop quantum optimal control experiment, demonstrating the applicability of the method to autonomous optimisation of bosonic quantum states.

</details>


### [183] [Scaled Caldeira-Leggett Dynamics: Deterministic Trajectories and the Classical Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02054)
*S. V. Mousavi,S. Miret-Artés*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Bohmian语言框架下研究了Caldeira-Leggett模型中开放量子系统的高温动力学，通过密度矩阵的极坐标形式推导出广义连续性方程和Hamilton-Jacobi方程。研究发现有效势在经典极限下不消失且不能简单分解为量子与热贡献之和，导致温度依赖的经典轨迹即使在热环境中也保持确定性，与Langevin描述的随机动力学形成对比。通过引入量子性参数对Caldeira-Leggett方程进行缩放，探索了量子到经典的转变，并应用于高斯态展示了热效应与转变参数对相干性和干涉的联合抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解开放量子系统在高温环境下的量子-经典过渡机制，揭示热环境对量子动力学的影响方式，并阐明Bohmian描述与Langevin描述在经典极限下的本质差异。

Method: 采用Bohmian力学框架，将密度矩阵表示为极坐标形式，推导广义连续性方程和Hamilton-Jacobi方程；引入量子性参数对Caldeira-Leggett方程进行缩放处理；以高斯态为例进行数值或解析分析。

Result: 有效势在经典极限下不消失且不能分解为加性量子与热贡献；温度依赖的经典轨迹保持确定性而非随机性；高斯态中相干性和干涉被热效应与转变参数联合逐渐抑制。

Conclusion: Bohmian方法提供了一种不同于Langevin描述的开放量子系统视角，其基于系综的本质决定了轨迹的确定性特征，为量子-经典转变提供了新的理解途径。

Abstract: The dynamics of an open quantum system in the high-temperature regime of the Caldeira-Leggett model using a Bohmian language is investigated. By expressing the density matrix in polar form, generalized continuity and Hamilton-Jacobi equations are obtained. Thermal effects appear explicitly in the former, while they influence the latter only indirectly through an effective potential. Remarkably, the effective potential does not vanish in the classical limit and cannot, in general, be decomposed into simple additive quantum and thermal contributions. Instead, it retains a nontrivial structure leading to a residual temperature dependence in the resulting dynamics. As a consequence, the resulting temperature-dependent-classical trajectories remain deterministic even in the presence of a thermal environment. This behavior contrasts with the stochastic dynamics observed in the Langevin description and reflects the ensemble-based nature of the present approach. To further explore the quantum-to-classical transition, a scaled version of the Caldeira-Leggett equation is introduced by scaling both the density matrix and Planck constant through a quantumness parameter. This parameter takes the value one in the fully quantum regime and smoothly approaches zero in the classical limit. Applications to Gaussian states demonstrate a gradual suppression of coherence and interference, governed jointly by thermal effects and the transition parameter.

</details>


### [184] [Efficient re-sampling in quasi-probability decompositions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02075)
*Sara Santos,Stefan Woerner,Vincenzo Savona,Julien Gacon*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对拟概率分解（QPD）的重加权策略，通过在具有相同变分结构的电路间复用采样结果，显著降低QPD的指数级采样开销。该方法首先被用于估计参数化量子态之间的保真度，随后应用于量子几何张量实部的估计，在存在真实硬件噪声时优于其他标准估计技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期量子设备受噪声和硬件限制，促使研究者发展以增加采样开销换取电路复杂度降低的算法方法（如QPD）。然而QPD的采样开销通常随系统规模指数增长，严重限制了其实用性。因此需要设计策略来降低这种采样开销，使QPD方法在变分量子算法中更具可行性。

Method: 提出一种面向具有相同变分结构（跨参数设置）电路的QPD重加权策略。核心思想是在不同参数设置之间复用采样结果，从而减少整体所需的采样次数。具体验证路径包括：(1) 用该方法估计参数化量子态之间的保真度，建立对指数级QPD采样开销的可控机制，同时保持状态编码幺正变换（ansatz）的结构；(2) 将该方法结合同步扰动随机逼近（SPSA）来估计量子几何张量的实部。

Result: 在保真度估计中，该方法能够有效控制QPD的指数采样开销。在真实硬件噪声条件下，结合SPSA估计量子几何张量实部时，该方法优于其他标准估计技术。

Conclusion: 重加权策略有潜力显著扩展基于QPD的方法在变分量子算法中的适用范围，为解决QPD采样开销指数增长这一关键瓶颈提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Near-term quantum devices are limited by noise and hardware constraints, motivating algorithmic approaches that trade circuit complexity for increased sampling overhead. Quasi-probability decompositions (QPDs), for example, allow replacing non-local operations by multiple circuits with local operations, but the associated sampling overhead generally scales exponentially and limits their practicality. In this work, we introduce a reweighting strategy for QPDs for circuits with the same variational structure across parameter settings, reusing samples and thereby reducing the sampling overhead. We first demonstrate this approach by estimating fidelities between parameterized quantum states, a key primitive in variational time evolution and quantum kernel methods. Importantly, this setup allows controlling the exponential QPD sampling overhead while preserving the structure of the state-encoding ansatz. We then apply the method to estimate the real part of the quantum geometric tensor using the simultaneous perturbation stochastic approximation and find that, in the presence of realistic hardware noise, our method outperforms other standard estimation techniques. These results highlight the potential of reweighting strategies to extend the applicability of QPD-based methods in variational quantum algorithms.

</details>


### [185] [Field-Free Transverse Aharonov--Bohm Phase Gate for an Orbital $l$-Qubit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02090)
*Ju Gao,Fang Shen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限壁环形波导中自旋分辨的Dirac守恒流对自由空间Aharonov-Bohm（AB）Bessel模的推广，证明约束将核心排除的有限壁环形波导中幸存轨道电流转化为直行传播模的横向Aharonov-Bohm（TAB）传播相位。论文推导了自旋无关的轨道相位Δφ_ln∝lΦL_int⟨ρ^{-2}⟩_ln/v_z，并提出利用相反绕向模|±l⟩构建同路径量子比特实现R_z(2δ_l)旋转，具有差分读出和共模相位抑制特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由空间AB Bessel模已被证明携带依赖于磁通的方位角Schrödinger概率流和动力学轨道角动量。研究者希望将此响应推广到自旋分辨的守恒Dirac流，并探讨在有限壁环形波导约束下，AB效应如何转化为可操作的相位操作，实现无场引导相位门。

Method: 论文采用有限壁环形波导模型，分析核心排除结构中自旋分辨的守恒Dirac流。通过保留完整的倏逝尾并考察径向梯度电流在自旋反转下的行为，将边界贡献封闭处理。利用径向梯度的反转特性推导出自旋无关的轨道相位表达式，并建立|±l⟩模作为量子比特的量子门实现方案，分析其直接一阶串扰的角谐波条件（m=±2l）。

Result: 在100μm波导段、a=20nm、R=30nm、E_z=10meV、|l|=10的参数下，灵敏度达到0.1885 mrad/mG，R_z(π)旋转在16.67G磁场下实现。有限壁约束成功将固有的方位角Dirac流转化为引导的无场相位操作。

Conclusion: 有限壁环形波导约束将Dirac粒子的固有方位角流转化为引导的、无需外部磁场的相位操作。该方案通过自旋无关轨道相位实现量子比特旋转，具备差分读出的鲁棒性，并明确了串扰抑制条件，为无场量子相位门提供了新的物理实现途径。

Abstract: Free-space Aharonov--Bohm (AB) Bessel modes are known to carry a flux-dependent azimuthal Schrödinger probability current and kinetic orbital angular momentum. We extend this response to the spin-resolved conserved Dirac current of a core-excluded finite-wall annular guide and show that confinement converts its surviving orbital component into a transverse Aharonov--Bohm (TAB) propagation phase for a straight traveling mode whose centroid path has zero projection onto $\mathbf A$. The radial-gradient current reverses under spin reversal; retaining the complete evanescent tail closes it as a boundary contribution, leaving a spin-independent orbital phase $Δφ_{ln}\propto lΦL_{\rm int}\langleρ^{-2}\rangle_{ln}/v_z$. Opposite-winding modes $|\pm l\rangle$ form a same-path qubit implementing $R_z(2δ_l)$ with differential readout and common-mode phase rejection, while direct first-order crosstalk requires the angular harmonic $m=\pm2l$. For a $100\,μ\mathrm{m}$ section with $a=20\,\mathrm{nm}$, $R=30\,\mathrm{nm}$, $E_z=10\,\mathrm{meV}$, and $|l|=10$, the sensitivity is $0.1885\,\mathrm{mrad/mG}$ and $R_z(π)$ occurs at $16.67\,\mathrm{G}$. Finite-wall confinement thus turns intrinsic azimuthal Dirac current into a guided field-free phase operation.

</details>


### [186] [Better accuracy with fewer qubits: Single-particle basis set optimization for quantum chemistry on quantum computers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02119)
*Subimal Deb,V. S. Prasannaa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于遗传算法优化的量子化学基组（MSTO-kG，k=2-11），在保持较少量子比特和两比特门数量的前提下，获得与甚至优于6-31G、cc-pVQZ等传统高精度基组的原子和分子能量，显著降低了VQE、QPE、HHL等量子算法在近端量子设备上的资源开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期量子计算机噪声较大，量子化学计算受限于少量轨道（active space）。但即使使用合理的单粒子基组，小活性空间也会丢失大量关联能。因此需要设计量子比特高效（qubit-efficient）的中等质量基组，在有限量子资源下保留更多关联能。

Method: 采用遗传算法结合激进优化（aggressive refinement）策略，对现有最小基组（STO基组）进行再优化，生成H到F原子的MSTO-kG基组（k=2-11）；在FCI（C2使用CISD）层次下计算原子及分子（H2、Li2、C2、LiH、BeH、BeH2）能量；并比较VQE、QPE、HHL三种量子算法下所需的量子比特数、两比特门数及T门数量。

Result: MSTO基组在FCI层次下，H到F的基态能量与6-31G相当甚至更低；Li的MSTO基组甚至超越cc-pVQZ。分子方面H2表现不佳（与文献一致），其余分子（Li2、C2、LiH、BeH、BeH2）结果与6-31G相当或更优。MSTO基组在VQE、QPE和HHL下比竞争基组消耗更少的量子比特和两比特门，QPE和HHL的逻辑T门数也显著降低。

Conclusion: MSTO基组为近端量子计算机上的量子化学计算提供了更精确且更少量子比特消耗的解决方案，为中等规模量子设备上的实用化量子化学模拟铺平了道路。

Abstract: In spite of recent advances, quantum computers are expected to be sufficiently noisy in the coming few years to the extent of limiting quantum chemical calculations to relatively small number of orbitals. However, even with reasonable quality single particle basis sets, small active spaces with limited orbitals can result in a significant fraction of correlation energy being lost, motivating the design of moderate quality qubit-efficient basis sets for quantum algorithms. We begin by reoptimizing the existing minimal basis sets using a genetic algorithm-inspired approach in conjunction with aggressive refinement strategies, and generate modified minimal basis sets (MSTO-kG basis; k = 2-11) for atoms from H through F. The ground state energies of H through F using our MSTO bases at FCI level of theory yield ground state energies that are comparable or sometimes even lower than those obtained using 6-31G basis sets. In the case of Li, the MSTO bases surpass the performance of cc-pVQZ bases. Thus, we obtain better atomic energies with same number of qubits relative to STO bases, and better/comparable energies with fewer qubits relative to higher quality bases. In the case of molecules, H2 performs poorly; a finding that is consistent with an earlier work in literature. For other molecules, Li2, C2, LiH, BeH and BeH2, the FCI results (except C2 for which we employ CISD) from our bases are comparable to/outperform those from 6-31G basis. Finally, we compare the resources required between different bases and find that MSTO bases yield better energies than the competing basis sets while incurring fewer qubits and two-qubit gates with VQE, QPE, and HHL. The logical T-gate counts are also found to be considerably lower for QPE and HHL respectively. Overall, our work paves way for more accurate yet less qubit-hungry quantum chemical calculations using near-term quantum computers.

</details>


### [187] [Unconditionally successful quantum Time-Marching algorithm via LCU for nonlinear Burgers equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02130)
*Niccolo Fonio,Giuseppe Di Molfetta,Pierre Sagaut*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于量子晶格气算法的量子模拟方法，能够无条件成功（无概率失败）地模拟Burgers方程等非线性偏微分方程，这是首个时间步可无概率失败串联的Burgers方程量子算法。核心思想是利用线性组合酉算子（LCU）框架中量子测量的随机性与经典晶格气算法固有随机性之间的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
