Vacula,I. Vaiman,J. F. Valdés Galicia,L. Valore,P. van Dillen,E. Varela,V. Vašíčková,A. Vásquez-Ramírez,D. Veberič,I. D. Vergara Quispe,S. Verpoest,V. Verzi,J. Vicha,S. Vorobiov,J. B. Vuta,A. A. Watson,A. Weindl,M. Weitz,L. Wiencke,H. Wilczyński,B. Wundheiler,B. Yue,A. Yushkov,E. Zas,D. Zavrtanik,M. Zavrtanik*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了Pierre Auger天文台对由磁矩诱导的有源-惰性中微子跃迁产生的超越标准模型物理的灵敏度。这种偶极子门户相互作用可在运动学阈上增强中微子-核子截面，影响超高能中微子探测率。通过未探测到UHE中微子候选体，作者在90%置信水平下推翻了中微子通量依赖的跃迁磁矩约束，覆盖1 TeV-100 TeV惰性中微子质量范围，并扩展到前人未探索的参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索惰性中微子与有源中微子之间由磁偶极矩介导的跃迁（偶极门户模型），这一机制属于超越标准模型（BSM）物理，可能在高能下显著增强中微子-核子相互作用截面，从而在超高能中微子实验中产生可观测信号，但此前该参数空间尚未被充分利用。

Method: 利用Pierre Auger天文台的超高能中微子探测数据，同时考虑下行和掠地穿越（Earth-skimming）两种中微子探测通道。两种通道对增强通量与修正截面的响应不同，可用以区分这两种效应。在1 TeV-100 TeV惰性中微子质量范围内，基于未观察到UHE中微子候选体这一事实，推导出通量依赖的90%置信水平跃迁磁矩约束。

Result: 在假设的通量情景下，获得了与通量相关的、味道无关的跃迁磁矩上限，这些约束将现有界限扩展到此前未被探索的参数空间区域。

Conclusion: Pierre Auger天文台的观测数据能有效约束惰性中微子偶极门户相互作用的参数空间，为探索该BSM物理场景提供了新的实验限制，且下行与掠地通道的对比为区分通量增强与截面修正提供了判别力。

Abstract: We investigate the sensitivity of the Pierre Auger Observatory to physics beyond the Standard Model arising from magnetic-moment-induced transitions between active and heavy sterile neutrinos. Such dipole portal interactions can enhance neutrino-nucleon cross sections above a kinematic threshold set by the sterile neutrino mass, leading to observable modifications of neutrino detection rates at ultrahigh energies (UHE). We estimate the impact of these interactions on both down-going and Earth-skimming neutrino detection channels, the contrasting responses of which enable discrimination between an enhanced neutrino flux and a modified interaction cross section. Using the non-observation of UHE neutrino candidates, we derive neutrino-flux-dependent constraints with 90% confidence-level on the transition magnetic moment for sterile neutrino masses in the range 1 TeV-100 TeV. Under the assumed flux scenarios, the resulting flavor-independent limits extend existing bounds into previously unexplored parameter space.

</details>


### [225] [Magnetic field modifications to the pion-quark vertex in the Linear Sigma Model with quarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01701)
*Alejandro Ayala,Flávia Fialho,Adrián Lara,Ana Mizher,Javier Rendón*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用线性Sigma模型（含夸克）作为QCD低能有效理论，计算了恒定均匀磁场对夸克-中性π介子顶点的修正。由于平移对称性丧失，即使树级也需在坐标空间中使用Ritus波函数而非平面波进行计算；作者同时计算了单圈修正，适用于任意朗道能级，并用Schwinger本征时间法在最低朗道能级下验证了两者结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强磁场环境中（如中子星内部、重离子碰撞早期），夸克-介子相互作用顶点的修正对理解QCD物质性质至关重要。由于磁场破坏平移不变性，标准动量空间计算方法失效，需要发展新方法来正确处理这一修正。

Method: 采用线性Sigma模型（LσM）作为低能有效理论，在配置空间中计算夸克-中性π介子顶点：树级用Ritus波函数替代平面波处理；单圈级别分别用Ritus形式和Schwinger本征时间法两种独立方法计算并交叉验证，结果适用于任意朗道能级，并以最低朗道能级为例展示。

Result: 获得了外磁场下夸克-中性π介子顶点的树级及单圈修正表达式，适用于任意朗道能级；在最低朗道能级极限下，Ritus形式与Schwinger本征时间法两种方法给出的单圈修正结果完全一致，验证了计算的正确性。

Conclusion: 在恒定均匀磁场下，由于平移对称性破缺，即使树级过程也需要在配置空间中以Ritus波函数处理；两种独立方法在最低朗道能级下给出一致的单圈结果，确认了该计算框架的可靠性，为后续研究磁场下QCD有效理论提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: We compute the modification to the quark-neutral pion vertex, induced by a constant and uniform magnetic field, using the Linear Sigma Model with quarks as a low energy effective theory of QCD. The vertex is modified even at tree-level since, due to the loss of translational invariance, the calculation should be carried out in configuration space, with the quark described by Ritus wave functions instead of plane waves. We also compute the one-loop vertex modification. These modifications are found for arbitrary Landau levels occupied by the quark. We illustrate the result for the case where the quark occupies the lowest Landau level. To check the result, we also compute the one-loop vertex modification using the Schwinger proper-time method with the quark occupying the lowest Landau level and find the same result as in the case where the Ritus formalism is used.

</details>


### [226] [Jet-by-jet energy correlators as stochastic probes of the parton-to-hadron transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01764)
*Jingyu Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究喷注能量-能量关联函数（EEC）的逐喷注协方差结构，而非传统均值。在Pythia样本中发现壳层变量稀疏且强非高斯，交叉壳层相关性无法用喷注级摘要、排列或角度打乱重现。在Pythia和Herwig的24种生成器-半径-动量配置中，强子级协方差迹更小且相邻壳层相关性更大，但其变化幅度和角度依赖在两种生成器间存在差异，表明逐喷注EEC协方差提供了均值谱中缺失的部分子到强子转变信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统EEC仅报告系综平均，无法揭示不同角度区域在逐喷注层面如何协同涨落。为获取喷注内部涨落结构及部分子-强子转变过程中丢失的信息，需要对逐喷注EEC协方差进行系统研究。

Method: 保留每个喷注的分箱两点点关联函数，研究角壳层权重的分布与协方差；使用高统计量Pythia样本分析稀疏性与非高斯性；通过五种喷注级摘要回归壳层变量、壳层置换和构成角重排来检验协方差来源；在Pythia与Herwig中对24种生成器-半径-动量配置比较部分子级与强子级样本的协方差迹和相邻壳层相关性，并进行特征位置分析。

Result: 壳层变量稀疏且强非高斯，协方差中存在跨壳层结构且分布于多模态上。喷注级摘要回归仅解释8-16%的协方差迹。排列与角重排无法复现离对角相关性。在所有配置中强子级协方差迹更小、0.30≤r/R<1范围内相邻壳层相关性更大，但两种生成器在变化的大小和角度依赖上不同，特征位置分析未发现共同标度。

Conclusion: 逐喷注EEC协方差包含均值谱所不具备的部分子到强子转变信息，可作为描述QCD碎片化过程中涨落相关性的新探针，并为未来更敏感的碎片化模型约束提供方向。

Abstract: Energy-energy correlators are usually reported as ensemble averages and therefore do not specify how different angular regions fluctuate together from jet to jet. We retain the binned two-point correlator for each jet and study the distribution and covariance of its angular-shell weights. In a high-statistics Pythia sample, the shell variables are sparse and strongly non-Gaussian, and their covariance contains nonzero cross-shell structure spread over many modes. Regressing each shell on five jet-level summaries leaves 84-92% of the covariance trace, while shell-wise permutation and constituent-angle shuffling do not reproduce the observed off-diagonal correlations. We then compare separately generated parton- and hadron-level samples in Pythia and Herwig. Across 24 generator-radius-momentum configurations, the hadron-level covariance trace is smaller and the average neighboring-shell correlation over 0.30 <= r/R < 1 is larger. The size and angular dependence of the change differ between the generators, and a characteristic-position analysis does not identify a common scale. Jet-by-jet EEC covariance thus provides information on the parton-to-hadron transition that is absent from the mean spectrum.

</details>


### [227] [Odderon exchange in high-energy $K^0_S$ regeneration at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01768)
*P. Filip,M. Taševský,V. A. Khoze,R. Pasechnik,M. G. Ryskin,B. G. Zakharov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新审视通过高能中性K介子再生探测Odderon交换的可能性，分析了在LHC实验点前向区域测量0.2-2 TeV K_L^0介子物质内转换的可行性，涵盖相干前向再生和非前向衍射再生两种互补方案，并评估了各种竞争机制和背景限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Odderon作为QCD中C宇称为奇的规范玻色子交换，其直接探测是强相互作用物理的重要目标。中性K介子再生过程中Odderon诱导的再生相位可产生可测的信号，但此前缺乏系统评估LHC几何条件下的实验可行性及其限制因素。

Method: 论文采用两部分互补分析：(1) 复现原始相干前向再生估计，扩展到液氢、C、Cu、Pb等不同再生器材料并考虑K介子衰减，分析TeV能量下Odderon再生相导致的π^0π^0衰变顶点分布畸变；(2) 分析0.2-0.8 TeV较低能量下的非前向（衍射）再生，分别估计Odderon、Pomeron-Odderon割线、ω-Reggeon和光子交换对再生振幅的贡献，并提出主动双再生器相减策略。

Result: 相干前向模式中，Odderon再生相位导致的顶点分布畸变在TeV能量下可测，但面临两个主要限制：来自作用点的初级K_S^0存活构成严重基线要求，且电磁C=-1（光子交换）振幅与Odderon信号相竞争，破坏其作为纯净Odderon测量的解释。非前向衍射模式下，初级K_S^0污染自然被抑制，但中子诱导的纯中性奇异数产生和非弹性Regge背景成为主导限制因素，需要量化背景抑制以实现可观测的Odderon信号。

Conclusion: 论文系统评估了两种再生探测方案各自的优劣和实际限制，明确了相干与非相干模式中需要克服的不同瓶颈，并提出了主动双再生器相减策略作为实现Odderon可观测信号的关键实验方案。

Abstract: We revisit the possibility of detecting the Odderon exchange through high-energy neutral-kaon regeneration, focusing on the in-matter $K^0_L\to K^0_S$ conversion of $(\sim\!0.2-2)$~TeV $K^0_L$ mesons originating from $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13.6$~TeV, and on the practical constraints of realizing such a measurement in the very-forward region of an LHC interaction point. The analysis has two complementary parts. First, we reproduce the original coherent-forward-regeneration estimates for a liquid-hydrogen regenerator and extend them to realistic C, Cu and Pb regenerators of an LHC-compatible geometry, including neutral-kaon attenuation. We show that an Odderon-induced regeneration phase produces a measurable distortion of the $K^0\toπ^0π^0$ decay-vertex distribution at TeV kaon energies, but that the survival of primary $K^0_S$ mesons from the interaction point imposes severe baseline requirements, while a competing electromagnetic $C=-1$ (photon-exchange) amplitude limits the interpretation of the coherent mode as a clean Odderon measurement. Second, we examine non-forward (diffractive) regeneration at lower kaon energies of $0.2-0.8$~TeV, where the primary-$K^0_S$ contamination is naturally suppressed, and estimate the competing Odderon, Pomeron--Odderon-cut, $ω$-Reggeon and photon-exchange contributions to the regeneration amplitude. We identify neutron-induced neutral-only strangeness production and inelastic Regge backgrounds as the dominant limitations, quantify the background suppression required for an observable Odderon signal, and formulate the ingredients of an active double-regenerator subtraction strategy.

</details>


### [228] [Gluon condensate effects on heavy quarkonium spectral functions and thermal dissociation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01801)
*Fei Wang,Zi-qiang Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文采用改进的软墙AdS/QCD模型，通过包含胶子凝聚反作用的膨胀子黑洞几何，研究了胶子凝聚对重矢量介子（粲夸克偶素和底夸克偶素）热谱函数及熔解模式的影响。结果表明：温度升高会展宽并抑制谱峰（介子离解），而更强的胶子凝聚则显著减缓峰展宽并增强谱权重，阻碍热离解，从而在夸克-胶子等离子体中稳定重夸克偶素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究胶子凝聚如何调节重夸克偶素（charmonium和bottomonium）在有限温度下的谱函数和熔解行为，为理解胶子凝聚对QCD介质中重夸克偶素稳定性的作用提供全息证据。

Method: 使用改进的软墙AdS/QCD模型，扩展至有限温度，通过引入一致包含胶子凝聚背反应的膨胀子黑洞几何。数值追踪谱共振峰随温度和胶子凝聚强度的演化。

Result: 温度升高系统性展宽并抑制谱峰（介子离解）；更强的胶子凝聚显著减缓峰展宽并增强基态和激发态的谱权重，表明胶子凝聚阻碍热离解，稳定夸克-胶子等离子体中的重夸克偶素。结果与已有研究一致。

Conclusion: 胶子凝聚在热介质中起着稳定重夸克偶素的作用，这一结论从热谱函数视角为胶子凝聚的稳定化作用提供了新的全息证据。

Abstract: We investigate how the gluon condensate modifies the thermal spectral functions and melting patterns of heavy vector mesons, specifically charmonium and bottomonium, using an improved soft-wall AdS/QCD model. The framework is extended to finite temperature via a dilaton black hole geometry that consistently incorporates the backreaction from the gluon condensate. We numerically track the evolution of spectral resonance peaks as functions of both temperature and gluon condensate strength. Our calculations reveal that increasing temperature systematically broadens and suppresses spectral peaks, signaling in-medium dissociation. In contrast, a stronger gluon condensate considerably mitigates peak broadening and enhances the spectral weight of both ground and excited states. This behavior indicates that the gluon condensate hinders thermal dissociation, thereby stabilizing heavy quarkonia within the quark-gluon plasma. These findings are consistent with existing studies and provide new holographic evidence for the stabilising role of the gluon condensate from the perspective of thermal spectral functions.

</details>


### [229] [Measuring the trilinear Higgs self-coupling in Higgs boson pair production at multi-TeV muon colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01888)
*Kingman Cheung,Jae Sik Lee,Soojin Lee,Chen Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文评估了多TeV缪子对撞机通过矢量玻色子融合过程测量希格斯玻色子三线性自耦合修饰因子κ₃的灵敏度，利用b̄bb̄b最终态，在3 TeV/1 ab⁻¹和10 TeV/10 ab⁻¹两种条件下进行分析。通过结合SPANet配对网络、拓扑数据分析及两个专用分类器，最终在68%置信水平下得到3 TeV时为0.80<κ₃<1.29，10 TeV时为0.96<κ₃<1.05，10 TeV测量精度达到几百分比水平，远超HL-LHC的预期精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三线性希格斯自耦合决定了希格斯势能的形状，对其精确测量是未来对撞机的核心目标。HL-LHC对κ₃的测量精度有限，需要探索更高能量和更高亮度的新对撞机方案（如多TeV缪子对撞机）来显著提升测量精度。

Method: 在矢量玻色子融合产生希格斯对的过程中，利用b̄bb̄b最终态进行分析。事件分为两个互补区域：四喷注的解析区域和两个大半径喷注的增强区域。信号提取结合了三种技术：基于SPANet方法的监督式喷注-希格斯配对网络、事件能量流的拓扑数据分析、以及两个专用分类器D_HH（信号-本底分离）和D_κ₃（κ₃形状信息）。最终通过对两个分类器输出分布的二维似然拟合来提取耦合常数κ₃。

Result: 在68%置信水平下，3 TeV时得到0.80<κ₃<1.29，10 TeV时得到0.96<κ₃<1.05。10 TeV下的测量精度达到几百分比水平（统计学主导的投影），显著优于HL-LHC的预期精度。

Conclusion: 多TeV缪子对撞机在测量三线性希格斯自耦合方面具有巨大潜力，特别是在10 TeV能量下能够达到几百分比的精度，远超HL-LHC的能力，为精确检验希格斯势能形状提供了有力途径。

Abstract: The trilinear Higgs self-coupling determines the shape of the Higgs potential, and its measurement is a central goal of future colliders. We assess the sensitivity of multi-TeV muon colliders to the coupling modifier $κ_3$ in Higgs boson pair production via vector boson fusion, using the $b\bar{b}b\bar{b}$ final state at $\sqrt{s}=3$ TeV with $1$ ab$^{-1}$ and at $10$ TeV with $10$ ab$^{-1}$. Events are analyzed in two complementary regions, a resolved region with four jets and a boosted region with two large-radius jets. To extract the signal from backgrounds a few orders of magnitude larger, we combine a supervised jet-to-Higgs pairing network based on the SPANet approach, topological data analysis of the event energy flow, and two dedicated classifiers, $D_{\rm HH}$ for the signal-to-background separation and $D_{κ_3}$ for the $κ_3$ shape information. The coupling is extracted from a two-dimensional likelihood fit to the distribution of the two classifier outputs. Combining the two regions, we obtain $0.80<κ_3<1.29$ at $3$ TeV and $0.96<κ_3<1.05$ at $10$ TeV at $68\%$ confidence level. The $10$ TeV determination reaches the few-percent level in this statistics-limited projection, which surpasses by a large amount the precision projected for the HL-LHC.

</details>


### [230] [Thermal Leptogenesis in the BNT Model of Neutrino Mass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01890)
*P. S. Bhupal Dev,Srubabati Goswami,Debashis Pachhar,Drona Vatsyayan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究BNT模型中的中微子质量与热轻子生成机制，该模型包含标量四重态Φ和一对矢量费米子三重态Σ。中微子质量通过tree-level的dimension-7算符和one-loop的dimension-5算符产生，即使在TeV量级的新物理尺度下也能自然容纳sub-eV中微子质量。作者发现对于分级谱的费米子三重态，热轻子生成要求MΣ≳10^7 GeV；而在准简并区间，CP不对称的共振增强可将该尺度降至O(TeV)，从而与模型原本的TeV尺度现象学和对撞机可测性相协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BNT模型为TeV尺度的中微子质量起源提供了一个有吸引力的框架，但需要验证该模型能否同时实现成功的热轻子生成（即解释宇宙重子不对称），从而在统一框架下同时解释中微子质量和宇宙物质-反物质不对称性。

Method: 研究BNT模型中中微子质量的产生机制（tree-level dimension-7与one-loop dimension-5算符），并分析热轻子生成在该框架下的可行性。分别考虑分级谱和准简并谱两种情况下费米子三重态的CP不对称性及其共振增强效应，推导轻子生成所需要的质量尺度约束。

Result: 对于分级谱的费米子三重态，热轻子生成可行要求MΣ≳10^7 GeV；在准简并区间，由于CP不对称的共振增强，该尺度可降至O(TeV)，使模型能够在TeV尺度上同时实现成功的热轻子生成并与对撞机实验可测性兼容。

Conclusion: BNT模型在准简并区间可通过共振轻子生成实现TeV尺度的成功热轻子生成，这表明该模型能够同时满足中微子质量、宇宙重子不对称起源以及对撞机可测性这三个要求，是一个极具吸引力的TeV尺度新物理候选模型。

Abstract: We investigate neutrino mass and thermal leptogenesis in the Babu-Nandi-Tavartkiladze (BNT) model featuring a scalar quadruplet ($Φ$) and a pair of vector-like fermion triplets ($Σ$). In this framework, neutrino masses are generated via an effective dimension-7 operator $LLHH(H^{\dagger}H)/Λ^3$ at the tree level and via the dimension-5 operator $LLHH/Λ$ at the one-loop level. It naturally accommodates sub-eV neutrino masses even if the new physics scale $Λ$ is $\mathcal{O}(\rm TeV)$, thus making the model a compelling target for experimental searches. We explore the viability of thermal leptogenesis in this model, which is distinct from the canonical seesaw-based leptogenesis due to the presence of vector-like fermions. We find that leptogenesis is viable for $M_Σ\gtrsim 10^{7}$ GeV for a hierarchical spectrum of fermion triplets. However, in the quasi-degenerate regime, resonant enhancement of the $CP$ asymmetry lowers this scale down to $\mathcal{O}({\rm TeV})$, reconciling successful leptogenesis with the originally motivated TeV-scale phenomenology and testability of the model at colliders.

</details>


### [231] [A quality-coupling relation in chiral $U(1)_{B-L}$ axion model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01897)
*Yu-Cheng Qiu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出用规范化的手征U(1)_{B-L}对称性来保护QCD轴子质量。两个复标量场的真空期望值破缺U(1)_{B-L}，其中一组相位被规范玻色子吃掉，另一组成为QCD轴子。由于PQ对称性是偶然对称性，不存在PQ荷分配的模糊性。高质量轴子意味着轴子与U(1)_{B-L}规范玻色子（或规范Majoron）之间存在大的反常耦合。轴子质量引入了一个"质量下限"（quality floor），即由轴子质量决定的与SM费米子之间的最小耦合，扩展了QCD轴子的参数空间并提高了可测试性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QCD轴子作为强CP问题的解决方案，其质量通常需要一个高精度（quality）的PQ对称性。本文旨在通过一个规范化的手征U(1)_{B-L}对称性来自然保护轴子质量，避免PQ电荷分配的任意性，并探索由此产生的新物理耦合及可测试性。

Method: 采用两个复标量场破缺U(1)_{B-L}规范对称性，其中一个相位自由度被规范玻色子吸收（成为纵向分量），另一个相位成为QCD轴子。由于PQ对称性在该构造中是偶然（accidental）出现的，因此没有PQ电荷分配的模糊性。通过分析轴子与规范玻色子的反常耦合关系，推导出轴子质量对耦合强度的约束，进而导出一个与SM费米子耦合的"质量下限"。

Result: 发现高轴子质量要求轴子与U(1)_{B-L}规范玻色子之间存在大的反常耦合。该约束进一步转化为轴子与SM费米子之间的最小耦合（quality floor），从而扩大了QCD轴子在实验上可探测的参数空间。

Conclusion: 通过规范化的U(1)_{B-L}对称性能自然保护QCD轴子质量，消除PQ荷分配模糊性，并产生由轴子质量决定的"质量下限"，增强了QCD轴子的实验可测试性。

Abstract: The quality of the QCD axion can be protected by a gauge symmetry. This work considers a gauged chiral $U(1)_{B-L}$. The vacuum expectation values of two complex scalars break $U(1)_{B-L}$. One linear combination of their two phase degrees of freedom is eaten by the $U(1)_{B-L}$ gauge boson, while the other becomes the QCD axion. Since the PQ symmetry is accidental, there is no ambiguity in the PQ charge assignment. High axion quality implies a large anomalous coupling between the axion and the $U(1)_{B-L}$ gauge boson (or gauged Majoron). A `quality floor', namely a minimal coupling determined by the axion quality, arises for the coupling between the axion and SM fermions, extending the parameter space of QCD axions toward improved testability.

</details>


### [232] [The $(2+1)$-dimensional Gross-Neveu-Yukawa model at finite temperature, density, and magnetic field within the Functional Renormalization Group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02280)
*Justin L. P. Mauldin,Dirk H. Rischke*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用泛函重整化群（FRG）在局域势近似下，超越平均场研究了(2+1)维Gross-Neveu-Yukawa模型在有限温度、密度和磁场下的相图，发现了多阶一级相变、de Haas–van Alphen振荡以及随磁场增强而向高温移动的临界端点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强磁场下(2+1)维费米系统的手征对称性破缺与相结构，超越平均场近似以捕捉更丰富的非微扰物理行为。

Method: 采用泛函重整化群（FRG）的局域势近似（LPA），并引入流体动力学算法求解有效势的重整化群流方程，从而能够比以往方法更深入地推进到红外区域。

Result: 发现手征凝聚在相图不同区域呈现非平凡行为：小磁场大化学势下出现多个一级相变和de Haas–van Alphen振荡；随磁场增强，临界端点向更高温度移动。

Conclusion: 磁场通过磁催化效应增强手征对称性破缺，同时FRG结合流体动力学算法能够揭示超越平均场的丰富相图结构。

Abstract: We investigate the phase diagram of the (2+1)-dimensional Gross-Neveu-Yukawa (GNY) model at finite temperature, density, and magnetic field beyond mean-field, using the Functional Renormalization Group (FRG) in the local potential approximation. Large magnetic fields result in magnetic catalysis, a dimensional reduction of the system, and enhancement of chiral symmetry breaking. We employ a hydrodynamical algorithm to solve the FRG flow equation for the effective potential, which allows for go further into the infrared region than with previously used methods. We find that the chiral condensate exhibits non-trivial behavior in various regions of the phase diagram: several first-order phase transitions and de Haas -- van Alphen oscillations at small magnetic field and large chemical potential, as well as a critical endpoint which shifts to higher temperature with increasing magnetic field.

</details>


### [233] [FCC precision requests: challenges for Monte Carlos and phenomenology tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02476)
*Z. Was*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文讨论加速器实验中三个精度水平（差于0.3%、约0.3%、优于0.3%）下的物理精度需求，重点聚焦于优于0.3%情形下需要同时处理理论和实验效应的蒙特卡洛程序。作者回顾了Bryan Lynn、Robin Stuart、Dima Bardin、Wolfgang Hollik和S. Jadach等人在精度物理领域的奠基性贡献，并列举了从FOWL到KKMC等12个代表性程序项目，概述了方法论领域及开发过程中的简化与偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为满足高精度（优于0.3%）加速器实验的需求，需要蒙特卡洛程序同时考虑理论和实验效应。作者旨在回顾和总结这一领域多年来的方法论进展、程序开发历程以及奠基性工作，为后续研究者提供参考框架。

Method: 作者以精度水平分类加速器实验的歧义性，识别出优于0.3%情形下的七大方法论领域（相空间对称性、矩阵元制备与因子化、程序开发流程、测试策略、用户交互、软件工具、合作者与竞争者），并梳理了12个关键蒙特卡洛程序的演进脉络。

Result: 识别出需要同时处理理论与实验效应的精确蒙特卡洛程序，列举了FOWL、GENRAP、Mustraal、Koralb、Lesko、Tauola、KoralZ、Lumlog、Oldbab、Bhlumi、Bhwide和KKMC等程序项目的演进，指出了开发过程中的简化和偏差，并说明指数化与因子化问题由其他讲者覆盖。

Conclusion: 高精度物理需要多学科多年协同努力，理论基础由Lynn、Stuart、Bardin、Hollik和Jadach等人奠定。开发过程非线性演进，存在简化与偏差，文献综述尚不完整，更多细节需参考其他报告和会议论文。

Abstract: One can define three levels of ambiguities for the accelerator experiments: worse than 0.3%, around 0.3% and better than 0.3%. I will address issues important, for the last case, when Monte Carlo programs that simultaneously account for theoretical and experimental effects are necessary. To complete efforts, simultaneous effort in many specialities over years was necessary. The monumental projects of Bryan Lynn, Robin Stuart, Dima Bardin, Wolfgang Hollik and contributions of S. Jadach in the domain of precision physics need to be mentioned, they provide theoretical foundations. Incomplete lists of methodology domains and projects, usually left into references, appendices, and private notes include: (i) Phase space--> symmetries (ii) matrix element preparation --> factorizations (iii) program and development process design (iv) testing strategies (v) user interaction (vi) software tools (vii) partners and competitors.
  Usually we were publishing our own projects, let me mention incomplete lists of methodology domains and projects, which were usually left aside into references, appendices, and private notes: The work started on the basis of previous efforts which can be listed following names of the programs: (i) FOWL, (ii) GENRAP, (iii) Mustraal, (iv) Koralb, (v) Lesko, (vi) Tauola, (vii) KoralZ, (viii) Lumlog, (ix) Oldbab, (x) Bhlumi, (xi) Bhwide and (xii) KKMC. I will focus on some of these points. Others, hopefully, are covered in other talks. In particular I do not need to cover exponentiation and some issues of factorization; see contributions to the proceedings by B.F.L. Ward and A. Tapadar. Developments took years and did not follow a straight line; that is why there are simplifications, biases. Also a review of literature could not be completed.

</details>


### [234] [Two-loop amplitude for $t\bar{t}W$ production at hadron colliders in the leading colour approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02527)
*Mattia Pozzoli*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次给出了顶夸克-反顶夸克对与W玻色子联合产生过程的领头色两圈QCD振幅的精确计算，并讨论了对涉及嵌套平方根、椭圆函数和高复杂度代数表达式的处理方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顶夸克对与W玻色子的联合产生过程（ttW）是重要的标准模型过程，精确的理论预言对于精确检验标准模型和探索新物理至关重要。两圈QCD修正的计算在技术上极具挑战性，因为涉及嵌套平方根、椭圆函数等复杂解析结构，此前尚无精确计算结果。

Method: 采用微分方程方法计算特殊函数，利用有限域技术计算有理系数，以应对计算中的解析复杂性（嵌套平方根、椭圆函数等）和高阶代数复杂度。

Result: 首次完成了ttW联合产生过程的领头色两圈QCD振幅的精确计算，最终结果以一组特殊函数和有理系数的形式表达。

Conclusion: 该工作代表了粒子物理中两圈振幅计算的重要进展，为ttW过程的更高阶QCD修正提供了关键基础，并展示了处理复杂解析结构（椭圆函数、嵌套平方根）的有效方法。

Abstract: In this contribution I present the first exact calculation of the leading-colour two-loop QCD amplitude for the associated production of a top-anti-top pair and a W boson. I discuss strategies to address the complexity of the computation, which involves complicated analytic structures, such as nested square roots, elliptic functions, and expressions with a high degree of algebraic complexity. The final result is expressed in terms of a set of special functions, which are evaluated using the method of differential equations, and rational coefficients, evaluated via finite field techniques.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

### [235] [Topological model for derived category associated to sphere with four binaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00163)
*Jianmin Chen,Li Fan,Yu Qiu,Yiting Zheng*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文为加权射影直线$\mathbb{CP}^1_\omega$（类型$(2,2,2,2)$）的导出范畴构建了一个几何模型，该模型由带四个"二进制"点的分次球面构成。通过建立不可分解刚性对象与特定弧之间的双射，将Hom空间维度与有向相交数联系起来。进一步将此几何实现推广到聚类范畴$\mathcal{C}(\mathbb{CP}^1_\omega)$中，利用四孔球面上的标记弧（tagged arcs）来描述不可分解刚性对象，并用标记相交数表示Hom空间维度。当加权点为$(0,1,\infty,\frac{1}{2})$时，两种对应关系均与自同构群和映射类群的自然作用相容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加权射影直线的导出范畴和聚类范畴在表示论与聚类代数理论中扮演重要角色，但对其几何模型的研究仍不充分。尤其是类型$(2,2,2,2)$的加权射影直线，因其特殊的加权结构，需要一种几何直观来理解其不可分解刚性对象及Hom空间结构，从而为聚类范畴提供几何实现。

Method: 构造带四个标记点的分次球面作为$\mathbb{CP}^1_\omega$的几何模型。核心方法包括：(1) 建立不可分解刚性对象与特定弧集合之间的双射对应；(2) 利用有向相交数计算Hom空间维度；(3) 在聚类范畴$\mathcal{C}(\mathbb{CP}^1_\omega)$中引入标记弧（tagged arcs）的概念，推广几何实现；(4) 验证当加权点为特定构型时，对应关系与自同构群和映射类群的自然作用相容。

Result: 成功构建了$\mathbb{CP}^1_\omega$导出范畴的几何模型，建立了不可分解刚性对象与弧之间的双射，并证明Hom空间维度可由有向相交数精确刻画。同时给出了聚类范畴$\mathcal{C}(\mathbb{CP}^1_\omega)$中不可分解刚性对象的标记弧几何实现，其Hom空间维度由标记相交数表示，且在特定加权点配置下与群作用相容。

Conclusion: 该工作为类型$(2,2,2,2)$的加权射影直线提供了完整的几何不变量描述，深化了对这类聚类范畴结构的理解，并展示了算术结构与几何（曲面上的弧）之间深刻的内在联系，为更一般情形的加权射影直线几何模型研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: We construct a geometric model for the derived category of the weighted projective line $\mathbb{CP}^1_ω$ of type $(2,2,2,2)$, given by a graded sphere with four binaries. Precisely, we give a bijection between the set of indecomposable rigid objects and the set of certain arcs, such that the dimensions of $\operatorname{Hom}$-spaces are computed by oriented intersection numbers. As applications, we provide a geometric realization of the indecomposable rigid objects in the cluster category $\mathcal{C}(\mathbb{CP}^1_ω)$ via tagged arcs on the sphere with four punctures, where $\operatorname{Hom}$-space dimensions are expressed in terms of tagged intersection numbers. When the weighted points of $\mathbb{CP}^1_ω$ are $(0,1,\infty,\frac{1}{2})$, both the correspondences are compatible with the natural actions of the automorphism groups and mapping class groups.

</details>


### [236] [On bricks of one-point extension algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00395)
*Qi Wang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究单点扩张代数Λ=B[E]的砖（brick）有限性。利用Λ-模的标准三元组描述，通过单射条件和标量稳定子条件刻画了混合砖，并在B是E-可见有限的前提下，将混合砖解释为格拉斯曼流形中的轨道，从而给出Λ砖有限性的充要判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 砖有限性（brick-finiteness）是表示论中重要的有限性概念，对于单点扩张代数这类构造，理解其砖的结构并给出砖有限性的判据，有助于深入认识代数的表示范畴性质。

Method: 利用Λ-模的标准三元组描述（即通过B-模、E的某个幂以及Ext群中的映射三元组来刻画），提出两个条件（单射条件和标量稳定子条件）来区分混合砖；在B为E-可见有限的假设下，将混合砖与格拉斯曼流形上的群轨道建立对应关系，进而推导砖有限性的充要条件。

Result: 给出了混合砖的刻画定理，并在E-可见有限假设下，将混合砖等价地表示为格拉斯曼流形中的轨道，同时导出了Λ为砖有限代数的必要且充分的条件。

Conclusion: 作者为单点扩张代数的砖结构提供了完整的刻画，确立了混合砖与格拉斯曼流形轨道之间的对应，并给出了砖有限性的充要判据，丰富了单点扩张代数表示理论的研究。

Abstract: Let $Λ=B[E]$ be the one-point extension algebra of $B$ by the extension module $E$. Using the standard triple description of $Λ$-modules, we characterize mixed bricks through an injectivity condition and a scalar-stabilizer condition. Under the assumption that $B$ is $E$-visible finite, we interpret mixed bricks as orbits in Grassmannians and derive a necessary and sufficient criterion for the brick-finiteness of $Λ$.

</details>


### [237] [Affine vertex algebras and an affine analog of Barbasch-Vogan's construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00428)
*Peng Shan,Wenbin Yan,Qixian Zhao*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 这是一篇综述性论文，基于作者的联合工作，旨在解释关于简单仿射顶点代数 L_k(g) 的相伴簇与简单模的两个猜想，其中 g 为单连通单李代数，k 为临界水平之上的整数水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该论文旨在阐明两个关于简单仿射顶点代数相伴簇和简单模的猜想，帮助读者理解这些猜想的陈述及其背后的核心思想。

Method: 作为综述性论文，本文通过系统性地解释两个猜想的表述、背景与证明思路，基于作者已有的联合研究成果进行归纳与阐述。

Result: 论文给出了两个关于相伴簇和简单模的猜想的清晰陈述以及其背后的思想解释，覆盖了单连通单李代数在临界水平之上的整数水平情形。

Conclusion: 该综述为理解简单仿射顶点代数 L_k(g) 的相伴簇和简单模猜想提供了详细的解释框架与理论背景。

Abstract: This is an expository paper based on the authors' joint works. The goal is to explain the statements and the ideas behind two conjectures on associated varieties and simple modules of simple affine vertex algebras $L_k(\mathfrak{g})$ for a simple and simply-laced Lie algebra $\mathfrak{g}$ and a integer level $k$ above the critical level.

</details>


### [238] [Zelevinsky Segments and Hyperspecial Branching Laws for All Depth-Zero Representations of GL_N(F)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00941)
*Runze Wang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文对非阿基米德局部域上一般线性群的不可约深度零表示，完全确定了在超特殊极大紧子群的幂单根原子上的固定向量空间分解，并以Zelevinsky参数显式描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度零表示在p-adic表示论中占据核心地位，其超特殊分叉律（hyperspecial branching law）是理解表示结构的重要工具，但此前对于所有不可约深度零表示缺乏完整且显式的分解描述。

Method: 首先对任意未分歧连通约化群证明取幂单根原固定向量的函子将p-adic侧的Zelevinsky–Aubert对偶与有限侧的Alvis–Curtis对偶联系起来；对于一般线性群，结合Bushnell–Kutzko类型、有限Harish–Chandra级、Iwahori–Hecke代数以及Zelevinsky分类，给出完整的超特殊分叉律。

Result: 分解由Zelevinsky参数显式描述：不可约成分由Kostka数和Zelevinsky分解数控制，自然介于由表示的Zelevinsky段及其Aubert对偶段产生的两个分拆之间；一般情形下结果完全由Kostka数给出；一般深度零Bernstein块中，重数是对应单块重数的乘积。

Conclusion: 该工作为所有深度零不可约表示提供了完备且有效的超特殊分叉律，建立了p-adic侧Zelevinsky–Aubert对偶与有限侧Alvis–Curtis对偶之间的函子性联系。

Abstract: For every irreducible depth-zero representation of the general linear group over a non-Archimedean local field, parameterized by multisets of segments in the sense of Zelevinsky, we determine the complete decomposition of the space of vectors fixed by the pro-unipotent radical of a hyperspecial maximal compact subgroup. The decomposition is described explicitly in terms of the Zelevinsky parameters: the irreducible constituents are governed by Kostka numbers and Zelevinsky decomposition numbers, and they are naturally bounded between two partitions arising from the Zelevinsky segments of the representation and of its Aubert dual. In the generic case the result is given entirely by Kostka numbers. For general depth-zero Bernstein blocks, the multiplicities are products of the corresponding single-block multiplicities.
  We first prove, for an arbitrary unramified connected reductive group, that the functor of taking pro-unipotent fixed vectors intertwines Zelevinsky--Aubert duality on the p-adic side with Alvis--Curtis duality on the finite side. The proof for general linear groups combines Bushnell--Kutzko types, finite Harish--Chandra series, Iwahori--Hecke algebras, and the Zelevinsky classification to provide a complete and effective hyperspecial branching law for all irreducible depth-zero representations.

</details>


### [239] [Residue construction of quantized Coulomb branches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01379)
*Daniil Klyuev*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究量化BFN库仑分支在局域化（abelianization）映射下的像，描述了对任意G,N的量化库仑分支在局域化下的图像，并给出了与Ginzburg–Kapranov–Vasserot构造DAHA类似的根与留数条件。作为推论，为零或少量流形的量化锥形库仑分支提供了Gaiotto–Okazaki球面迹的数学构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化BFN库仑分支是数学物理中的重要对象，其局域化（abelianization）映射下的像此前并未被系统描述。作者旨在填补这一空白，为任意规范群和流形提供完整的描述，并为球面迹给出严格的数学构造。

Method: 利用局域化（abelianization）映射，将量化BFN库仑分支嵌入到环面情形，通过根与留数条件（类似于Ginzburg–Kapranov–Vasserot对DAHA的构造）来描述其像。对无环的quiver规范理论等情形，该描述适用于任意流形参数；在部分情况下需取一般位置或形式流形参数。

Result: 给出了任意G,N下量化BFN库仑分支局域化像的完整描述，由根与留数条件刻画。作为推论，为零或少量流形的量化锥形库仑分支提供了Gaiotto–Okazaki球面迹的数学构造。

Conclusion: 该工作系统性地解决了量化BFN库仑分支的局域化描述问题，并将球面迹的物理构造提升为严格的数学理论。

Abstract: We describe the image of a quantized BFN Coulomb branch $\mathcal{A}_{G,N}^{\hbar=1}$ under localization (abelianization) map for any $G,N$. In most cases, such as quiver gauge theories without loops, this description works for any flavors, sometimes we need to take generic or formal flavor parameters. The answer is given by a roots and residue condition similar to Ginzburg---Kapranov---Vasserot construction of DAHA arXiv:alg-geom/9512017. As a corollary, for any quantized conical Coulomb branch (and zero or small flavors) we provide a mathematical construction of the sphere trace introduced by Gaiotto and Okazaki arXiv:1911.11126.

</details>


### [240] [Cluster Algebras for Bosonic Plethysm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00963)
*Yelin Fan,Jiarui Fei*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文构造了环 R_{ℓ,m}=Sym(Sym²V⊗W)^{U_V} 的显式斜对称可种子 Σ_{ℓ,m}，通过限制和折叠带标志的 m-箭 Kronecker 箭图的行列式种子实现，并证明其对应的簇代数等于该环，且具有多项式冻结系数、redeening 序列、theta 基扩展区域以及 Jacobi–Trudi 型的对称平方 plethysm 系数公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究对称平方的张量商空间上的不变式环 R_{ℓ,m} 的簇代数结构，并建立其 theta 基、权纤维与表示论重数及 plethysm 系数之间的联系。

Method: （1）通过限制（restriction）和折叠（folding）带标志的 m-箭 Kronecker 箭图的行列式种子（determinantal seed），构造斜对称可种子 Σ_{ℓ,m}；（2）利用集群代数理论证明 R_{ℓ,m} 等于其上簇代数，并验证冻结系数的多项式性；（3）建立 theta 基跨越冻结边界的条件，刻画为有理多面体锥 C_{ℓ,m}，并给出优化的冻结变量以得到显式不等式系统；（4）利用 Jacobi–Trudi 恒等式将对称平方 plethysm 系数表示为倾斜交替和。

Result: 对任意 ℓ,m≥2，证明 R_{ℓ,m}=U(Σ_{ℓ,m})（上簇代数）且冻结系数为多项式；Σ_{ℓ,m} 具有 redeening 序列；theta 基恰好对参数落在有理多面体锥 C_{ℓ,m} 内时跨越冻结边界扩展；theta 基的权纤维计数多分次最高权重数；通过 Jacobi–Trudi 恒等式将对称平方 plethysm 系数表示为这些计数的有限交错和；给出了 C_{ℓ,m} 的显式有限不等式系统。

Conclusion: 论文为不变式环 R_{ℓ,m} 提供了完整的簇代数描述，建立了 theta 基与表示论重数之间的桥梁，并对对称平方 plethysm 系数给出了新的组合公式，深化了簇代数与不变式理论及表示论的交叉研究。

Abstract: Let $\Bbbk$ be an algebraically closed field of characteristic zero, let $V=\Bbbk^\ell$ and $W=\Bbbk^m$, and set \[ \mathcal R_{\ell,m}=\operatorname{Sym}(\operatorname{Sym}^2V\otimes W)^{U_V}. \] We construct an explicit skew-symmetrizable seed $Σ_{\ell,m}$ by restricting and folding the determinantal seed for the flagged $m$-arrow Kronecker quiver. For every $\ell,m\ge2$, we have \[ \mathcal R_{\ell,m}=\mathcal U(Σ_{\ell,m}), \] with polynomial frozen coefficients, and $Σ_{\ell,m}$ admits a reddening sequence. The theta basis extends across the frozen boundary exactly for parameters in a rational polyhedral cone $\mathscr C_{\ell,m}$. Its weight fibers count the multigraded highest-weight multiplicities of $\mathcal R_{\ell,m}$, and the Jacobi--Trudi identity expresses symmetric-square plethysm coefficients as finite alternating sums of these counts. Optimized frozens give an explicit finite system of inequalities for $\mathscr C_{\ell,m}$.

</details>


### [241] [Chevalley-Eilenberg cohomology of linearly reductive Lie algebras in the Verlinde category](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01576)
*Pavel Etingof,Serina Hu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究Verlinde融合范畴Ver_p^+中线性约化李代数g的Chevalley-Eilenberg上同调，证明了g无不变元，并给出H^•_CE(g)与外部代数∧^•E_g^*之间的分次代数同构，同时将该上同调与群概形G=exp(g)的de Rham上同调等同，推广了Borel-Chevalley关于复半单李代数上同调的经典定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在将半单李代数上同调的经典结果（Borel-Chevalley定理）推广到正特征p≥5情形的Verlinde范畴框架中，特别是研究Ver_p^+中线性约化李代数的结构及其上同调性质。

Method: 利用Ver_p^+范畴的结构，对线性约化李代数g证明其无不变元（即单位对象不是g的和因子）；定义E_g为若干分量g_m的Frobenius扭转移位直和；随后证明Chevalley-Eilenberg上同调H^•_CE(g)同构于外部代数∧^•E_g^*，并通过群概形G=exp(g)建立与de Rham上同调的等同以及Hopf代数结构的一致性；最后利用简单模上同调为零的结论推导一般有限维模的上同调公式。

Result: 主要结果为分次代数同构H^•_CE(g)≅∧^•E_g^*，且该代数识别为de Rham上同调H^•_dR(G)；对非平凡作用的简单g-模V有H^•_CE(g,V)=0，从而对任意有限维模V有H^•_CE(g,V)≅∧^•E_g^*⊗V^g。这恢复了Borel-Chevalley定理及其在充足大的正特征情形下的类比。

Conclusion: 该工作将经典半单李代数上同调理论推广到Verlinde范畴设定中，建立了Chevalley-Eilenberg上同调与外部代数、de Rham上同调之间的统一框架，并涵盖了特征零与充足大正特征两种情形，体现了Verlinde范畴方法的普适性。

Abstract: Let $k$ be an algebraically closed field of characteristic $p\geq 5$, and let $\mathrm{Ver}_p^+$ be the even part of the Verlinde fusion category $\mathrm{Ver}_p$, the semisimplification of $\mathrm{Rep}_k(\mathbb Z/p)$. Let $\mathfrak g$ be a linearly reductive Lie algebra in $\mathrm{Ver}_p^+$, i.e., one whose finite-dimensional representations are semisimple. A basic class of examples is obtained by semisimplifying a simple Lie algebra over $k$ equipped with the action of $\mathbb Z/p$ by a principal unipotent element, when $p$ exceeds its Coxeter number. We prove that $\mathfrak g$ is invariantless, i.e., that the unit object is not a summand of $\mathfrak g$. For odd $m$ with $3\leq m\leq p-2$, set $\mathfrak{g}_m:=\operatorname{Hom}_{\mathrm{Ver}_p^+}(L_m,\mathfrak g)$ and $E_{\mathfrak g}:=\bigoplus_{3\leq m\leq p-2,\ m\ {\rm odd}}\mathfrak g_m^{(1)}[m]$, where $(1)$ denotes Frobenius twist. Our main result is an isomorphism of graded algebras $H^\bullet_{\mathrm{CE}}(\mathfrak g)\cong\bigwedge^\bullet E_{\mathfrak g}^*$. We also identify this algebra with the de Rham cohomology $H^\bullet_{\mathrm{dR}}(G)$ of the group scheme $G=\exp(\mathfrak g)$ and show that the induced graded Hopf algebra structure agrees with the standard one on the exterior algebra. Moreover, if $V$ is a simple $\mathfrak g$-module on which $\mathfrak g$ acts nontrivially, then $H^\bullet_{\mathrm{CE}}(\mathfrak g,V)=0$. Hence for every finite-dimensional $\mathfrak g$-module $V$ one has $H^\bullet_{\mathrm{CE}}(\mathfrak g,V)\cong\bigwedge^\bullet E_{\mathfrak g}^*\otimes V^{\mathfrak g}$. This recovers the theorem of Borel and Chevalley on the cohomology of complex semisimple Lie algebras and its analogue in sufficiently large positive characteristic.

</details>


### [242] [The double super Yangians in type A for arbitrary $0^m 1^n$-sequences and their bosonic representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01031)
*Pengfa Xu,Hongda Lin,Honglian Zhang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文引入了与任意0^m1^n序列s相关的双重超Yangian DY_h(gl_{m|n}^s)和DY_h(sl_{m|n}^s)，建立了Drinfeld表示与R-矩阵表示之间的显式同构，推广了量子Berezinian的概念，并构造了level-1玻色子表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超Yangian作为量子群的重要推广，在数学物理中有着广泛应用。已有的双重超Yangian研究局限在标准序列情形，需要系统地建立更一般序列（0^m1^n序列）下双重超Yangian的表示论基础，特别是建立Drinfeld表示与R-矩阵表示之间的同构关系。

Method: 本文首先显式构造了DY_h(gl_{m|n}^s)的Drinfeld表示与R-矩阵表示之间的同构；然后将量子Berezinian的概念推广到双重超Yangian DY_h(gl_{m|n}^s)，并利用量子Berezinian构造DY_h(sl_{m|n}^s)的R-矩阵表示，同时证明其与Drinfeld表示同构；最后利用Drinfeld电流生成元给出level-1玻色子表示。

Result: 建立了DY_h(gl_{m|n}^s)两种表示的显式同构；推广了量子Berezinian并证明了DY_h(sl_{m|n}^s)的R-矩阵表示与Drinfeld表示同构；给出了level-1玻色子表示。

Conclusion: 本文完整地建立了与任意0^m1^n序列相关的双重超Yangian的两种标准表示之间的等价性，并通过量子Berezinian统一了gl和sl情形，同时提供了level-1玻色子实现，为后续研究奠定了代数基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce the double super Yangian $\mathrm{DY}_{h}(\mathfrak{gl}_{m|n}^{\mathfrak{s}})$ and $\mathrm{DY}_{h}(\mathfrak{sl}_{m|n}^{\mathfrak{s}})$ associated with any fixed $0^{m}1^{n}$--sequence $\mathfrak{s}$. First, we establish an explicit isomorphism between the Drinfeld and R--matrix presentations of $\mathrm{DY}_{h}(\mathfrak{gl}^{\mathfrak{s}}_{m|n})$. We then generalize the notion of the quantum Berezinian to $\mathrm{DY}_{h}(\mathfrak{gl}_{m|n}^{\mathfrak{s}})$, and employ it to construct the R--matrix presentation of $\mathrm{DY}_{h}(\mathfrak{sl}_{m|n}^{\mathfrak{s}})$ and prove that it is isomorphic to the Drinfeld presentation. As an application, we present level--1 bosonic representations for $\mathrm{DY}_{h}(\mathfrak{gl}_{m|n}^{\mathfrak{s}})$ and $\mathrm{DY}_{h}(\mathfrak{sl}_{m|n}^{\mathfrak{s}})$ in terms of their Drinfeld current generators.

</details>


### [243] [A uniform decomposition theorem for invariant differential operators on imprimitive complex reflection groups G(r,p,n)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01474)
*Jean Kaboré,Ibrahim Nonkané*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该论文研究多项式环在判别式处的局部化关于不变环上微分算子环的模结构，利用Ariki–Terasoma–Yamada的高阶Specht多项式给出简单分量及其显式生成元；证明基于判别式的Jacobian引理和双中心化子论证；特殊情况恢复并大幅缩短了W(D_n)和W(B_n)的已知分解定理；最后首次将Nonkané的Galois下降范畴等价应用于G(r,p,n)，给出简单直和项的另一种不含生成元的扭不变描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究不可约不可本原复反射群G(r,p,n)的不变环上微分算子作用下的模结构分解。已有的分解定理适用于实反射群W(D_n)和W(B_n)，作者希望统一并推广到更一般的复反射群G(r,p,n)，同时获得更简洁的证明和显式生成元。

Method: 首先通过Jacobian引理计算G(r,p,n)的判别式，结合双中心化子论证来刻画模结构；利用Ariki–Terasoma–Yamada的高阶Specht多项式提供简单分量的显式生成元；并首次将Nonkané的Galois下降等价范畴应用于G(r,p,n)，用于给出简单直和项的不含生成元的备选描述。

Result: 得到了G(r,p,n)的多项式环在判别式处局部化关于不变环上微分算子环的模分解，显式描述了其简单分量及生成元；恢复并大幅简化了实反射群W(D_n)和W(B_n)的已知分解定理；对G(r,r,n)和G(r,1,n)给出了显式处理及D_2、D_3、B_2的工作示例与中心幂等元；并通过Galois下降给出了简单直和项的第二种（扭不变）描述。

Conclusion: 该论文统一且推广了复反射群G(r,p,n)情形下多项式环局部化的模结构分解理论，既给出了显式生成元（Specht多项式），也提供了不含生成元的范畴等价描述，并简化了已有实反射群的分解定理。

Abstract: {We study the module structure of the polynomial ring, localized at the discriminant, over the ring of differential operators on the ring of invariants of the imprimitive complex reflection group $G(r,p,n)$, describing its simple components with explicit generators given by the higher Specht polynomials of Ariki--Terasoma--Yamada. The proof rests on a Jacobian lemma computing the discriminant of $G(r,p,n)$, combined with a double-centralizer argument. As particular cases ($r=2$, $p=2$ or $p=1$) we recover, and considerably shorten, the known decomposition theorems for the real reflection groups $W(D_n)$ and $W(B_n)$; we also treat $G(r,r,n)$ and $G(r,1,n)$ explicitly, with worked examples ($D_2$, $D_3$, $B_2$) and their central idempotents. Finally, applying the Galois descent equivalence of categories of Nonkané to $G(r,p,n)$ for the first time gives a second, generator-free description of the simple summands as twisted invariants.}

</details>


### [244] [Intertwining Operators for Siegel Parabolics over Finite Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01485)
*Nir Elber,Hahn Lheem*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限域上经典子群G（GL_{2n}的子群）的退化主级数表示，证明了其多重性自由性及对一般特征标χ的不可约性，并讨论了特定的交织算子I及其相关组合学，包括可对角化的反对角三角矩阵族、矩阵Gauss和，以及对称和交错可逆矩阵的迹与行列式的均匀分布结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该论文旨在深入理解有限域上经典群（GL_{2n}的经典子群）的退化主级数表示的结构，特别是其不可约性与多重性自由性质。同时，通过交织算子I的研究，期望建立表示论与组合学（如矩阵Gauss和、矩阵族的特征值行为）之间的深层联系，并得出关于对称和交错矩阵分布的显式结果。

Method: 论文采用有限域上表示论的方法，具体包括：(1) 考虑经典子群G及其Siegel抛物子群P的退化主级数表示Ind_P^Gχ；(2) 研究一般（generic）特征标χ下的不可约性和多重性自由性；(3) 引入特定的交织算子I，通过其作用构造可对角化的反对角（antitriangular）矩阵族并分析其特征值；(4) 将算子I应用于特定的表示向量，导出各种矩阵Gauss和，并利用其求值结果推导对称和交错可逆矩阵的迹与行列式的均匀分布。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 证明了退化主级数表示Ind_P^Gχ对一般特征标χ是多重性自由的且不可约；(2) 交织算子I产生了具有良好特征值行为的可对角化反对角三角矩阵族；(3) 获得了各种矩阵Gauss和的显式求值；(4) 由此得到了对称和交错可逆矩阵的迹与行列式的显式均匀分布（equidistribution）结果。

Conclusion: 这项工作将有限域上经典群的表示论与矩阵组合学紧密联系起来，既深化了退Fermaid主级数表示的结构理论，又通过交织算子这一桥梁获得了矩阵Gauss和的求值和对称/交错矩阵的均匀分布等组合性质的新结果，展示了表示论工具在数论组合问题中的有效应用。

Abstract: We consider degenerate principal series representations $\operatorname{Ind}_P^Gχ$ over finite fields, where $G$ is a classical subgroup of $\operatorname{GL}_{2n}$, and $P$ is the Siegel parabolic subgroup. For example, we show that this representation is always multiplicity-free and irreducible for generic characters $χ$. We then discuss a particular intertwining operator $I$ on $\operatorname{Ind}_P^Gχ$ and its related combinatorics. Firstly, this operator $I$ produces families of diagonalizable antitriangular matrices with well-behaved eigenvalues. Secondly, applying $I$ to a special vector in $\operatorname{Ind}_P^Gχ$ leads us to various matrix Gauss sums, whose evaluations imply an explicit equidistribution result of the trace and determinant of symmetric and alternating invertible matrices.

</details>


### [245] [Hecke algebra representations from the Katz-Long-Moody construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01727)
*Haru Negami*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了Katz-Long-Moody (KLM)构造，精确分类了其产生的辫群表示何时能因子化通过Hecke代数，并给出了因子化通过Temperley-Lieb代数的精确判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解KLM构造产生的辫群表示与Hecke代数和Temperley-Lieb代数之间的关系，这对于连接拓扑量子场论与表示论具有重要意义。

Method: 对KLM构造进行系统分析，分类辫群表示因子化通过Hecke代数的条件，并针对Temperley-Lieb代数给出精确判据。

Result: 证明了除一个例外（两股辫的特殊族）外，表示因子化通过Hecke代数的性质仅依赖于自由群部分的本征值，而与构造参数无关；同时给出了因子化通过Temperley-Lieb代数的精确判据。

Conclusion: KLM构造与Hecke代数及Temperley-Lieb代数的关系已得到精确刻画，为理解辫群表示的结构提供了完整的分类结果。

Abstract: We study the Katz-Long-Moody (KLM) construction and classify exactly when the resulting braid group representations factor through Hecke algebras. We show that, except for one exceptional two-strand family, this property depends only on the eigenvalues of the free-group part and is independent of the construction parameter. We also give an exact criterion for when the resulting representations factor through Temperley-Lieb algebras.

</details>


### [246] [Some remarks on realizations defining diagrammatic Hecke categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02187)
*Amit Hazi*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明图式Hecke范畴中常用的Demazure满射性和抛物性质假设并非必要，并给出了非平衡实现中旋转标量的非归纳描述以及更简单的平衡性判定准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图式Hecke范畴由实现（realization）构造，而实现推广了Coxeter群的反射表示概念。为保证所构造的范畴具有良好性质，通常需要施加Demazure满射性和抛物性质（用于反球商）等假设。作者希望探究这些假设是否真正必要，并简化相关的判定条件。

Method: 作者通过分析实现的结构，证明了Demazure满射性和抛物性质假设实际上是多余的。同时，对于非平衡实现，给出了旋转标量的非归纳（non-inductive）描述；对于平衡性，提出了一个更简单的判定判据。

Result: 证明了Demazure满射性和抛物性质假设并非必要条件；给出了非平衡实现下旋转标量的非归纳描述；提供了检测平衡性的更简单判据。

Conclusion: 图式Hecke范畴的核心性质并不依赖于这些额外的假设，从而简化了理论框架并扩展了适用范围。平衡性的判定也因此变得更为直接。

Abstract: Every diagrammatic Hecke category is constructed from a realization, which generalizes the notion of a reflection representation of a Coxeter group. Common assumptions on realizations to ensure that the resulting categories are well behaved include Demazure surjectivity and the parabolic property (for anti-spherical quotients). We show that these assumptions are in fact unnecessary. We also give a non-inductive description of the rotational scalars for unbalanced realizations, as well as a simpler criterion to check for balancedness.

</details>


### [247] [On a question by G. Navarro on the character field generation of odd-degree characters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02266)
*Ulrich Thiel*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文通过构造显式反例，否定了Navarro在2023年提出的猜想：有限群的奇数次特征的特征域并非总是由单个特征值生成，无论对不可解群还是可解群均不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Navarro在[Publ. Mat. 67 (2023)]中提出了一个开放性问题：有限群的奇数次特征（odd-degree character）的特征域是否由单个特征值生成。该问题涉及有限群表示论中特征域的结构性质，回答它有助于深入理解特征值所生成的域与群结构之间的关系。

Method: 作者通过构造显式例子来回答该问题。分别在非可解群和可解群两类情形下给出具体的反例，证明存在奇数次特征，其特征域不能由任何单一特征值生成。

Result: 结果表明Navarro的问题在一般情况下答案为否：无论是非可解群还是可解群，都存在有限群的奇数次特征，其特征域不能由单个特征值生成。

Conclusion: Navarro关于奇数次特征的特征域由单个特征值生成的猜想在一般情况下不成立。作者通过显式反例表明，该性质在可解群和非可解群中均可能失败，从而排除了这一猜想在一般有限群框架下的可能性。

Abstract: In [Publ. Mat. 67 (2023), no. 1, 173-198], G. Navarro asked whether the character field of an odd-degree character of a finite group is generated by a single character value. We show by explicit examples that the answer is negative in general, for non-solvable as well as solvable groups.

</details>

### [248] [Explicit description of certain 3-point K-theoretic Gromov-Witten invariants for flag manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00359)
*Satoshi Naito,Daisuke Sagaki*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文给出了全旗流形X=G/B上，与例外微小基本权λ（位于W·ϖᵢ轨道上）相关的线丛类O(-λ)的3点、亏格0、K理论Gromov-Witten不变量的显式描述，该描述基于量子Bruhat图。这是对作者先前工作中量子K理论除数公理的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在将经典的量子K理论除数公理推广到更一般的情形。之前的工作已获得量子K理论除数公理，本文希望将其推广到与微小基本权相关的线丛类，从而对更广泛的一类三点K理论Gromov-Witten不变量给出显式计算。

Method: 关键在于利用量子K理论环QK_T(X)中推广的Chevalley公式。该公式计算带权重λ的线丛类O(-λ)与量子乘积的作用，由此推导出三点K理论Gromov-Witten不变量的显式公式，并以量子Bruhat图的语言加以描述。

Result: 获得了量子K理论三点不变量⟨O(-λ), O^w, O_u⟩_d的显式描述（部分推广了量子K理论除数公理）。该描述以量子Bruhat图的结构给出，适用于λ位于微小基本权W·ϖᵢ的Weyl群轨道上的情形。

Conclusion: 本文通过推广的Chevalley公式和量子Bruhat图，部分推广了量子K理论除数公理，为更广一类K理论Gromov-Witten不变量的计算提供了显式方法。

Abstract: We give an explicit description, in terms of the quantum Bruhat graph, of the (torus-equivariant) 3-point, genus 0, $K$-theoretic Gromov-Witten invariants $\langle \mathcal{O}(- λ), \mathcal{O}^{w}, \mathcal{O}_{u} \rangle_{d}$ for the (full) flag manifold $X = G/B$, where $\mathcal{O}(- λ)$ denotes the class in the (torus-equivariant) $K$-theory ring $K_{T}(X)$ of $X$ of the line bundle $\mathcal{O}_{X}(- λ) = G \times_{B} \mathbb{C}_λ$ over $X = G/B$ associated to a weight $λ\in W \varpi_i$ lying in the Weyl group orbit of a minuscule fundamental weight $\varpi_i$, and $\mathcal{O}_{u}$, $\mathcal{O}^{w}$ are the Schubert and opposite Schubert classes in $K_{T}(X)$ for $u, w \in W$. This result can be thought of as a partial generalization of the quantum $K$-theoretic divisor axiom, which we obtained in our previous work; our proof utilizes a generalization of the Chevalley formula in the (torus-equivariant) quantum $K$-theory ring $QK_{T}(X)$ of $X$, which computes the quantum product with the line bundle class $\mathcal{O}(- λ)$ associated to the weight $λ$ above.

</details>


### [249] [On Separable and Frobenius Cowreaths of type $(A \otimes H^{\mathrm{op}}, H,ψ)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00676)
*Fabio Renda*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文发展了一种关于类型为$(A \otimes H^{\mathrm{op}},H,ψ)$的cowreath的一般理论，利用Hopf代数上的积分代替Casimir态射来研究可分性，并给出Frobenius性质的充要条件，从而统一推广了已有文献中的结果，并展示了可分但不Frobenius的cowreath例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此类cowreath已在多个文献中作为幺半范畴中(h-)可分和Frobenius余代数的例子被研究，但已有方法较为繁琐且难以推广到高维情形。作者希望建立一种更一般的理论，使已有结果能够以更简洁的方式被恢复，并能扩展到更高维度的cowreath。

Method: 关键观察是：当Hopf代数H具有双射的对合映射（antipode）时，应使用余代数$(H,ψ)$上的积分来代替Casimir态射研究可分性，因为积分更易于分类。对于Frobenius性质，作者通过态射$(\mathrm{Id}_A \otimes μ)ρ_A$是否内蕴（inner）来刻画cowreath的Frobenius性。

Result: 得到了两个主要结果：(1) 一个cowreath $(A \otimes H^{\mathrm{op}},H,ψ)$是Frobenius的当且仅当态射$(\mathrm{Id}_A \otimes μ)ρ_A$是内蕴的（其中μ是$H^*$中特殊的群元）；(2) Frobenius cowreath总是(h-)可分的，但反之不然——文章末尾构造了(h-)可分但不Frobenius的cowreath例子。

Conclusion: 本文建立的一般理论简化了已有文献中关于此类cowreath的研究，将结果推广到更高维情形，并明确区分了(h-)可分性与Frobenius性这两个概念之间的关系，表明前者是后者的必要条件但非充分条件。

Abstract: Cowreaths of type $(A \otimes H^{\mathrm{op}},H,ψ)$ have been investigated in [10,19-21,27] as examples of (h-)separable and Frobenius coalgebras in monoidal categories. They are entwining structures built from a Hopf algebra $H$ and an $H$-comodule algebra $(A,ρ_A)$. In this article we develop a general theory that allows us to recover results from the aforementioned papers in an easier way and also to extend them to cowreaths in higher dimension. Focusing on separability, the crucial observation is that, when $H$ has bijective antipode, one should work with integrals on the coalgebra $(H,ψ)$ in place of Casimir morphisms, for they are easier to classify. On the other hand, in dealing with Frobenius properties, we obtain that a cowreath $(A \otimes H^{\mathrm{op}},H,ψ)$ is Frobenius if and only if the morphism $(\mathrm{Id}_A \otimes μ)ρ_A$ is inner ($μ$ is the distinguished grouplike element in $H^*$). While Frobenius cowreaths are always (h-)separable, examples of (h-)separable cowreaths that are not Frobenius will be presented at the end of this article.

</details>


### [250] [Classification of irreducible highest weight modules for the parafermion vertex algebras $N_k(\mathfrak{sl}_2)$ at arbitrary level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02382)
*Drazen Adamovic,Qing Wang*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文对所有k≠0的universal parafermion vertex algebra N^k(sl_2)及其简单商N_k(sl_2)进行了不可约最高权模的完全分类。通过给出Zhu代数A(N^k(sl_2))的显式展示（四变量多项式商掉三个显式多项式生成的理想），证明了该代数是关于权二、三场类生成的子代数的秩三自由模。不可约最高权模由双参数族L_k[x,y]参数化，并通过自由场实现构造。此外，每个L_k[x,y]可实现为universal affine vertex algebra V^k(sl_2)的不可约权模M的N^k(sl_2)-子模；在非整数容许水平下，证明了L_k[x,y]为N_k(sl_2)-模当且仅当M为L_k(sl_2)-模，从而完成了所有非整数容许水平下N_k(sl_2)不可约最高权模的分类，并额外分类了临界水平N_{-2}(sl_2)的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Parafermion vertex algebras是与仿射顶点代数和W-代数密切相关的重要对象，其表示理论对于理解共形场论中的扩展对称性具有关键意义。此前N^k(sl_2)的最高权模分类问题在一般水平下尚未解决，且简单商N_k(sl_2)仅在正整数水平下由Arakawa、Lam和Yamada完成分类，非整数容许水平下的分类仍是公开问题。本文旨在填补这一空白，给出完整的分类结果。

Method: 首先通过计算Zhu代数A(N^k(sl_2))的显式展示，将其表示为四变量多项式代数模去由三个显式多项式生成的理想的商，并证明其作为秩三自由模的结构。利用Zhu代数结构与顶点代数模的对应关系，结合自由场实现构造双参数族L_k[x,y]作为所有不可约最高权模。接着建立N^k(sl_2)-模L_k[x,y]与V^k(sl_2)的不可约权模M之间的嵌入关系，利用仿射顶点代数在容许水平下的已知分类结果，将N_k(sl_2)模的分类归结为L_k(sl_2)模的分类。

Result: 对所有k≠0完成了N^k(sl_2)不可约最高权模的完全分类，由双参数族L_k[x,y]参数化。给出了Zhu代数的显式展示。对所有非整数容许水平完成了N_k(sl_2)不可约最高权模的分类，证明其与L_k(sl_2)模分类的对应关系。额外完成了临界水平k=-2下N_{-2}(sl_2)的分类。

Conclusion: 本文通过Zhu代数和自由场实现的方法，完整解决了sl_2型parafermion顶点代数在所有k≠0水平下不可约最高权模的分类问题，特别填补了非整数容许水平和临界水平的空白，为该方向进一步研究（如更一般简单李代数的推广）奠定了坚实的基础。

Abstract: Let $N^k(\mathfrak{sl}_2)$ be the universal parafermion vertex algebra and $N_k(\mathfrak{sl}_2)$ its simple quotient. We classify all irreducible highest weight $N^k(\mathfrak{sl}_2)$-modules for every $k\neq 0$. We give a presentation of Zhu's algebra $A(N^k(\mathfrak{sl}_2))$ as a quotient of a polynomial algebra in four variables by an ideal generated by three explicit polynomials. This presentation also shows that $A(N^k(\mathfrak{sl}_2))$ is a free module of rank three over the polynomial subalgebra generated by the classes of the fields of weights two and three. The irreducible highest weight $N^k(\mathfrak{sl}_2)$-modules are parametrized by a two-parameter family $L_k[x,y]$, $(x,y)\in\mathbb C^2$, constructed using a free-field realization.
  We also prove that each $N^k(\mathfrak{sl}_2)$-module $L_k[x,y]$ can be realized as an $N^k(\mathfrak{sl}_2)$-submodule of an irreducible weight module $M$ for the universal affine vertex algebra $V^k(\mathfrak{sl}_2)$. At non-integral admissible levels, we prove that $L_k[x,y]$ is an $N_k(\mathfrak{sl}_2)$-module if and only if the associated $V^k(\mathfrak{sl}_2)$-module $M$ is an $L_k(\mathfrak{sl}_2)$-module. This gives the classification of irreducible highest weight $N_k(\mathfrak{sl}_2)$-modules at all non-integral admissible levels. We also classify the irreducible highest weight modules for the simple parafermion algebra $N_{-2}(\mathfrak{sl}_2)$ at the critical level. At positive integral levels, the irreducible $N_k(\mathfrak{sl}_2)$-modules were previously classified by Arakawa, Lam, and Yamada.

</details>


### [251] [The crossed braided tensor category of twisted and untwisted representations of the Heisenberg conformal net](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02574)
*Adrià Marín-Salvador*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文证明了Heisenberg共形网Heis的扭曲/未扭曲表示范畴是一个关于群ℝ的连续Tambara-Yamagami范畴，并计算了结合子与ℤ/2-交叉辫子结构。通过对ℤ/2-等变化化，显式计算了固定点共形网Heis^ℤ/2的表示范畴，首次给出了包含张量积为不可约表示直接积分的共形网表示范畴的显式计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形网的表示范畴通常不满足有限性条件，其张量积可能涉及直接积分而非有限直和。目前缺乏对含有连续型张量积的共形网表示范畴的显式计算，作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 首先证明Heis共形网的表示范畴为关于实数群ℝ的连续Tambara-Yamagami范畴，计算其结合子和ℤ/2-交叉辫子结构；随后通过对ℤ/2-等变化化（equivariantization）操作，得到固定点共形网Heis^ℤ/2的带辫子连续张量范畴，并给出显式描述。

Result: 建立了Heis表示范畴为连续Tambara-Yamagami范畴的结构定理，并通过ℤ/2-等变化化显式计算了Heis^ℤ/2的表示范畴。这是首次对含有不可约表示直接积分张量积的共形网表示范畴进行显式完整的计算。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解非有限型共形网表示范畴提供了首个具体的典范范例，推动了连续张量范畴理论在共形场论中的应用。

Abstract: We show that the category of twisted/untwisted representations of the Heisenberg conformal net $\text{Heis}$ is a continuous Tambara-Yamagami category for the group $\mathbb{R}$. We compute the associators and the $\mathbb{Z}/2$-crossed braiding. By taking a $\mathbb{Z}/2$-equivariantization, we obtain an explicit computation of the braided continuous tensor category of representations of the fixed-points conformal net $\text{Heis}^{\mathbb{Z}/2}$. This provides the first explicit computation of a category of representations of a conformal net containing irreducible representations whose tensor product is a direct integral of irreducible representations.

</details>


### [252] [Construction of PBW and canonical bases in the folding extension algebras for symmetrizable types](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25495)
*Yumeng Wu*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文研究Lusztig在带容许自同态a的quiver表示空间上的perverse sheaves构造所引出的扩展代数，构建了折叠KLR代数的偏斜群代数R(ν)⟨a⟩，并证明了Ext函子保持带符号基与晶体结构，最终在有限型情形下实现了对canonical basis与PBW basis的几何范畴化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Varagnolo和Vasserot的方法推广到Lusztig的对称化（symmetrizable）设定中，研究带容许自同态a的quiver表示空间上的perverse sheaves所引出的扩展代数，从而构造折叠KLR代数并建立其几何范畴化理论。

Method: 利用Lusztig的容许自同态构造偏斜群代数R(ν)⟨a⟩；证明Ext^∙( ,L)保持带符号基与晶体结构；在有限型情形下定义标准模与对偶标准模，分析其对偶行为并研究Grothendieck群；证明不可分解投射模类（canonical basis）到对偶标准模类（PBW basis）的转移矩阵为上三角且对角元为1。

Result: 成功构造了对称化设定下带容许自同态的偏斜群代数，证明了Ext函子保持带符号基和晶体结构，并证明canonical basis到PBW basis的转移矩阵为上三角且对角元为1，从而在对称化情形下实现了对这两组基及其关系的几何范畴化。

Conclusion: 该工作将Varagnolo–Vasserot方法扩展至Lusztig的对称化设定，通过构造折叠KLR代数的偏斜群代数，在有限型情形下为canonical basis与PBW basis及其关系提供了统一的几何范畴化框架。

Abstract: We study extension algebras arising from Lusztig's construction of perverse sheaves on quiver representation spaces equipped with an admissible automorphism $a$, leading to folding Khovanov--Lauda--Rouquier (KLR) algebras. Extending the method of M. Varagnolo and E. Vasserot to Lusztig's symmetrizable setting, we construct the skew group algebra $R(ν)\langle\mathbf{a}\rangle$, where $\mathbf{a}$ is induced by the admissible automorphism $a$, and show that $\Ext^{\bullet}(\ ,L)$ preserves the signed basis and the crystal structure. In this framework, for finite types, we define standard and dual standard modules, analyze their behavior under dualities, and study the resulting Grothendieck groups. We prove that the transition matrix from the classes of indecomposable projective modules, which give the canonical basis, to the dual standard modules, which give the PBW basis, is upper triangular with diagonal entries equal to one. This provides a geometric categorification of these bases and of their relation in the symmetrizable setting.

</details>
